### [1] [From Barrier to Bridge: The Case for AI Data Center/Power Grid Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03090)
*Noman Bashir,Rob Sherwood,Le Xie,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AI超大规模训练数据中心打破了电力系统百年来的“负荷多样性”假设，其瞬时功率波动达数百兆瓦。本文主张数据中心与电力行业需从“隐性共存”转向“显性协同发展”，并提出了联合容量规划、多时间尺度控制等关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电网依赖于百万级用户需求互不相关（即负荷多样性）这一统计假设，从而产生平滑且可预测的总负荷。然而，单个AI超算训练园区的功率可达中型城市规模，且由单一同步任务驱动，秒级功率波动高达数百兆瓦，打破了上述核心假设。算力与电力基础设施的深度纠缠亟需两个历史上相互脱节的行业进行变革性的协调。

Method: 本文采用跨学科分析视角，首先界定了数据中心与电力行业在设计原则、运营理念及经济激励机制上的差异，剖析导致文化与技术错位的根源。在此基础上，提出了一个涵盖联合容量规划、多时间尺度控制、算力-电力协议栈设计以及市场机制创新的系统性研究框架。

Result: 揭示了AI算力负荷特性对电网稳定性的根本性冲击，论证了现有行业分离模式的不可持续性。明确了实现算力与电力协同发展所需的关键技术路径，包括跨行业的容量统筹、适应快速波动的控制机制以及标准化的交互协议栈。

Conclusion: 为支撑AI的未来可持续发展，数据中心与电力行业必须摒弃各自为政的传统模式，建立显性的协同发展机制，通过跨领域的联合规划与技术创新应对算力-电力深度融合带来的挑战。

Abstract: For over a century, the electric grid has relied on a single statistical assumption: \emph{load diversity}, the principle that the uncorrelated demands of millions of small consumers produce a smooth, predictable aggregate. AI training data centers break that assumption. A single hyperscale training campus can draw power comparable to a mid-sized city, driven by one tightly synchronized job whose demand swings by hundreds of megawatts in seconds. This paper argues that the resulting entanglement of compute and power infrastructure requires a shift from implicit coexistence to explicit co-development between the historically decoupled data center and electric power industries. We introduce the distinct design principles, operational philosophies, and economic incentives of each sector, and show why their cultural and technical misalignment makes coordination difficult. We identify key research directions, from joint capacity planning, multi-timescale control, a compute--power protocol stack, to market innovation, that must be pursued to power the future of AI sustainably and reliably.

</details>


### [2] [VDCores: Resource Decoupled Programming and Execution for Asynchronous GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03190)
*Zijian He,Adrian Sampson,Yiying Zhang,Zhiyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VDCores提出了一种解耦编程与执行模型，通过将异步硬件单元抽象为资源隔离的虚拟核心，并基于依赖关系自动重叠内存与计算操作，在LLM推理任务中平均提升24%解码吞吐量，同时显著降低编程复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU依赖专用异步硬件单元来提供高性能，但现有软件栈仍采用整体式内核模型，导致编程与执行模型与异步硬件不匹配，硬件利用率低下。

Method: VDCores将异步硬件执行单元抽象为资源隔离的虚拟核心（Virtual Cores），并将工作负载表示为依赖连接的微操作（micro-ops）。该方法移除了程序员的静态编排负担，基于依赖关系和资源就绪状态自动实现内存与计算的重叠执行。同时设计了GPU专用编程模型和运行时系统以最小化实现开销。

Result: 在GH200、H100和RTX 6000 Pro GPU上的四个LLM推理工作负载测试中，VDCores平均提升解码吞吐量24%，在动态输入场景下最高提升77%，同时将内核编程和特化工作量减少90%。

Conclusion: VDCores成功实现了高效的解耦抽象，显著提升了异步硬件资源利用率和解码吞吐量，大幅降低了编程复杂度，为异步GPU编程提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly rely on specialized and asynchronous hardware units to deliver high performance. Yet these units are often underutilized because today's GPU software stacks still organize programming and execution around a monolithic kernel model that mismatches asynchronous hardware. To address this issue, Virtual Decoupled Engines (VDCores) presents a new decoupled programming and execution model for asynchronous GPUs. VDCores abstracts asynchronous hardware execution units as resource isolated virtual cores and represents workloads as dependency-connected micro-operations (micro-ops). this abstraction removes static orchestration from the programmer, enables automatic overlap of memory and compute based on dependency and resource readiness, and thereby improves utilization of asynchronous hardware resources.
  Realizing such a decoupled abstraction efficiently on today's GPUs is itself challenging, VDCores addresses this through a GPU-specialized programming model and GPU runtime design that preserves the flexibility while minimizing implementation overhead. Across four LLM inference workloads on GH200, H100, and RTX 6000 Pro GPUs, VDCores significantly improves decoding throughput by 24% on average and by up to 77% under dynamic inputs, while reducing kernel programming and specialization effort by 90%. We have open sourced VDCores at https://github.com/vdcores/vdcores.

</details>


### [3] [On Solving Problems of Substantially Super-linear Complexity in $N^{o(1)}$ Rounds in the MPC Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03376)
*Andrzej Lingas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在本地内存受限且机器数量不超过N的MPC模型中，若试图用N^{o(1)}轮协议解决显著超线性时间复杂度的问题，则单机每轮局部计算的平均时间复杂度指数必须高于该问题本身的时间复杂度指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在MPC模型中是否可以用极少轮次（N^{o(1)}轮）的并行协议来解决那些在串行计算下需要显著超线性时间复杂度的问题，从而探索并行计算在受限资源下加速复杂问题的可能性与极限。

Method: 在MPC模型下对问题的计算复杂度进行理论分析，设定机器数量不超过N、本地内存受限的条件，推导在N^{o(1)}轮协议中单机每轮局部计算的平均时间复杂度与原问题时间复杂度之间的指数关系。

Result: 证明了当MPC机器不具备相对较大的本地内存且机器数量不超过N时，N^{o(1)}轮协议中单机每轮局部计算的平均时间复杂度（以本地内存大小计）的指数必须大于原问题时间复杂度的指数。

Conclusion: 在本地内存受限、机器数量有限的MPC模型下，用极少轮次的并行协议显著加速超线性时间复杂度问题是困难的，局部计算的复杂度下界被严格约束。

Abstract: We study the possibility of designing $N^{o(1)}$-round protocols for
  problems of substantially super-linear polynomial-time (sequential)
  complexity in the model of Massively Parallel Computation, where $N$
  is the input size. We show that if the machines are not equipped
  with relatively large local memory and their number does not exceed
  $N$, then the exponent of the average time complexity of the local
  computation performed by a machine in a round (in terms of local
  memory size) in such protocols must be larger than the exponent of
  the time complexity of the given problem.

</details>


### [4] [Enhancing Performance Insight at Scale: A Heterogeneous Framework for Exascale Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03561)
*Dragana Grbic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个加速的HPC性能分析基础设施，通过高性能C++ API和GPU并行技术实现了大规模遥测数据的快速诊断，并在实际超算系统上验证了其显著加速效果和实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着E级计算系统达到前所未有的并发规模，传统的性能分析工具在海量遥测数据处理开销方面面临巨大挑战，亟需一种能够高效处理大规模并行系统性能数据的新型分析基础设施。

Method: 开发了基于高性能C++ API和GPU并行加速的hpcanalysis框架，并实现了拓扑感知工作流，将逻辑性能异常映射到物理Slingshot互连坐标，同时集成了三维性能模型以重构迭代行为。

Result: C++ API在Aurora上处理100,000个MPI rank的数据摄入时间为9.69秒；GPU加速层在分析100,000条执行轨迹时相比CPU处理实现高达314倍加速；在Frontier上对GAMESS工作负载识别出32.28%的潜在加速机会；成功定位了Aurora上22个不同机架的网络拥塞。

Conclusion: 该加速基础设施成功解决了E级系统的性能分析瓶颈，实现了大规模并行系统的高吞吐量诊断，并通过与外部工具的无缝集成提供了复杂的分析模型，证明了其在实际超算环境中的实用价值。

Abstract: As exascale systems reach unprecedented concurrency, traditional performance analysis tools struggle with the overhead of massive-scale telemetry. We present an accelerated infrastructure for the hpcanalysis framework that leverages a high-performance C++ API and GPU parallelism to enable high-throughput diagnostics. Our C++ API achieves a 9.69-second ingestion time for 100,000 MPI ranks on Aurora. Furthermore, our GPU-accelerated layer achieves up to 314x speedup over CPU-based processing when analyzing 100,000 execution traces. Finally, we implement a topology-aware workflow that maps logical performance outliers to physical Slingshot interconnect coordinates, localizing network congestion across 22 distinct racks on Aurora.
  We also demonstrate how the framework's advanced interface seamlessly integrates with external tools to provide sophisticated analytical models. We introduce a novel tri-dimensional performance model that "re-materializes" iterative behavior from execution traces; using this model, we identified a 32.28% potential speedup for a GAMESS workload on Frontier.

</details>


### [5] [Lifting to tensors when compiling scientific computing workloads for AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03566)
*Nick Brown,Gabriel Rodriguez-Canal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将OpenMP标注的科学计算循环自动映射到AMD AI引擎(AIE)的编译流程，无需大量代码修改即可在NPU上获得与多核CPU相当的性能，同时显著降低能耗。CPU-NPU协同执行可实现最高40%的性能提升和15%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用架构（如FPGA和AMD AI引擎）在科学计算中具有能效和性能优势，且AIE已集成到AMD CPU中，特别适合边缘计算等资源受限场景。然而，将现有代码移植到AIE架构需要大量专业知识和时间成本，这是阻碍其广泛应用的主要挑战。

Method: 提出一种编译流水线，通过将应用程序语义提升到张量表示来捕获OpenMP标注循环的意图。利用高层张量信息的丰富性，实现通用科学计算循环到AIE的高效映射，仅需OpenMP装饰的循环即可，大幅降低目标架构的代码复杂性。

Result: 在6个AI和科学计算内核基准测试中，NPU在float32精度下的性能与多核CPU相当，且在所有情况下均降低了能耗。对于两个科学计算内核，CPU与NPU协同执行相比仅使用CPU实现了最高40%的性能提升和15%的能耗降低。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了通用科学计算代码到AIE架构的低成本移植，证明了高层张量语义在架构映射中的有效性。CPU-NPU异构协同执行可为科学计算应用带来显著的性能和能效收益，为边缘计算等资源受限场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: It has been demonstrated that specialised architectures, such as FPGAs and AMD's AI Engines (AIEs), have the potential to deliver energy and performance advantages for scientific computing. Given the integration of AIEs into AMD's CPUs, this is an interesting potential avenue especially when executing on the edge or making better use of local compute constrained resources. However, a major challenge is in enabling existing codes to run on this architecture without extensive modification. Put simply, it requires significant expertise and time to port codes to the AIE's execution model.
  In this paper we explore a compilation pipeline for efficiently mapping loops in general purpose, scientific codes to AIEs. Lifting the semantics of an application into tensors, we demonstrate that this is able to capture the intention of general purpose loops annotated with OpenMP and such high-level tensor information provides a richness that is effective when mapping to the AIEs. Requiring only an OpenMP decorated loop, our approach significantly reduces code complexity when targeting the architecture. For six kernel benchmarks, representing AI and scientific computing, using our approach the NPU performs comparatively to the multicore CPU for float32, in all cases at reduced energy to solution. For two scientific computing kernels running across both the CPU and NPU together delivers up to a 40% improvement in performance and 15% reduction in energy usage compared to the CPU alone.

</details>


### [6] [A Workflow-Oriented Framework for Asynchronous Human-AI Collaboration in Hybrid and Compute-Intensive HPC Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03743)
*Sergio Mendoza,Cedric Bhihe,Natalia Zamora,David Modesto,Jose Martin Bugallo Batalla,Jesus Gomez Canovas,Rafel Palomo Avellaneda,Miguel Perez Espinosa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种支持异步人机协作的工作流框架，解决了高计算强度HPC环境中实时交互困难的问题，允许在保持计算任务运行的同时进行人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险的国防和安全背景下部署AI系统时，人工参与至关重要。然而，由于计算强度高和资源限制，在HPC环境中进行实时人机交互是不切实际的，需要一种能够支持异步协作的解决方案。

Method: 开发了一个工作流框架，支持跨混合基础设施（包括HPC集群、本地机器和云平台）的异步人机协作。该框架可在定义的检查点暂停工作流以接收人工输入，而无需停止底层的计算任务，从而防止资源闲置并实现非阻塞式监督。框架支持SLURM调度、容器化和原生任务。

Result: 在MareNostrum 5等系统上的模型训练中成功应用了该框架，验证了其在可移植性、效率和操作AI工作流监督方面的优势，实现了在不中断计算资源的情况下进行人工干预。

Conclusion: 该框架有效地解决了HPC环境中人机协作的挑战，为需要人工判断和适应性的AI工作流提供了一种高效、可移植且具备监督能力的解决方案，适用于高计算需求的关键应用场景。

Abstract: Human involvement is critical in training and deploying AI systems in high-stakes defence and security contexts. However, real-time interaction is impractical in HPC environments due to compute intensity and resource constraints. We present a workflow framework that enables asynchronous human-AI collaboration across hybrid infrastructures, including HPC clusters, local machines, and cloud platforms. Workflows can pause at defined checkpoints for human input without halting underlying compute jobs, preventing idle resources and enabling non-blocking supervision. The framework supports interaction with SLURM-based scheduling, containerized and native tasks, and is customized for scenarios requiring human judgment and adaptability. We demonstrate its application in model training on systems like MareNostrum 5, highlighting benefits in portability, efficiency, and oversight in operational AI workflows.

</details>


### [7] [Implementing True MPI Sessions and Evaluating MPI Initialization Scalability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03983)
*Hui Zhou,Kenneth Raffenetti,Yanfei Guo,Michael Wilkins,Rajeev Thakur*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文描述了MPICH为实现真正的MPI Sessions功能所进行的重大内部重构，证明了真正的Sessions能通过显式分层设计提供显著的可扩展性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI-4标准引入的Sessions模型旨在消除对MPI_COMM_WORLD的依赖，为百亿亿次系统提供更好的可扩展性。然而，MPICH虽然在发布时支持MPI-4标准，但内部仍依赖全局世界通信器，未能实现Sessions模型的完整设计意图。

Method: 对MPICH进行重大内部重构，描述支持真正Sessions所需的架构变更，并采用显式分层设计方法来实现MPI与MPI_COMM_WORLD的解耦。

Result: 实验评估表明，真正的Sessions通过采用显式分层设计，能够提供显著的可扩展性优势。

Conclusion: 通过内部架构重构实现的真正Sessions支持能够有效提升MPI实现的可扩展性，证明了Sessions模型在百亿亿次系统中的价值。

Abstract: Sessions is one of the major features introduced in the MPI-4 standard. It offers an alternative to the traditional world communicator model by allowing applications to construct communicators from process sets, thereby eliminating the dependency on MPI_COMM_WORLD. The Sessions model was proposed as a more scalable solution for exascale systems, where MPI_COMM_WORLD was viewed as a potential scalability bottleneck. However, supporting Sessions is a significant challenge for established codebases like MPICH due to the deep integration of the world model in traditional MPI implementations. Although MPICH added support for the MPI-4 standard upon its release, it still internally relied on a global world communicator. This approach enabled applications written using the Sessions model to function, but it did not fulfill the full design intent of Sessions, which meant to decouple MPI from MPI_COMM_WORLD. We describe MPICH effort to support true MPI Sessions, including a major internal refactoring. We describe the architectural changes required to support true Sessions and evaluate the resulting implementation scalability. Our results demonstrate that true Sessions can offer significant scalability benefits by adopting explicit hierarchical designs.

</details>

### [8] [StateSMix: Online Lossless Compression via Mamba State Space Models and Sparse N-gram Context Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02904)
*Roberto Tacconelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了StateSMix，一种无需预训练、纯CPU运行的轻量级无损压缩器，结合Mamba风格状态空间模型与稀疏n-gram混合，在enwik8基准上超越LZMA2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无损压缩方法依赖预训练模型或大规模外部依赖，难以在资源受限环境中高效部署。本文旨在构建一个完全自包含、从零训练、无需GPU与外部依赖的压缩系统，以实现高效、通用的数据压缩。

Method: 提出StateSMix框架，核心包括：1）在线逐token训练的Mamba风格状态空间模型（约120K参数），实时预测BPE token概率；2）九个稀疏n-gram哈希表（2-gram至32-gram）通过softmax不变的logit偏置机制提供精确模式记忆；3）熵自适应缩放机制动态调节n-gram贡献，避免神经模型已校准时过度修正；4）采用算术编码完成最终压缩，实现为纯C代码并支持AVX2 SIMD与OpenMP并行化。

Result: 在enwik8基准上，StateSMix在1MB、3MB、10MB输入上分别达到2.123、2.149、2.162 bpb，相比xz -9e（LZMA2）分别提升8.7%、5.4%、0.7%。消融实验表明SSM单独贡献46.6%的压缩提升并可在无n-gram时超越xz，n-gram表额外带来4.1%增益。OpenMP并行化在4核上实现1.9倍加速，系统在普通x86-64硬件上处理约2000 tokens/秒。

Conclusion: StateSMix证明了将轻量级状态空间模型与传统n-gram方法结合，可在无预训练、无GPU条件下实现超越经典LZMA2的压缩性能，为资源受限环境下的高效无损压缩提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present StateSMix, a fully self-contained lossless compressor that couples an online-trained Mamba-style State Space Model (SSM) with sparse n-gram context mixing and arithmetic coding. The model is initialised from scratch and trained token-by-token on the file being compressed, requiring no pre-trained weights, no GPU, and no external dependencies. The SSM (DM=32, NL=2, approximately 120K active parameters per file) provides a continuously-updated probability estimate over BPE tokens, while nine sparse n-gram hash tables (bigram through 32-gram, 16M slots each) add exact local and long-range pattern memorisation via a softmax-invariant logit-bias mechanism that updates only non-zero-count tokens. An entropy-adaptive scaling mechanism modulates the n-gram contribution based on the SSM's predictive confidence, preventing over-correction when the neural model is already well-calibrated. On the standard enwik8 benchmark, StateSMix achieves 2.123 bpb on 1 MB, 2.149 bpb on 3 MB, and 2.162 bpb on 10 MB, beating xz -9e (LZMA2) by 8.7%, 5.4%, and 0.7% respectively. Ablation experiments establish the SSM as the dominant compression engine: it alone accounts for a 46.6% size reduction over a frequency-count baseline and beats xz without any n-gram component, while n-gram tables provide a complementary 4.1% gain through exact context memorisation. OpenMP parallelisation of the training loop yields 1.9x speedup on 4 cores. The system is implemented in pure C with AVX2 SIMD and processes approximately 2,000 tokens per second on commodity x86-64 hardware.

</details>


### [9] [eOptShrinkQ: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression Through Optimal Spectral Denoising and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02905)
*Pei-Chun Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了eOptShrinkQ，一种基于随机矩阵理论的Transformer KV缓存压缩方法。通过将KV缓存分解为低秩共享上下文和满秩残差，结合最优奇异值收缩和TurboQuant量化，在2.2比特下实现了优于TurboQuant 3.0比特的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到Transformer注意力头中的KV缓存可以自然分解为低秩的共享上下文分量和满秩的每词元残差，这种结构可以用spiked随机矩阵模型很好地描述。现有的量化方法在处理各向异性数据时需要额外的异常值处理和内积偏置校正，增加了系统复杂度。

Method: 提出eOptShrinkQ两阶段压缩流程：(1) 使用最优奇异值收缩算法(eOptShrink)自动提取共享结构，通过BBP相变自动选择秩；(2) 对满足薄壳性质且坐标离域化的残差使用TurboQuant进行标量量化。谱去噪恢复了标量量化所需的各向同性，消除了异常值处理和内积偏置校正的需求。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Ministral-8B上的实验表明：(1) 在每头MSE和内积保真度方面，eOptShrinkQ在相同质量下比TurboQuant节省近1比特/条目；(2) 在LongBench（16个任务）上，2.2比特/条目的eOptShrinkQ优于3.0比特的TurboQuant；(3) 在多针检索任务中，2.2比特的eOptShrinkQ接近或超过未压缩的FP16性能。

Conclusion: 基于随机矩阵理论的理论基础提供了三个保证：通过BBP相变的自动秩选择、残差上可证明的近零内积偏置、以及确保近似最优量化失真的坐标离域化。谱去噪可作为检索密集型任务的有益正则化器。

Abstract: We show that the key-value (KV) cache in transformer attention heads admits a natural decomposition into a low-rank \emph{shared context} component and a full-rank \emph{per-token} residual, well described by the spiked random matrix model. This observation leads to eOptShrinkQ, a two-stage compression pipeline: optimal singular value shrinkage (eOptShrink) automatically extracts the shared structure, and the residual -- which satisfies the \emph{thin shell property} with delocalized coordinates -- is quantized by TurboQuant~\citep{zandieh2025turboquant}, a recently proposed per-vector scalar quantizer with near-optimal distortion guarantees. By restoring the isotropy that scalar quantization assumes, spectral denoising eliminates the need for both outlier handling and dedicated inner product bias correction, freeing those bits for improved reconstruction.
  The theoretical grounding in random matrix theory provides three guarantees: automatic rank selection via the BBP phase transition, provably near-zero inner product bias on the residual, and coordinate delocalization ensuring near-optimal quantization distortion. Experimentally, we validate eOptShrinkQ on Llama-3.1-8B and Ministral-8B across three levels: per-head MSE and inner product fidelity, where eOptShrinkQ saves nearly one bit per entry over TurboQuant at equivalent quality; end-to-end on LongBench (16 tasks), where eOptShrinkQ at $\sim$2.2 bits per entry outperforms TurboQuant at 3.0 bits; and multi-needle retrieval, where eOptShrinkQ at 2.2 bits closely matches or exceeds uncompressed FP16, suggesting that spectral denoising can act as a beneficial regularizer for retrieval-intensive tasks.

</details>


### [10] [An End-to-End Framework for Building Large Language Models for Software Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02906)
*Jingkai He,Pengfei Chen,Chenghui Wu,Shuang Liang,Ye Li,Gou Tan,Xiadao Wen,Chuanfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpsLLM，一个面向软件运维领域的专用大语言模型，通过人机协同构建高质量数据、监督微调和引入领域过程奖励模型的强化学习，在QA和RCA任务上显著优于现有开源和闭源模型，并将开源三个版本及数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在软件运维领域尚未实现高效且有效的端到端智能运维，主要受限于数据质量低、知识碎片化以及学习不充分等问题。为了探索大语言模型在软件运维中的潜力，需要一个能够同时支持知识问答和根因分析的专用模型。

Method: 提出OpsLLM的构建流程：1）引入人在回路（Human-in-the-Loop）机制从海量运维原始数据中筛选高质量数据，构建微调数据集；2）基于该数据集进行监督微调得到基础模型；3）在强化学习阶段引入领域过程奖励模型（DPRM），优化模型在根因分析任务上的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 在多种难度的任务上，OpsLLM有效学习并融合了运维领域知识，在QA任务上准确率提升0.2%~5.7%，在RCA任务上提升2.7%~70.3%，均优于现有开源和闭源LLM，同时展现出强大的迁移能力。

Conclusion: OpsLLM证明了领域专用LLM在软件运维中的可行性与优越性，将开源7B、14B和32B三个参数版本及15K微调数据集，为后续研究和应用提供资源支持。

Abstract: In the field of software operations, Large Language Models (LLMs) have attracted increasing attention. However, existing research has not yet achieved efficient and effective end-to-end intelligent operations due to low-quality data, fragmented knowledge and insufficient learning. To explore the potential of LLMs in software operations, we propose OpsLLM, a domain-specific LLM that supports both knowledge-based question answering (QA) and root cause analysis (RCA). Moreover, we disclose the detailed workflow for building LLMs specifically in the software operations domain. First, a Human-in-the-Loop mechanism is introduced to curate highquality data from a large collection of operational raw data and construct a fine-tuning dataset. Then, based on the data, supervised fine-tuning is conducted to achieve a base model. Furthermore, we introduce a domain process reward model (DPRM) during the reinforcement learning stage to optimize the accuracy and reliability of the fine-tuned model on RCA tasks. Experimental results on the tasks with diverse difficulties demonstrate that OpsLLMs effectively learns and aligns with the operational domain knowledge infused, outperforming existing open-source and closed-source LLMs in accuracy with improvements of 0.2%~5.7% on QA tasks and 2.7% ~70.3% on RCA tasks, while exhibiting strong transferability. Moreover, we will open-source three versions of OpsLLM with 7B, 14B and 32B parameters, along with a 15K fine-tuning dataset.

</details>


### [11] [On the Invariants of Softmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02907)
*Wonsuk Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了“能量场”（行中心化的注意力logit），发现了两类不变性：机制级不变性（数学推导的零和约束、秩界、谱特征）和模型级规律（跨模型普遍存在的方差离域化现象），并提出了“键不相关性”概念，这些发现具有实际应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力将每个查询-键交互映射为概率分布，但其底层结构尚未被深入探索。本文旨在揭示注意力机制中潜在的数学结构和不变性规律。

Method: 定义“能量场”作为行中心化的注意力logit，从两个维度分析不变性：1）机制级不变性——基于softmax注意力的代数结构推导；2）模型级规律——在多个自回归语言模型家族上进行实证检验，分析不同上下文长度和输入文本下的表现。

Result: 发现机制级不变性包括：行零和约束、由头维度决定的秩界、以及由此衍生的谱特征。发现模型级规律：能量场方差在键位置上分布均匀（离域化），不集中于少数位置，这源于键矩阵的“键不相关性”属性。秩界将能量场限制在低维子空间中。

Conclusion: 这些不变性具有实际意义：秩界可用于理解注意力机制的表达能力限制；键不相关性可提供每头训练监控指标。所有结果在不同上下文长度和输入文本上均得到验证。

Abstract: Softmax attention maps every query--key interaction into a probability distribution, but the underlying structure remains largely unexplored. We define the \emph{energy field}, the row-centered attention logit, and show that it exhibits invariant properties across models, architectures, and inputs.
  Two classes of invariants emerge. \emph{Mechanism-level} invariants follow from the algebraic structure of softmax attention. They include a per-row zero-sum constraint, a rank bound determined by the head dimension, and spectral signatures that follow from them. \emph{Model-level} regularities are not required by the mechanism, yet hold in every autoregressive language model we test, spanning several architecture families. The energy field distributes its variance over key positions without concentrating at a few. This delocalization traces to a property of the key matrix we call \emph{key incoherence}.
  These invariants have practical consequences. The rank bound confines the energy field to a low-dimensional subspace. Key incoherence yields a per-head training monitor. All results are verified at multiple context lengths and input texts.

</details>


### [12] [Delay, Plateau, or Collapse: Evaluating the Impact of Systematic Verification Error on RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02909)
*Kazuki Egashira,Mark Vero,Jasper Dekoninck,Florian E. Dorner,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了系统性的验证错误对可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的影响。与以往认为错误仅减缓训练的结论不同，研究发现系统性的假阳性错误会导致模型性能从次优平台期到完全崩溃，其后果取决于错误的具体模式而非总体错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLVR研究主要将验证器错误视为随机的、独立的噪声，认为其仅会减缓训练速度而对最终性能影响有限。然而，现实中的验证器（如静态代码检查器）往往会产生系统性错误，这可能导致模型从结构性错误的奖励信号中学习到不良的一致性行为，因此需要深入研究此类系统性错误的影响。

Method: 通过在算术任务上进行受控实验，研究者系统地引入不同类型的验证错误，包括系统性假阴性（系统性地错误判定正确答案为错误）和系统性假阳性（系统性地错误判定错误答案为正确），并观察这些错误模式对RLVR训练过程和最终性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）系统性假阴性错误的影响类似于随机噪声；（2）系统性假阳性错误会导致广泛的行为差异，从次优性能平台期到性能完全崩溃；（3）关键发现是这些结果不由总体错误率决定，而是由引入的错误的具体模式决定，这使得预先缓解变得困难。

Conclusion: 与先前的结论相反，现实的验证错误能够关键性地塑造RLVR的结果，验证器的质量不能仅通过样本级别的错误率来理解。研究强调了在RLVR应用中需要更深入地理解和控制验证器的系统性错误模式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a powerful approach for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). While RLVR is designed for tasks with verifiable ground-truth answers, real-world verifiers (e.g., static code checkers) can introduce errors into the reward signal. Prior analyses have largely treated such errors as random and independent across samples, concluding that errors merely slow training with limited effect on final performance. However, practical verifiers tend to exhibit systematic errors. This introduces a risk of models learning unwanted consistent behavior from a structurally incorrect reward signal. In this work, we study the impact of such systematic verification errors on RLVR. Through controlled experiments on arithmetic tasks, we show that systematic false negatives lead to similar effects as random noise. On the other hand, systematic false positives can cause a wide range of behaviors from sub-optimal plateaus to performance collapse. Crucially, these outcomes are not determined by the overall error rate but by the specific pattern of introduced errors, making pre-hoc mitigation difficult. Our results show that, in contrast to prior conclusions, realistic verification errors can critically shape RLVR outcomes and that verifier quality has to be understood beyond its sample-level error rate.

</details>


### [13] [Agentic AI-Based Joint Computing and Networking via Mixture of Experts and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02911)
*Robert-Jeron Reifert,Alaa Alameer Ahmad,Hayssam Dahrouj,Aydin Sezgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于智能体AI的网络优化框架，将混合专家架构与大语言模型相结合，利用LLM作为语义网关动态编排优化专家，在通信计算网络中实现了接近最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来的6G移动网络将配备多样化的专业化优化专家，但如何根据高层意图和不确定性描述来选择、组合和编排这些专家成为一个关键挑战，需要可扩展的机制来实现这一目标。

Method: 提出一种基于智能体AI的网络优化框架，将混合专家架构与大语言模型集成，其中LLM作为语义网关对运营商目标进行推理并动态组合合适的优化智能体；以模型无关的方式构建框架，将人类可读的网络意图与底层资源分配决策桥接；以联合通信计算网络为例，设计了涵盖吞吐量、公平性和时延驱动目标的专用优化专家库。

Result: 数值仿真表明，该智能体MoE框架在与穷举专家组合相比时一致实现接近最优的性能，同时在包括时延最小化和吞吐量最大化在内的多种目标上优于单个专家的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功实现了异构目标和操作条件下的灵活优化，为6G网络中专业化优化专家的智能编排提供了有效的解决方案，验证了基于LLM的智能体方法在网络优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Future sixth-generation (6G) mobile networks are envisioned to be equipped with a diverse set of powerful, yet highly specialized, optimization experts. Such a promising vision is concurrently expected to give rise to the need for scalable mechanisms that can select, combine, and orchestrate such experts based on high-level intent and uncertainty descriptions. In this paper, we propose an agentic artificial intelligence (AI)-based network optimization framework that integrates mixture of experts (MoE) architectures with large language models (LLMs). Under the proposed framework, the employed LLM acts as a semantic gate to reason over operator objectives and dynamically compose suitable optimization agents. The proposed framework is formulated in a model-agnostic manner and bridges human-readable network intents with low-level resource allocation decisions, enabling flexible optimization across heterogeneous objectives and operating conditions. As a representative instantiation, we apply the framework to a joint communication and computing network and design a library of specialized optimization experts covering throughput, fairness, and delay-driven objectives under both regular and robust conditions. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed agentic MoE framework consistently achieves near-optimal performance compared to exhaustive expert combinations while outperforming individual experts across diverse objectives, including delay minimization and throughput maximization.

</details>


### [14] [Generate, Filter, Control, Replay: A Comprehensive Survey of Rollout Strategies for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02913)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Xunyi Jiang,Chuhan Wang,Zhenwei Tang,Difan Jiao,Zihan Huang,Yuxin Xiong,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Xintong Li,Raghav Jain,Nikki Kuang,Sizhe Zhou,Bowen Jin,Zhendong Chu,Tong Yu,Ryan Rossi,Kuan-Hao Huang,Jingbo Shang,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述提出了GFCR（生成-过滤-控制-回放）生命周期分类法，为基于强化学习的大型语言模型推理后训练提供了一个优化器无关的轨迹策略统一框架，并分析了可靠性、覆盖率和成本敏感性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型语言模型的强化学习后训练中，轨迹设计决定了优化器学习的数据质量，但现有研究往往对此报告不足。缺乏统一的框架来系统化地理解和分析轨迹策略，这导致了方法之间的比较和复现困难。

Method: 提出了Generate-Filter-Control-Replay (GFCR)生命周期分类法，将轨迹管道分解为四个模块化阶段：生成（提出候选轨迹）、过滤（通过验证器构建中间信号）、控制（在预算下分配计算并做出决策）、回放（跨轨迹保留和重用样本）。同时引入了可靠性、覆盖率和成本敏感性的标准分类体系来刻画轨迹权衡。

Result: 成功整合了多种方法，包括可验证奖励的强化学习、过程监督、基于评判者的门控、引导式和树/段轨迹、自适应计算分配、早退和部分轨迹、吞吐量优化，以及用于自我改进的回放/重组方法。通过数学、代码/SQL、多模态推理、工具使用智能体等案例研究验证了框架的有效性，并提供了诊断索引来映射常见轨迹病理与GFCR模块的缓解措施。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解和设计轨迹策略提供了统一视角，有助于构建可复现、计算高效且值得信赖的轨迹管道。同时指出了开放性挑战，为未来的研究方向提供了指导，促进了大型语言模型推理能力的系统性提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training tool for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs). In these systems, the rollout, the trajectory sampled from a prompt to termination, including intermediate reasoning steps and optional tool or environment interactions, determines the data the optimizer learns from, yet rollout design is often underreported. This survey provides an optimizer-agnostic view of rollout strategies for RL-based post-training of reasoning LLMs. We formalize rollout pipelines with unified notation and introduce Generate-Filter-Control-Replay (GFCR), a lifecycle taxonomy that decomposes rollout pipelines into four modular stages: Generate proposes candidate trajectories and topologies; Filter constructs intermediate signals via verifiers, judges, critics; Control allocates compute and makes continuation/branching/stopping decisions under budgets; and Replay retains and reuses artifacts across rollouts without weight updates, including self-evolving curricula that autonomously generate new training tasks. We complement GFCR with a criterion taxonomy of reliability, coverage, and cost sensitivity that characterizes rollout trade-offs. Using this framework, we synthesize methods spanning RL with verifiable rewards, process supervision, judge-based gating, guided and tree/segment rollouts, adaptive compute allocation, early-exit and partial rollouts, throughput optimization, and replay/recomposition for self-improvement. We ground the framework with case studies in math, code/SQL, multimodal reasoning, tool-using agents, and agentic skill benchmarks that evaluate skill induction, reuse, and cross-task transfer. Finally, we provide a diagnostic index that maps common rollout pathologies to GFCR modules and mitigation levers, alongside open challenges for building reproducible, compute-efficient, and trustworthy rollout pipelines.

</details>


### [15] [AutoRAGTuner: A Declarative Framework for Automatic Optimization of RAG Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02967)
*Xintan Zeng,Yongchao Liu,Yice Luo,Jiajun Zhen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AutoRAGTuner，一个声明式、配置驱动的自动化框架，用于统一管理RAG系统的全生命周期并自动优化超参数，在降低工程开销的同时提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强生成（RAG）系统性能高度依赖于复杂的架构设计和超参数配置，当前主要依赖低效的人工调优，缺乏系统化的自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出了AutoRAGTuner框架，采用模块化架构通过组件注册机制解耦管道阶段；引入领域元素模型（DEM）统一异构数据表示；集成自适应贝叶斯优化引擎实现端到端的超参数自动调优。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在从基础到基于图的多种RAG管道中均优于默认基线；声明式配置语言使架构调整的代码变更量减少了高达95%，显著降低了工程开销。

Conclusion: AutoRAGTuner为构建可演化、可复用的RAG系统提供了一个系统化可优化的基础，有效解决了RAG系统架构复杂和调优困难的问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances LLMs, but performance is highly sensitive to complex architecture designs and hyper-parameter configurations, which currently rely on inefficient manual tuning. We present AutoRAGTuner, a declarative, configuration-driven framework that automates the RAG life cycle: construction, execution,evaluation, and optimization. AutoRAGTuner employs a modular architecture to decouple pipeline stages through a component registration mechanism. To unify heterogeneous data, we introduce the Domain-Element Model (DEM), representing objects as atomic elements with bidirectional pointers to support nodes, edges, and hyperedges. Furthermore, AutoRAGTuner integrates an adaptive Bayesian optimization engine for end-to-end hyper-parameter tuning. Experimental results demonstrate AutoRAGTuner's architectural generality: across diverse RAG pipelines, ranging from vanilla to graph-based, the framework consistently outperforms default baselines. Notably, AutoRAGTuner significantly mitigates engineering overhead, where its declarative configuration language enables a up to 95\% reduction in code churn for architectural adjustments. Overall, AutoRAGTuner provides a systematically optimizable foundation for building evolvable and reusable RAG systems.

</details>


### [16] [When Safety Geometry Collapses: Fine-Tuning Vulnerabilities in Agentic Guard Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02914)
*Ismail Hossain,Sai Puppala,Jannatul Ferdaus,Md Jahangir Alam,Yoonpyo Lee,Syed Bahauddin Alam,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在良性数据上进行标准领域微调会导致安全防护模型完全丧失安全对齐，本文揭示了潜在安全几何结构破坏是根本原因，并提出Fisher加权安全子空间正则化（FW-SSR）方法有效缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现安全防护模型在完全良性数据上进行标准领域专业化微调时会丢失所有安全对齐能力，这种失败并非来自对抗性操纵。为理解这一现象的根本原因并开发有效的缓解方法，作者研究了潜在安全几何结构（即指导分类的有害-良性表征边界）在良性微调下的演变过程。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：(1) 通过SVD提取逐层安全子空间，追踪良性微调下安全边界的演变，使用CKA等指标量化安全几何结构的破坏程度；(2) 提出Fisher加权安全子空间正则化（FW-SSR），结合基于对角Fisher信息的曲率感知方向权重和随任务-安全梯度冲突自适应缩放的λ_t参数，作为训练时惩罚项主动锐化安全子空间。

Result: Granite Guardian发生完全崩溃：拒绝率从85%降至0%，CKA降至零，100%输出变得模糊。专业化假设解释了这种灾难性脆弱性：集中的安全表征高效但极度脆弱。FW-SSR成功恢复Granite Guardian 75%的拒绝率（CKA=0.983），将WildGuard的攻击成功率降至3.6%（低于未修改基线）。结构表征几何（CKA、Fisher分数）比绝对位移指标更可靠地预测安全行为。

Conclusion: 良性微调对安全防护模型构成严重威胁，其根本机制是潜在安全几何结构的破坏。几何结构的集中程度决定了模型的脆弱性。基于几何的监控应成为智能体部署中防护模型评估的必要组成部分，FW-SSR为解决此问题提供了有效的训练时正则化方法。

Abstract: A guard model fine-tuned on entirely benign data can lose all safety alignment -- not through adversarial manipulation, but through standard domain specialization. We demonstrate this failure across three purpose-built safety classifiers -- LlamaGuard, WildGuard, and Granite Guardian -- deployed as protection layers in agentic AI pipelines, and show that it originates in the destruction of latent safety geometry: the structured harmful -- benign representational boundary that guides classification. We extract per-layer safety subspaces via SVD on class-conditional activation differences and track how this boundary evolves under benign fine-tuning. Granite Guardian undergoes complete collapse --  refusal rate drops from 85\% to 0\%, CKA falls to zero, and 100\% of outputs become ambiguous -- a severity exceeding prior findings on general-purpose LLMs, explained by the specialization hypothesis: concentrated safety representations are efficient but catastrophically brittle. To mitigate this, we propose Fisher-Weighted Safety Subspace Regularization (FW-SSR), a training-time penalty combining (i) curvature-aware direction weights derived from diagonal Fisher information and (ii) an adaptive $λ_t$ that scales with task-safety gradient conflict. FW-SSR recovers 75\% refusal on Granite Guardian (CKA = 0.983) and reduces WildGuard's Attack Success Rate to 3.6\% -- below the unmodified baseline -- by actively sharpening the safety subspace rather than merely anchoring it. Across all three models, structural representational geometry (CKA, Fisher score) predicts safety behavior more reliably than absolute displacement metrics, establishing geometry-based monitoring as a necessary component of guard model evaluation in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [17] [From Synthesis to Clinical Assistance: A Strategy-Aware Agent Framework for Autism Intervention based on Real Clinical Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02916)
*Junhong Lai,Shuzhong Lai,Yanhao Yu,Wanlin Chen,Chenyu Yan,Haifeng Li,Lin Yao,Yueming Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASDAgent框架，通过DoctorAgent和ChildAgent解决自闭症干预数据稀缺和策略不一致问题，实现高质量对话合成和临床决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)的AI辅助早期强化行为干预(EIBI)发展受限于数据稀缺；通用大语言模型(LLMs)难以严格遵循应用行为分析(ABA)的标准化程序，导致交互虽然语言流畅但策略不一致。

Method: 提出ASDAgent框架，包含两个专门组件：(i) 配备观察-思考-行动-纠正(O-T-A-C)推理循环的DoctorAgent，通过使ABA执行显式化和可控化解决LLM的策略崩溃问题；(ii) 利用概率行为建模的ChildAgent，缓解数据同质性，模拟多样化和非确定性的ASD反应模式。

Result: ASDAgent生成的对话紧密模仿人类治疗师的策略分布（KL散度：0.083）；在真实自闭症干预中，与人类专家的策略一致性接近80%；合成数据有效将专业临床知识蒸馏到小型语言模型(SLMs)中，显著增强其治疗能力。

Conclusion: ASDAgent成功统一了高保真干预对话合成和临床决策支持，为解决ASD干预中的数据稀缺和策略一致性问题提供了有效方案，并能将临床知识有效转移至小型模型中。

Abstract: The development of AI-assisted Early Intensive Behavioral Intervention (EIBI) for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is severely constrained by data scarcity. Furthermore, while Applied Behavior Analysis (ABA) serves as the gold standard for clinical intervention, general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to strictly adhere to its standardized procedures, often resulting in interactions that are linguistically fluent but strategically inconsistent. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{ASDAgent}, a strategy-aware framework designed to unify high-fidelity intervention dialogue synthesis and clinical decision support. \textsc{ASDAgent} incorporates two specialized components to solve distinct problems: (i) a \textsc{DoctorAgent} equipped with an Observe-Think-Act-Correct (O-T-A-C) reasoning loop, which resolves the issue of strategy collapse in LLMs by making ABA execution explicit and controllable; and (ii) a \textsc{ChildAgent} that utilizes probabilistic behavior modeling to mitigate data homogeneity, simulating diverse and non-deterministic ASD response patterns. Experiments demonstrate that dialogues generated by \textsc{ASDAgent} closely mirror the strategy distribution of human therapists (KL divergence: 0.083). In real autism intervention, \textsc{ASDAgent} achieves nearly 80\% strategic consistency with human experts. Moreover, we show that synthetic data produced by \textsc{ASDAgent} effectively distills professional clinical knowledge into small language models (SLMs), significantly enhancing their therapeutic capabilities.

</details>


### [18] [PRISM-CTG: A Foundation Model for Cardiotocography Analysis with Multi-View SSL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02917)
*Sheng Wong,Ravi Shankar,Beth Albert,Hao Fei,Lin Li,Imane Ben M'Barek,Manu Vatish,Gabriel Davis Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM-CTG，首个用于胎心宫缩图（CTG）分析的大规模自监督基础模型。它通过整合三个互补的预训练任务，利用大规模无标签临床数据学习可迁移的领域级表征，在7个下游任务中表现优异，并展现出强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有监督深度学习CTG分析模型受限于标注数据稀缺和患者队列规模小，导致大量具有生理信息价值的临床记录未被充分利用。为了突破这一局限，需要一种能够利用大规模无标签数据学习通用表征的方法。

Method: 提出了PRISM-CTG（Physiology-aware Representation Learning via Integrated Self-supervision and Metadata），一个临床导向的自监督基础模型。该模型采用多视图自监督框架，联合优化三个互补的代理任务：随机投影引导的掩码信号重建、临床变量预测和特征分类。每个任务配备专属的任务特定token以实现专业化表征学习，并通过受控交叉注意力机制促进临床上下文间的信息交换。创新性地将通常被忽视的患者元数据和领域知识转化为监督目标，引导学习具有临床意义的表征。

Result: 在产前和产时领域的7个下游CTG任务上进行了广泛实验，PRISM-CTG持续优于领域内基线和自监督学习基线。在两个数据集的外部验证中表现出强大的泛化能力，且性能可与在更大规模私有标注数据集上训练的研究结果相媲美。

Conclusion: 这是首个为CTG引入大规模基础模型并学习领域级表征的研究，证明了通过自监督学习利用大规模无标签临床数据进行生理感知表征学习的有效性，为CTG分析提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Supervised deep learning models for automated CTG analysis are typically constrained by narrowly curated labelled datasets and limited patient cohorts, leaving substantial volumes of physiologically informative clinical recordings untapped. To address this limitation, we propose Physiology-aware Representation Learning via Integrated Self-supervision and Metadata for CTG (PRISM-CTG), a clinically grounded self-supervised foundation model (FM) for CTG that leverages large-scale unlabelled recordings to learn transferable domain-level representations. PRISM-CTG is pretrained using a multi-view self-supervised framework that jointly optimises 3 complementary pretext objectives: random-projected guided masked signal reconstruction, clinical variable prediction, and feature classification. Each objective is associated with a dedicated task-specific token, enabling specialised representation learning, while controlled cross-attention facilitates information exchange across clinical context. By reframing patient metadata and domain knowledge, which are often underutilised in conventional training as prediction targets, Prism-CTG transforms readily available clinical information into additional supervisory targets that guide clinically meaningful representation learning. Extensive experiments across 7 downstream CTG tasks in both antepartum and intrapartum domains demonstrated that PRISM-CTG consistently outperforms in-domain and SSL baselines. Notably, PRISM-CTG demonstrated strong generalisation under external validation on 2 datasets, while achieving comparable performance to studies trained on substantially larger, privately labelled datasets. To our knowledge, this is the first study to introduce large-scale FM for CTG that learns domain-level representations.

</details>


### [19] [Mitigating the reconstruction-detection trade-off in VAE-based unsupervised anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02918)
*Agathe Senellart,Maëlys Solal,Stéphanie Allassonnière,Ninon Burgos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了β-VAE在无监督异常检测中存在重建质量与检测性能的权衡，并提出Sparse VAE方法有效缓解了这一矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器（VAE）被广泛用于无监督异常检测，但在完全无监督的设定下，模型选择（特别是超参数选择）仍是一个未解难题。现有方法通常通过最小化正常样本的重建误差来选择超参数，但这种做法的合理性缺乏深入探究。

Method: 1. 系统分析β-VAE模型中重建质量与异常检测性能之间的权衡关系。
2. 评估不同随机种子下模型性能的变异性，并分析其与正常/异常样本潜在分布距离的关联。
3. 提出并验证两种缓解权衡的方法：beta-scheduling和Sparse VAE。

Result: 研究发现潜在空间受约束的β-VAE模型虽然能达到更高的异常检测指标，但重建质量会降低；随机种子引起的性能波动与正常和异常潜在分布之间的距离有关；Sparse VAE在保持高重建质量的同时显著提升了检测性能。

Conclusion: β-VAE模型中存在重建质量与异常检测能力的固有权衡，通过引入稀疏性约束（Sparse VAE）可以有效改善这一状况，为无监督异常检测中的模型选择提供了新的理论依据和实践方案。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders are widely used for unsupervised anomaly detection. Model selection however remains an open-question: to remain fully unsupervised, hyperparameters are often chosen to minimize the reconstruction error on normal samples. In this paper, we reveal a trade-off between reconstruction quality and anomaly detection among $β$-VAE models. Models with constrained latent space reach higher detection metrics but lower reconstruction quality. We also assess the performance variability across random seeds and show it is linked to the distance between normal and abnormal latent distributions. From this analysis, we justify and investigate two methods to mitigate the reconstructiondetection tradeoff: beta-scheduling and the Sparse VAE. The latter especially shows an improvement in detection while maintaining high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [20] [Heterogeneous Graph Importance Scoring and Clustering with Automated LLM-based Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02919)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于开放数据（OSM）的城市桥梁重要性评估方法。通过构建异构图并计算五维社会影响指标，利用UMAP降维和HDBSCAN聚类发现桥梁功能原型，最后使用大语言模型（LLM）自动生成具有政策相关性的聚类解释，并在多城市间验证了方法的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市桥梁网络是关键基础设施，其中断会对交通、应急服务和经济活动产生级联式的严重影响。现有研究面临三大挑战：（1）如何仅利用开放数据源量化多维度的桥梁重要性；（2）如何在不同城市间发现通用的桥梁功能原型；（3）如何自动生成具有政策相关性的解释。

Method: 首先，利用OpenStreetMap（OSM）数据构建包含桥梁、道路网络、建筑物和公共设施的异构图。其次，计算五个社会影响指标：交通荒漠评分、医院可达性评分、隔离风险评分、供应链影响评分和绿地可达性评分，形成52维特征向量。接着，使用UMAP进行降维，并利用HDBSCAN进行基于密度的聚类。最后，采用经过温度优化的LLM（Elyza8b，基于建筑领域语料库训练）对聚类结果进行自动解释。

Result: （1）建立了一条从OSM数据到可操作桥梁重要性排名的完整开放数据流水线；（2）提出了五指标评分方法，并实现了40倍的算力优化；（3）构建了UMAP+HDBSCAN聚类框架，并在多城市数据上进行了验证；（4）开发了LLM解释方法论，包含温度优化和模型选择依据；（5）证明了该方法仅需调整配置即可在不同城市间迁移应用。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一个完全基于开放数据、可迁移且自动化的城市桥梁重要性评估框架。该框架不仅解决了数据获取难和解释性差的问题，还为城市基础设施管理和政策制定提供了高效的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Urban bridge networks are critical infrastructure whose disruption can cascade into severe impacts on transportation, emergency services, and economic activity. This paper presents a comprehensive methodology for assessing bridge importance through heterogeneous graph analysis, unsupervised clustering, and automated interpretation via large language models (LLMs). Our approach addresses three fundamental challenges: (1) quantifying multi-dimensional bridge importance using only open data sources, (2) discovering functional bridge archetypes across different cities, and (3) generating policy-relevant interpretations automatically. We construct heterogeneous graphs from OpenStreetMap (OSM) data incorporating bridges, road networks, buildings, and public facilities. Five social impact indicators are computed: transit desert score, hospital access score, isolation risk score, supply chain impact score, and green space access score. These 52-dimensional feature vectors undergo dimensionality reduction via UMAP and density-based clustering via HDBSCAN. Discovered clusters are interpreted using temperature-optimized LLMs (Elyza8b, trained on construction domain corpus). (1) A complete open-data pipeline from OSM to actionable bridge importance rankings, (2) a five-indicator scoring methodology with 40$\times$ computational optimization, (3) a UMAP+HDBSCAN clustering framework validated on multi-city data, (4) an LLM interpretation methodology including temperature optimization and model selection rationale, and (5) transferability demonstration across cities via configuration-only adaptation.

</details>


### [21] [DeRelayL: Sustainable Decentralized Relay Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02935)
*Haihan Duan,Tengfei Ma,Yuyang Qin,Runhao Zeng,Wei Cai,Victor C. M. Leung,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DeRelayL的去中心化接力学习新范式，旨在解决大规模模型训练资源门槛高、普通用户难以受益的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器学习模型训练需要高昂的算力和资金成本，普通用户（如移动用户）虽为数据的主要生产者，却难以参与并从中受益。现有的协作训练范式（如联邦学习）主要关注隐私保护和群体模型聚合，缺乏可持续性和开放的参与机制，这导致了技术资源分配的不平等。

Method: 提出了去中心化接力学习（DeRelayL）系统。该系统允许无许可的参与者以接力（relay-like）的方式贡献于模型训练并共享模型。文章详细阐述了DeRelayL的架构与工作流程，并设计了激励机以确保系统的可持续性。

Result: 通过理论分析和数值仿真，验证了DeRelayL系统的有效性，证明了该架构和工作流在激励参与者贡献方面的可行性。

Conclusion: DeRelayL提供了一种可持续的协作训练解决方案，打破了大规模模型训练的资源壁垒，使得普通用户能够参与模型训练并共享成果，促进了人工智能技术的普惠化。

Abstract: In the era of big data, large-scale machine learning models have revolutionized various fields, driving significant advancements. However, large-scale model training demands high financial and computational resources, which are only affordable by a few technological giants and well-funded institutions. In this case, common users like mobile users, the real creators of valuable data, are often excluded from fully benefiting due to the barriers, while the current methods for accessing large-scale models either limit user ownership or lack sustainability. This growing gap highlights the urgent need for a collaborative model training approach, allowing common users to train and share models. However, existing collaborative model training paradigms, especially federated learning (FL), primarily focus on data privacy and group-based model aggregation. To this end, this paper intends to address this issue by proposing a novel training paradigm named decentralized relay learning (DeRelayL), a sustainable learning system where permissionless participants can contribute to model training in a relay-like manner and share the model. In detail, this paper presents the architecture and workflow of DeRelayL, designs incentive mechanisms to ensure sustainability, and conducts theoretical analysis and numerical simulations to demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [22] [From Packets to Patterns: Interpreting Encrypted Network Traffic as Longitudinal Behavioral Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01616)
*Rameen Mahmood,Omar El Shahawy,Souptik Barua,Zachary Beattie,Jeffrey Kaye,Xuhai "Orson'' Xu,Chao-Yi Wu,Danny Yuxing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究验证了加密智能手机网络流量可作为被动感知模态，用于捕捉睡眠、压力和孤独感相关的行为模式，并揭示了三种心理状态具有不同的时间结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类行为难以大规模持续观测，但日常设备使用会留下可测量的痕迹。研究旨在探索加密智能手机网络流量这一 ubiquitous、始终在线的被动感知模态，能否被动捕捉睡眠、压力和孤独感相关的行为模式。

Method: 采用带有每用户适配器的Transformer骨干网络建模共享行为结构，使其能表征典型个体行为及其偏差。应用稀疏自编码器提取可解释的行为特征，使用带Mundlak分解的广义估计方程，将个体间差异与个体内随时间变化分离。

Result: 发现三种心理结果反映不同的时间结构：压力主要与稳定的个体间差异相关，孤独感与个体内变化相关，睡眠障碍则两者兼具。这些个体内动态无法通过预定义的网络流量特征捕捉，证明了学习表征在纵向行为感知中的价值。

Conclusion: 研究确立了加密网络流量作为可行被动感知模态的地位，能揭示可解释的行为动态（特别是偏离个体基线的偏差），这些动态在原始流量特征中不可见。

Abstract: Human behavior is difficult to observe continuously at scale, yet it leaves measurable traces in everyday device use. We test whether encrypted smartphone network traffic -- a ubiquitous, always-on, passive sensing modality -- can passively capture behavioral patterns related to sleep, stress, and loneliness. We model shared behavioral structure using a transformer backbone with per-user adapters, allowing the model to represent both typical individual behavior and deviations from it. To make these representations interpretable, we apply a sparse autoencoder to extract behavioral features corresponding to distinct patterns of activity. We relate these features to sleep disturbance, stress, and loneliness using generalized estimating equations with Mundlak decomposition, separating between-person differences from within-person changes over time. We find that the three outcomes reflect distinct temporal structures: stress is primarily associated with stable between-person differences, loneliness with within-person variation, and sleep disturbance with a combination of both. Notably, these within-person dynamics are not captured by predefined network-traffic features, demonstrating the value of learned representations for longitudinal behavioral sensing. These results establish encrypted network traffic as a viable passive sensing modality, revealing interpretable behavioral dynamics -- particularly deviations from an individual's baseline -- that are not visible in raw traffic features.

</details>


### [23] [Proteo-R1: Reasoning Foundation Models for De Novo Protein Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02937)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Heli Qi,Hanqun Cao,Heng-Jui Chang,Zeqi Zhou,Haokai Zhao,Ma Jian,Carl Ma,Yu-Chi Cheng,Kuan Pang,Xiangru Tang,Zehong Wang,Guanlue Li,Hanchen Wang,Kejun Ying,Pan Lu,Chiho Im,Seungju Han,Peng Xia,Tinson Xu,Yinxi Li,Deyao Zhu,Pheng-Ann Heng,Naoto Yokoya,Masashi Sugiyama,Li Erran Li,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Proteo-R1框架，通过双专家架构将蛋白质设计中的“分子理解”与“几何生成”解耦，实现了推理引导的可控设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习蛋白质设计模型缺乏显式推理能力，直接合成分子几何结构而不考虑关键功能残基或相互作用，导致设计决策与采样动态纠缠，限制了模型的可解释性、可控性以及生化知识的系统性复用。

Method: 采用双专家架构：1) 多模态大语言模型(MLLM)作为理解专家，分析序列、结构和文本语境，识别关键的官能团残基；2) 基于扩散模型的生成专家，将这些残基级决策作为硬约束进行条件协同设计。该架构模拟了人类专家"先推理关键相互作用，后在约束下优化几何结构"的工程流程。

Result: 通过将推理转化为显式的残基级承诺（而非潜在的文本引导），实现了大语言模型推理与先进几何生成模型的稳定、可解释、模块化集成，提供了代码、数据和演示。

Conclusion: Proteo-R1成功地将分子理解与几何生成解耦，通过显式的残基级推理实现了可控且可解释的蛋白质设计，为LLM与生成模型的集成提供了模块化框架。

Abstract: Deep learning in \emph{de novo} protein design has achieved atomic-level fidelity. However, existing models remain largely non-deliberative: they directly synthesize molecular geometries without explicitly reasoning about which residues or interactions are functionally essential. As a result, design decisions are entangled with continuous sampling dynamics, limiting interpretability, controllability, and systematic reuse of biochemical knowledge. We introduce \textbf{Proteo-R1}, a reasoning-guided protein design framework that explicitly decouples \emph{molecular understanding} from \emph{geometric generation}. Proteo-R1 adopts a dual-expert architecture in which a multimodal large language model (MLLM) serves as an \emph{understanding expert}, analyzing protein sequences, structures, and textual context to identify key functional residues that govern binding and specificity. These residue-level decisions are then passed as hard constraints to a separate diffusion-based \emph{generation expert}, which performs conditional co-design while respecting the fixed interaction anchors. This factorization mirrors how human experts approach molecular engineering: first, reasoning about critical interactions, then optimizing geometry subject to those constraints. By operationalizing reasoning as explicit residue-level commitments rather than latent textual guidance, Proteo-R1 achieves stable, interpretable, and modular integration of LLM reasoning with state-of-the-art geometric generative models. Code, data, and demos are available at https://smiles724.github.io/r1/.

</details>


### [24] [PAMNet: Cycle-aware Phase-Amplitude Modulation Network for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02938)
*Yingbo Zhou,Yutong Ye,Zhiwei Ling,Shuhao Li,Rui Qian,Jian Xiong,Li Sun,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAMNet模型，通过显式解耦周期性模式的相位和幅度分量，在12个数据集上实现了最先进的多变量时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多变量时间序列预测方法要么通过复杂的模型架构（如Transformer）隐式提取周期性导致计算开销高，要么在显式建模周期分量时忽略了内在的相位-幅度耦合关系。

Method: 提出周期感知相位-幅度调制网络（PAMNet），核心是双分支调制器：相位分支使用循环嵌入捕获相位依赖的均值偏移，幅度分支建模强度变化以适应方差变化，通过轻量级元素级融合高效结合这两个分量，无需复杂的注意力机制即可显式建模它们之间的交互。

Result: 在12个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法通过新颖的相位-幅度解耦机制达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: PAMNet通过显式的相位-幅度解耦机制为时间序列预测中的周期建模提供了新视角，有效解决了现有方法的计算开销和建模缺陷问题。

Abstract: Reliable periodic patterns serve as a fundamental basis for accurate multivariate time series forecasting. However, existing methods either implicitly extract periodicity through complex model architectures (e.g., Transformers) with high computational overhead or overlook the intrinsic phase-amplitude coupling when modeling periodic components explicitly. To address these issues, we propose a novel Cycle-aware Phase-Amplitude Modulation Network (PAMNet) that explicitly decomposes periodic patterns into complementary phase and amplitude components. The core innovation lies in its dual-branch modulator, featuring dedicated learnable embeddings for phase positioning and amplitude modulation. The phase branch employs cyclical embeddings to capture phase-dependent mean shifts, while the amplitude branch models intensity variations to adapt to changes in variance. A lightweight modulator with element-wise fusion efficiently combines these components, enabling explicit modeling of their interactions without complex attention mechanisms. Extensive experiments on twelve real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance through its novel phase-amplitude decoupling mechanism, offering a new perspective for cyclical modeling in time series forecasting.

</details>


### [25] [From Static Analysis to Audience Dissemination: A Training-Free Multimodal Controversy Detection Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02939)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuDisAgent，一种无需训练的多智能体框架，通过模拟受众传播过程来检测社交媒体视频中的争议性内容，并在评论稀疏场景下设计了评论引导策略，显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态争议检测方法将其视为静态表示学习任务，直接从视频和评论中提取特征，但未能捕捉不同受众群体的多样化观点和评价。这些方法忽略了内容传播过程中不同受众群体的动态交互和解读过程。

Method: 提出AuDisAgent框架，将争议检测重构为动态传播过程。包含三个阶段：(1)三个专门的筛选智能体（视频智能体、评论智能体和交互智能体）分别从视觉、文本和跨模态视角进行初步评估；(2)当智能体无法达成共识时，激活观众小组智能体模拟不同背景和立场观众之间的讨论；(3)仲裁智能体基于完整推理链做出最终判断。此外，针对新发布视频评论稀少的冷启动问题，设计了评论引导策略，利用语义相似视频的历史评论作为初始评论上下文。

Result: 在公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架在评论丰富和评论稀疏两种场景下均显著优于现有的最先进（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 通过模拟真实世界的内容传播过程和受众互动，AuDisAgent成功捕捉了不同受众群体对同一内容的多样化解读和反应，有效识别潜在的争议性内容，为社交媒体视频平台的风险管理提供了更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal controversy detection (MCD) identifies controversial content in videos and their associated user comments, to support risk management for social video platforms.Prior research frames MCD as a static representation learning task, where features are directly extracted from videos and their accompanying comments. However, these methods fail to capture the diverse perspectives and evaluations from different audience groups. Inspired by the real-world process of content dissemination among audiences, we propose AuDisAgent, a training-free multi-agent framework that reformulates MCD as a dynamic propagation process.Our framework explicitly models audience dissemination through a structured multi-agent system. First, three specialized Screening Agents (Video Agent, Comment Agent, and Interaction Agent) conduct initial assessments from visual, textual, and cross-modal perspectives, respectively. For samples where the three agents cannot reach a consensus, a Viewing Panel Agent is activated to simulate post-screening discussions among audiences with diverse backgrounds and stances. This mechanism models how different audience groups interpret and react to the same content, uncovering latent controversial content that may emerge during the dissemination process. Finally, an Arbitration Agent renders the final judgment based on the complete reasoning chain from the preceding steps.In addition, to address the "cold-start" scenario where newly released videos have few or no comments, we design a Comment Bootstrapping Strategy that leverages historical public comments from semantically similar videos as the initial comment context. Extensive experiments on a public dataset demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in both rich-comment and limited-comment scenarios.

</details>


### [26] [PrismAgent: Illuminating Harm in Memes via a Zero-Shot Interpretable Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02940)
*Zihan Ding,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PrismAgent，一个零样本、多智能体、可解释的有害模因检测框架，通过模拟刑事案件调查流程，在无需标注数据的情况下显著优于现有零样本检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害模因检测方法严重依赖大规模标注数据，导致训练成本高昂且泛化能力有限；模因的快速传播使得有害内容检测变得至关重要，亟需一种低依赖、高效且可解释的检测方法。

Method: 提出PrismAgent框架，将有害模因检测任务概念化为刑事案件调查流程，设计了四个专业化智能体协同工作：分析员智能体在善意和恶意假设下改写模因以探测潜在意图；调查员智能体从无标注数据集中检索支持证据并构建上下文解释；检察官智能体基于三种解释进行三次独立的初步判断；法官智能体综合所有证据做出最终裁决。这种显式的多阶段推理链使模型具有内在可解释性。

Result: 在三个公共数据集上的大量实验表明，PrismAgent显著优于现有的零样本检测方法。该框架不仅实现了高性能检测，还通过明确的中间步骤解释提供了良好的可解释性。

Conclusion: PrismAgent成功实现了零样本有害模因检测，通过创新的多智能体协作机制和类刑事调查流程，有效解决了标注数据依赖问题，同时保证了检测结果的可解释性，为有害内容检测领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid spread of memes makes harmful content detection increasingly crucial, as effective identification can curb the circulation of misinformation. However, existing methods rely heavily on high-volume annotated data, which leads to substantial training costs and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we propose PrismAgent, a zero-shot, multi-agent, interpretable framework. PrismAgent conceptualizes this task as a criminal case investigation, employing four specialized agents responsible for the analysis, investigation, prosecution, and judgment stages within a structured collaborative workflow. In the first stage, the analyst agent paraphrases each meme under benevolent and malicious assumptions to probe its underlying intent. The investigator agent then retrieves supporting evidence from an unannotated dataset and constructs contextual interpretations for the meme and its variants. Next, the prosecutor agent performs three independent preliminary judgments by pairing the original meme with each of the three interpretations. Finally, the judge agent deliberates across all evidence to render a final verdict. Moreover, PrismAgent's explicit multi-stage reasoning chain makes the model inherently interpretable, as every intermediate step is explicitly explained rather than only producing a final detection result. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that PrismAgent significantly outperforms existing zero-shot detection methods.

</details>


### [27] [A Framework for Exploring and Disentangling Intersectional Bias: A Case Study in Fetal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02942)
*Aya Elgebaly,Joris Fournel,Benjamin Laine Jønch Jurgensen,Kamil Mikolaj,Anders Christensen,Martin Tolsgaard,Claes Ladefoged,Aasa Feragen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 医学AI中的偏差不完全源于数据代表性不足，图像质量（与采集参数相关）也是关键因素。本文提出一个框架检测交叉性偏差，并在胎儿超声案例中发现像素间距（PS）是影响模型性能的重要且易混淆的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学AI偏差研究常将问题归结为数据代表性不足。然而，在胎儿超声等任务中，即使代表性充足，图像质量（受采集条件、操作者经验及母体BMI等影响）仍会导致性能差异，且可能掩盖复杂的交互或混淆效应。

Method: 提出一种结构化框架用于探索和检测交叉性偏差，该框架结合了无监督切片发现、系统性因子分析和针对性交叉评估。研究者将此框架应用于超过94,000张胎儿体重估计超声图像，对比分析了最先进的深度学习模型与临床标准Hadlock公式。

Result: 分析发现，像素间距（PS）是导致性能差异的持续驱动因素，较高的PS在特定子群中可使性能提升高达24%。由于PS常随高BMI或低孕龄（GA）调整，这带来了显著的混淆风险。交叉分析表明，部分PS相关信号可由GA解释，但PS相关的改进在BMI分层中持续存在。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了图像采集参数（如PS）在医学AI偏差中起关键作用，且常与临床和人口统计学因素产生交互或混淆。这强调了在医学AI公平性研究中，必须进行感知采集和感知交互的评估。

Abstract: Bias in medical AI is often framed as a problem of representation. However, in image-based tasks such as fetal ultrasound, performance disparities can arise even when representation is adequate, because predictive accuracy depends strongly on image quality. Image quality is shaped by acquisition conditions and operator expertise, as well as patient-dependent factors such as maternal body mass index (BMI), all of which may correlate with sensitive demographic features. Consequently, observed disparities may reflect the combined influence of demographic, clinical, and acquisition-related factors rather than data imbalance alone, and may obscure underlying interaction or confounding effects. We propose a structured framework to explore and detect intersectional bias, combining unsupervised slice discovery, systematic factor-wise analysis, and targeted intersectional evaluation. In a case study of over 94{,}000 ultrasound images for fetal weight estimation, we analyze bias in a state-of-the-art deep learning (DL) model and the clinical standard Hadlock, a regression formula using biometric measurements. Pixel spacing (PS) -- a parameter considered suboptimal in current acquisition protocols -- emerged as a consistent driver of performance differences, with higher PS associated with improvements of up to 24\% in selected subgroups for both models. Because PS is often adapted in cases of high BMI or low gestational age (GA), this effect carries a substantial risk of confounding. Our intersectional analysis revealed that part of the PS-associated signal is explained by GA, while PS-related improvements persist across BMI strata, highlighting the importance of acquisition-aware and interaction-aware evaluation in medical AI fairness research.

</details>


### [28] [Healthcare AI GYM for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02943)
*Minbyul Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TT-OPD框架，通过在多轮对话的每个回合提供密集的结果感知KL正则化，有效解决了医疗AI多轮强化学习中的训练不稳定和智能体坍塌问题，在18个基准测试中的10个上取得了最佳性能，平均提升3.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床推理需要多步骤交互（采集病史、开立检查、解读结果、制定治疗方案），但目前缺乏一个能够覆盖广泛临床领域并提供专业工具的统一训练环境来训练可泛化的医疗AI智能体。现有的多轮强化学习方法存在训练不稳定、智能体退化为冗长的单轮独白、工具使用频率下降等问题，这些问题源于稀疏的终端奖励与序列化临床轨迹之间的错配。

Method: 1. 构建了gym环境：一个兼容gymnasium的环境，涵盖10个临床领域、3.6K+任务、135个领域专用工具和828K医学知识库。
2. 提出Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD)框架：一个自蒸馏框架，其中无梯度的EMA教师利用结果特权信息，在每个对话回合提供密集的、结果感知的KL正则化，以改善训练效率和稳定性。

Result: 1. 分析发现多轮智能体结构退化为冗长的单轮独白，表现为单调的长度爆炸和工具使用频率的同步下降。
2. 发现vanilla GRPO在部分基准上准确率较高但训练不稳定，响应长度振荡明显且收敛周期长。
3. TT-OPD在18个基准测试中的10个上取得最佳性能，相比非RL基线平均提升+3.9 pp，且具有更快的早期收敛、可控的响应长度和持续的多轮工具使用。

Conclusion: TT-OPD框架有效解决了医疗AI多轮强化学习中的训练不稳定和智能体坍塌问题，通过密集的结果感知KL正则化实现了更好的性能和训练效率，为训练可泛化的医疗AI智能体提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning demands multi-step interactions -- gathering patient history, ordering tests, interpreting results, and making safe treatment decisions -- yet a unified training environment provides the breadth of clinical domains and specialized tools to train generalizable medical AI agents through reinforcement learning remains elusive. We present a comprehensive empirical study of multi-turn agentic RL for medical AI, built on \gym{}, a gymnasium-compatible environment spanning 10 clinical domains with 3.6K+ tasks, 135 domain-specific tools, and a knowledge base of 828K medical passages. Our analysis reveals that agentic multi-turn structure degrades into verbose single-turn monologues, characterized by monotonic length explosion and a simultaneous erosion of tool-use frequency. We characterize how this collapse, alongside distillation instability, stems from the misalignment of sparse terminal rewards with sequential clinical trajectories. We find that vanilla GRPO achieves strong final accuracy on some benchmarks but suffers from training instability, evidenced by significant oscillations in response length and prolonged convergence periods. To improve training efficiency and stability, we propose Turn-level Truncated On-Policy Distillation (TT-OPD), a self-distillation framework where a gradient-free EMA teacher leverages outcome-privileged information to provide dense, outcome-aware KL regularization at every conversation turn. TT-OPD achieves the best performance on 10 of 18 benchmarks with an average +3.9~pp improvement over the non-RL baseline with faster early convergence, controlled response length, and sustained multi-turn tool use.

</details>


### [29] [Exploring Pass-Rate Reward in Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02944)
*Xin-Ye Li,Ren-Biao Liu,Yun-Ji Zhang,Hui Sun,Zheng Xie,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在无评论家的代码生成强化学习中，尽管测试用例通过率奖励缓解了奖励稀疏问题，但并未比二元奖励带来可靠的性能提升，原因在于梯度更新方向不一致且奖励信号存在误校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单元测试反馈的强化学习方法常因二元奖励（全通过/未通过）过于稀疏而导致学习困难。为解决此问题，学界常采用测试用例通过率作为替代奖励。本文旨在深入研究这种替代奖励在无评论家强化学习（如GRPO和RLOO）中的实际有效性及其内在机制。

Method: 研究者针对无评论家的强化学习算法（例如GRPO和RLOO）进行了一系列严格的对照实验。通过分析奖励密度和梯度方向，深入探究了测试用例通过率奖励的优化动力学，并考察了部分通过解对梯度更新的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管测试用例通过率奖励确实提供了更密集的奖励信号，但在受控实验中，它并未比二元奖励带来可靠的最终性能提升。分析发现，这是因为该奖励作为完全正确性的替代指标存在误校准，且同一组内的部分通过解会引发相互冲突的梯度方向，导致梯度相互抵消，无法有效推动模型向完全正确的解移动。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，在无评论家的强化学习设置下，仅依靠测试用例通过率奖励不足以有效提升代码生成能力。这表明未来的研究需要设计更优的奖励机制，使其优化目标与“完全正确”这一最终目标更好地对齐。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) from unit-test feedback has become a standard post-training recipe for improving large language models (LLMs) on code generation. However, the pass-all-tests binary reward can be sparse, yielding no learning signal on challenging problems where none of the sampled solutions passes all tests. A common remedy is to use the test-case pass rate as a surrogate reward.
  In this work, we study pass-rate rewards in critic-free RL for code generation (e.g., GRPO and RLOO) and report a consistent pattern across base models and algorithms: despite alleviating reward sparsity, pass-rate rewards do not reliably improve final performance over binary rewards in rigorous controlled experiments.
  To understand this discrepancy, we analyze reward density and the resulting gradient directions. We find that pass-rate rewards are denser, but the induced gradient updates do not consistently move probability mass toward full-pass solutions. This arises because test-case pass rate is a miscalibrated surrogate for progress toward full correctness, and partial-pass solutions within the same group can induce conflicting gradient directions that cancel out.
  Overall, our results suggest that, in critic-free RL, pass-rate rewards are insufficient to improve code generation and motivate reward designs that better align optimization with the goal of full correctness.

</details>


### [30] [RouteHijack: Routing-Aware Attack on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02946)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RouteHijack，一种针对MoE大模型的路由感知越狱攻击。通过识别并操控安全关键专家的路由决策，在七种MoE LLM上实现69.3%的平均攻击成功率，超越先前优化攻击3.2倍，并具备跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着混合专家（MoE）架构在大语言模型中被广泛采用，其安全鲁棒性成为关键问题。现有攻击方法存在显著局限：基于提示的越狱依赖启发式搜索且迁移性差，模型干预方法需要内部表示的特权访问，而基于优化的输入攻击受限于MoE不可微的路由机制，难以有效实施。

Method: RouteHijack首先通过响应驱动的专家定位，对比安全拒绝与有害补全情况下的激活，识别安全关键专家和有害专家。随后构建具有路由感知目标的对抗性后缀，该后缀抑制安全专家、促进有害专家，并防止生成过程中的早期拒绝。推理时仅需将优化的后缀附加到恶意提示后。

Result: 在七种MoE LLM上，RouteHijack实现69.3%的平均攻击成功率（ASR），性能超越先前优化攻击3.2倍。该方法在五种同级MoE变体上实现零样本迁移，将平均ASR从27.7%提升至61.2%；在三种基于MoE的视觉语言模型上，将平均ASR从2.47%提升至38.7%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了稀疏专家架构中存在的基础性漏洞，表明仅靠输出层面的安全对齐不足以防御此类攻击，迫切需要开发更深层次的防御机制来保障MoE模型的安全部署。

Abstract: Safety alignment is critical for the responsible deployment of large language models (LLMs). As Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly adopted to scale model capacity, understanding their safety robustness becomes essential. Existing adversarial attacks, however, have notable limitations. Prompt-based jailbreaks rely on heuristic search and transfer poorly, model intervention methods require privileged access to internal representations, and optimization-based input attacks remain output-centric and are fundamentally limited to MoE models due to the non-differentiable routing mechanism.
  In this paper, we present RouteHijack, a routing-aware jailbreak for MoE LLMs. Our key insight is that safety behavior is concentrated in a small subset of experts, creating an opportunity to steer model behavior by influencing routing decisions through input optimization. Building on this observation, RouteHijack first performs response-driven expert localization to identify safety-critical and harmful experts by contrasting activations under safe refusals and harmful completions. It then constructs adversarial suffixes with a routing-aware objective that suppresses safety experts, promotes harmful experts, and prevents early-stage refusal during generation. At inference time, the optimized suffix is appended to a malicious prompt, requiring only input access. Across seven MoE LLMs, RouteHijack achieves a 69.3\% average attack success rate (ASR), outperforming prior optimization-based attack by $3.2\times$. RouteHijack also transfers zero-shot across five sibling MoE variants, raising average ASR from 27.7\% to 61.2\%, and further generalizes to three MoE-based VLMs, increasing average ASR from 2.47\% to 38.7\%. These findings expose a fundamental vulnerability in sparse expert architectures and highlight the need for defenses beyond output-level alignment.

</details>


### [31] [Predicting Euler Characteristics and Constructing Topological Structure Using Machine Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02947)
*Gyunghun Yu,Seong Min Park,Han Gyu Yoon,Tae Jung Moon,Jun Woo Choi,Hee Young Kwon,Changyeon Won*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种从单张几何图像提取欧拉示性数的神经网络方法，通过生成自旋场并计算斯格明子数实现拓扑预测，无需大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受固态物理中磁性结构拓扑性质分析的启发，旨在解决神经网络提取图像拓扑特征时对大规模标注数据集的依赖问题，探索仅用单张图像推断欧拉示性数的可能性。

Method: 构建神经网络从输入图像生成单位向量场（解释为自旋构型），通过计算该自旋构型的斯格明子数预测欧拉示性数。针对自旋构型的非唯一性，引入包含交换相互作用、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用和各向异性的磁性哈密顿量作为物理约束的损失函数。

Result: 网络成功学会构建手性磁性纹理，仅在单张简单几何图像和斯格明子数计算的监督下实现拓扑预测，无需真实手性自旋构型标注。物理约束损失函数有效限制了自由度并改善了自旋构型的质量，在复杂几何形状上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了结合物理先验（磁性哈密顿量）与神经网络可在无大规模数据集条件下实现拓扑性质提取，为数据高效的拓扑特征学习提供了新范式，展示了在复杂几何形状与实际任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel approach to extract topological properties, specifically the Euler characteristic, from input images using neural networks without relying on large pre-existing datasets but with a single geometric image. Inspired by solid-state physics, where topological properties of magnetic structures are derived from spin field analysis, our model generates a unit vector field from an image, interpreted as a spin configuration. The Euler characteristic is then predicted by computing the skyrmion number of this generated spin configuration. Remarkably, the network learns to construct chiral magnetic textures without access to ground-truth chiral spin configurations, relying instead on only a single, simple geometric image and the straightforward skyrmion number computation. Furthermore, spin configurations generated by independently trained networks can be non-unique due to inherent degrees of freedom. To constrain these degrees of freedom and further refine the spin configuration, we incorporate a magnetic Hamiltonian, comprising exchange interaction, Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction, and anisotropy, as an additional, physics-informed loss function. We validate the model's efficacy on complex geometrical shapes and demonstrate its applicability to practical tasks.

</details>

*Yuxin Lu,Qian Qiao,Jiayang Sun,Min Cao,Guibo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的音频驱动说话人头像生成方法存在三个关键局限：阻碍实时推理的因果低效性、与时序连贯条件的不兼容性，以及长序列生成中的渐进漂移问题，这些问题共同限制了其在实时应用中的部署。

Method: AsymK-Talker包含三个关键组件：(1) 核心条件循环生成（KCLG），一种利用运动核心实现时序一致传播的因果分块生成范式；(2) 时序参考编码（TRE），将静态身份参考转换为时间感知的潜在表示以增强音视频同步；(3) 非对称核心蒸馏（AKD），一种师生蒸馏框架，教师模型以真实运动核心为条件进行监督，学生模型学习从生成的核心生成，确保长序列生成的鲁棒性。

Result: AsymK-Talker在视觉保真度和唇形同步指标上均取得了有前景的结果。

Conclusion: AsymK-Talker成功解决了现有扩散模型在实时和长序列说话人头像生成中的关键挑战，实现了高质量的实时生成，为实际应用部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in diffusion models have markedly enhanced the visual fidelity of audio-driven talking head generation. Nevertheless, existing methods are constrained by three critical limitations: causal inefficiency that impedes real-time inference, incompatibility with temporally coherent conditioning, and progressive drift over long-horizon generation, collectively hindering their deployment in real-time applications. To overcome these challenges, we introduce AsymK-Talker, a novel diffusion-distillation method designed for real-time and long-horizon talking head generation. AsymK-Talker comprises three key components: (1) Kernel-Conditioned Loop Generation (KCLG), a causal, chunk-wise generation paradigm that leverages motion kernels to enable temporally consistent propagation; (2) Temporal Reference Encoding (TRE), which converts a static identity reference into a time-aware latent representation to enhance audio-visual synchronization; and (3) Asymmetric Kernel Distillation (AKD), a teacher-student distillation framework wherein the teacher model conditions on ground-truth motion kernels for supervision, while the student learns to generate from generated kernels, thereby ensuring robustness during extended generation sequences. AsymK-Talker achieves promising results on both visual fidelity and lip synchronization metrics.

</details>
