Our main result shows that for every architecture whose input and hidden layers have width at least two, there exists an open set of identifiable parameters. This implies that the functional dimension of every such architecture is exactly the number of parameters minus the number of hidden neurons. We further show that minimal functional representations can still have non-trivial parameter redundancies. Finally, we establish a generic depth hierarchy, whereby for an open set of parameters the realized function cannot be represented generically by any shallower network.

</details>


### [108] [Spatiotemporal Convolutions on EEG signal -- A Representation Learning Perspective on Efficient and Explainable EEG Classification with Convolutional Neural Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03874)
*Laurits Dixen,Stefan Heinrich,Paolo Burelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在EEG信号分类中，相比传统的分离式1D卷积，使用2D时空卷积能显著减少高维任务的训练时间且不损失性能。虽然提取的频谱特征相似，但两种架构学习到的内部表征几何结构存在巨大差异，强调了架构驱动的编码对处理复杂多变量信号的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的浅层CNN模型在处理EEG信号时，通常在空间和时间维度上独立使用1D卷积层并在中间无非线性激活的情况下直接拼接。作者希望探究一种替代方案——二维（2D）时空卷积，并弄清楚尽管在数值上2D卷积等同于两个拼接的1D卷积，但这种方式对模型学习过程有何具体影响。

Method: 作者构建了1D CNN、2D CNN以及CNN+Transformer混合模型，并在低维（3通道）和高维（22通道）的BCI运动想象分类任务上进行了对比测试。通过分析训练效率、频谱特征重要性以及跨模型的表征相似性（representational similarity）来探究2D卷积优于1D卷积的根本原因。

Result: 实验表明，2D卷积在高维任务中能显著减少训练时间，同时保持分类性能。研究未发现模型间频谱特征重要性的差异，但揭示了明显的表征差异模式：1D和2D模型产生了截然不同的表征几何结构。

Conclusion: 作者建议在模型中采用2D卷积层以实现更快的训练和推理速度。同时，研究强调了架构驱动的编码在处理复杂多变量信号时的重要性，这种重要性体现在内部表征的差异上，而不仅仅是性能指标。

Abstract: Classification of EEG signals using shallow Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) is a prevalent and successful approach across a variety of fields. Most of these models use independent one-dimensional (1D) convolutional layers along the spatial and temporal dimensions, which are concatenated without a non-linear activation layer between. In this paper, we investigate an alternative encoding that operates a bi-dimensional (2D) spatiotemporal convolution. While 2D convolutions are numerically identical to two concatenated 1D convolutions along the two dimensions, the impact on learning is still uncertain. We test 1D and 2D CNNs and a CNN+transformer hybrid model in a low-dimensional (3-channel) and a high-dimensional (22-channel) BCI motor imagery classification task. We observe that 2D convolutions significantly reduce training time in high-dimensional tasks while maintaining performance. We investigate the root of this improvement and find no difference in spectral feature importance. However, a clear pattern emerges in representational similarity across models: 1D and 2D models yield vastly different representational geometries. Overall, we suggest an improved model with a 2D convolutional layer for faster training and inference. We also highlight the importance of architecturally-driven encoding when processing complex multivariate signals, as reflected in internal representations rather than purely in performance metrics.

</details>


### [109] [A Few-Step Generative Model on Cumulative Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03623)
*Zhiqi Li,Duowen Chen,Yuchen Sun,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于累积流图的统一少步生成建模框架，能够在保持生成质量的同时实现少步甚至单步生成，且无需增加模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受物理输运和动力学中的流图技术启发，作者旨在解决现有生成模型在长程概率空间输运中的效率问题。通过建立局部瞬时更新与有限时间输运之间的联系，使生成模型能够推理全局状态转换，从而实现快速且高质量的生成。

Method: 提出累积流图抽象概念，建立累积输运与累积参数化的统一框架。该方法可广泛适用于现有的扩散模型和流模型，仅需对时间嵌入和训练目标进行微小修改，不增加模型容量，即可支持少步乃至单步生成。

Result: 在图像生成、几何分布建模、联合预测和SDF生成等多种任务上验证了方法的有效性，在显著降低推理成本的同时保持了合成质量。

Conclusion: 该框架为扩散和流模型提供了一种统一的加速生成方案，证明了累积流视角在连接局部更新与全局输运方面的有效性，为少步高质量生成提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We propose a unified, few-step generative modeling framework based on \emph{cumulative flow maps} for long-range transport in probability space, inspired by flow-map techniques for physical transport and dynamics. At its core is a cumulative-flow abstraction that connects local, instantaneous updates with finite-time transport, enabling generative models to reason about global state transitions. This perspective yields a unified few-step framework built on cumulative transport and \revise{cumulative} parameterization that applies broadly to existing diffusion- and flow-based models without being tied to a specific prediction \revise{instantiation}. Our formulation supports few-step and even one-step generation while preserving synthesis quality, requiring only minimal changes to time embeddings and training objectives, and no increase in model capacity. We demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse tasks, including image generation, geometric distribution modeling, joint prediction, and SDF generation, with reduced inference cost.

</details>


### [110] [TabSurv: Adapting Modern Tabular Neural Networks to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03944)
*Stanislav Kirpichenko,Andrei Konstantinov,Lev Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TabSurv方法，将现代表格神经网络架构适配到生存分析任务中，在10个数据集上表现优于现有基线方法，其中基于Weibull分布参数化的深度集成模型效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习生存分析方法通常高度任务特定，限制了其他领域新方法的迁移，且可能引入影响性能的约束。因此需要一种能够有效适配现代表格神经网络架构到生存分析的通用方法。

Method: 提出TabSurv方法，使用Weibull分布或非参数生存预测适配现代表格架构；设计支持删失数据的新型直方图损失函数SurvHL；实现深度集成MLP模型，采用并行训练方式，在平均前优化生存分布参数以促进集成组件预测的多样性。

Result: 在10个多样化真实生存数据集上进行的全面实证评估显示，TabSurv在平均表现上持续优于RSF、DeepSurv、DeepHit、SurvTRACE等经典和深度学习基线方法；采用Weibull参数化的深度集成模型在C-index指标上达到最高平均排名。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了如何适配和训练现代表格神经网络以解决生存分析问题，提供了一种强大且可靠的方法，并公开了TabSurv的实现代码。

Abstract: Survival analysis on tabular data is a well-studied problem. However, existing deep learning methods are often highly task-specific, which can limit the transfer of new approaches from other domains and introduce constraints that may affect performance. We propose TabSurv, an approach that adapts modern tabular architectures to survival analysis using either the Weibull distribution or non-parametric survival prediction. TabSurv optimizes SurvHL, a novel histogram loss function supporting censored data. In addition to a baseline feed-forward network, we implement deep ensembles of MLPs for survival analysis within TabSurv. In contrast to prior work, the ensemble components are trained in parallel, optimizing survival distribution parameters before averaging, which promotes diversity across ensemble component predictions. We perform a comprehensive empirical evaluation of different proposed architectures on 10 diverse real-world survival datasets. Our results show that TabSurv consistently outperforms on average established classical and deep learning baselines, such as RSF, DeepSurv, DeepHit, SurvTRACE. Notably, deep ensembles with Weibull parametrization instead of non-parametric models achieve the highest average rank by C-index. Overall, our study clarifies how modern tabular neural networks can be adapted and trained to tackle survival analysis problems, offering a strong and reliable approach. The TabSurv implementation is publicly available.

</details>


### [111] [Information Plane Analysis of Binary Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03636)
*Maximilian Nothnagel,Bernhard C. Geiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在二元神经网络（BNN）中进行了信息平面（IP）分析，确定了互信息估计可靠的样本量与表示维度范围，发现后期压缩阶段常见，但压缩表示与泛化性能之间不存在一致的正相关关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息平面分析在估计高维、确定性表示的互信息时存在统计有效性问题，因此作者转向激活值离散且互信息有限的二元神经网络，以研究信息平面分析的可靠性及其对泛化性能的指示作用。

Method: 在二元神经网络（BNNs）上执行信息平面分析，刻画插件熵估计量的有限样本行为，确立互信息估计可靠的样本量N与表示维度D的取值范围，并在该范围内训练375个BNNs，系统研究后期压缩阶段的存在性及压缩表示与泛化性能的关系。

Result: 确定了互信息估计可靠的条件，超出该范围时经验互信息估计会饱和至log₂N，使信息平面轨迹失去信息量；在可靠范围内，后期压缩阶段在BNNs中频繁出现，但压缩的潜在表示与改进的泛化性能并不一致相关，两者关系高度依赖于任务、架构和正则化。

Conclusion: 信息平面分析在二元神经网络中需在特定的样本量与维度范围内才具有统计有效性；后期压缩现象虽常见，但不能作为泛化性能提升的可靠指标，压缩与泛化的关系因任务、架构和正则化策略而异。

Abstract: Information plane (IP) analysis has been suggested to study the training dynamics of deep neural networks through mutual information (MI) between inputs, representations, and targets. However, its statistical validity is often compromised by the difficulty of estimating MI from samples of high-dimensional, deterministic representations.
  In this work, we perform IP analyses on binary neural networks (BNNs) where activations are discrete and MI is finite. We characterise the finite-sample behaviour of the plug-in entropy estimator and identify regimes for sample size $N$ and representation dimensionality $D$ under which MI estimates are reliable. Outside these regimes, we show that empirical MI estimates saturate to $\log_2 N$, rendering IP trajectories uninformative.
  Restricting attention to the reliable regime, we train 375 BNNs to investigate the existence of late-stage compression phases and the relationship between compressed representations and generalisation performance. Our results show that while late-stage compression is frequently observed, compressed latent representations do not consistently correlate with improved generalization performance. Instead, the relationship between compression and generalisation is highly dependent on task, architecture, and regularisation.

</details>


### [112] [Flow Sampling: Learning to Sample from Unnormalized Densities via Denoising Conditional Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03984)
*Aaron Havens,Brian Karrer,Neta Shaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型和流匹配的无数据采样框架Flow Sampling，用于从未归一化密度函数（能量函数）中高效采样，并自然扩展至黎曼流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从未归一化密度函数采样是生成建模的重要问题，但能量函数评估通常计算代价高昂。现有方法在训练过程中需要大量能量函数评估，缺乏高效且可扩展的采样方法，尤其在非欧几里得几何空间中。

Method: 提出Flow Sampling框架，基于扩散模型和流匹配。训练目标以噪声样本为条件，回归到由能量函数构造的去噪扩散漂移（与标准扩散模型相反）。利用插值过程最小化训练期间的能量函数评估次数。方法自然扩展至黎曼流形，推导出常曲率流形（包括超球面和双曲空间）上条件漂移的闭式公式。

Result: 在合成能量基准、小肽、大规模摊销分子构象生成以及球面分布等多个任务上验证了方法的有效性，展示了强劲的经验性能。

Conclusion: Flow Sampling为无数据设置下的未归一化密度采样提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，并成功将扩散采样扩展到黎曼流形几何空间。

Abstract: Sampling from unnormalized densities is analogous to the generative modeling problem, but the target distribution is defined by a known energy function instead of data samples. Because evaluating the energy function is often costly, a primary challenge is to learn an efficient sampler. We introduce Flow Sampling, a framework built on diffusion models and flow matching for the data-free setting. Our training objective is conditioned on a noise sample and regresses onto a denoising diffusion drift constructed from the energy function. In contrast, diffusion models' objective is conditioned on a data sample and regresses onto a noising diffusion drift. We utilize the interpolant process to minimize the number of energy function evaluations during training, resulting in an efficient and scalable method for sampling unnormalized densities. Furthermore, our formulation naturally extends to Riemannian manifolds, enabling diffusion-based sampling in geometries beyond Euclidean space. We derive a closed-form formula for the conditional drift on constant curvature manifolds, including hyperspheres and hyperbolic spaces. We evaluate Flow Sampling on synthetic energy benchmarks, small peptides, large-scale amortized molecular conformer generation, and distributions supported on the sphere, demonstrating strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [113] [Uni-OPD: Unifying On-Policy Distillation with a Dual-Perspective Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03677)
*Wenjin Hou,Shangpin Peng,Weinong Wang,Zheng Ruan,Yue Zhang,Zhenglin Zhou,Mingqi Gao,Yifei Chen,Kaiqi Wang,Hongming Yang,Chengquan Zhang,Zhuotao Tian,Han Hu,Yi Yang,Fei Wu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了策略蒸馏（OPD）中信息状态探索不足和教师监督不可靠两个瓶颈，提出统一框架Uni-OPD，通过学生端的数据平衡策略和教师端的结果引导边际校准机制，实现了跨LLM和MLLM的广泛性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管策略蒸馏（OPD）在整合专家模型能力方面取得了实证成功，但其在何种条件下能产生可靠改进尚不清楚。现有方法受限于信息状态探索不足以及对学生生成轨迹的教师监督不可靠这两个基本瓶颈。

Method: 提出Uni-OPD统一框架，采用双视角优化策略：从学生视角，采用两种数据平衡策略促进对信息量大的学生生成状态的探索；从教师视角，提出结果引导的边际校准机制，恢复正确与错误轨迹之间的顺序一致性，确保聚合的token级指导与结果奖励保持顺序一致。

Result: 在5个领域和16个基准测试上进行了广泛实验，涵盖LLM和MLLM的单教师/多教师蒸馏、强到弱蒸馏以及跨模态蒸馏等多种设置，验证了Uni-OPD的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: Uni-OPD成功解决了OPD中的关键瓶颈，在多种蒸馏场景下表现出良好的有效性和通用性，为可靠的策略蒸馏提供了实践见解和有效方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has recently emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for consolidating the capabilities of specialized expert models into a single student model. Despite its empirical success, the conditions under which OPD yields reliable improvement remain poorly understood. In this work, we identify two fundamental bottlenecks that limit effective OPD: insufficient exploration of informative states and unreliable teacher supervision for student rollouts. Building on this insight, we propose Uni-OPD, a unified OPD framework that generalizes across Large Language Models (LLMs) and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), centered on a dual-perspective optimization strategy. Specifically, from the student's perspective, we adopt two data balancing strategies to promote exploration of informative student-generated states during training. From the teacher's perspective, we show that reliable supervision hinges on whether aggregated token-level guidance remains order-consistent with the outcome reward. To this end, we develop an outcome-guided margin calibration mechanism to restore order consistency between correct and incorrect trajectories. We conduct extensive experiments on 5 domains and 16 benchmarks covering diverse settings, including single-teacher and multi-teacher distillation across LLMs and MLLMs, strong-to-weak distillation, and cross-modal distillation. Our results verify the effectiveness and versatility of Uni-OPD and provide practical insights into reliable OPD.

</details>


### [114] [From Code to Prediction: Fine-Tuning LLMs for Neural Network Performance Classification in NNGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03686)
*Mahmoud Hanouneh,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在无需训练的情况下，仅凭神经网络架构代码预测其跨数据集性能的能力。实验表明，经过微调的DeepSeek-Coder-7B模型在仅使用代码的提示配置下达到了80%的预测准确率，证明了LLM具备从源代码中推理架构适用性的能力，且源代码比数据集元数据包含更丰富的判别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的AutoML方法主要关注生成输出并通过训练生成物进行评估，但LLM是否能够学习推理神经网络在不同数据集上的性能表现尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在探究LLM是否能够直接从神经网络架构代码中学习并预测其跨数据集的适用性。

Method: 作者在NNGPT框架中集成了一项分类任务，利用LEMUR数据集提供的标准化PyTorch实现和可复现的性能指标。任务设定为让微调后的LLM预测给定神经网络架构在两个图像分类数据集中的哪一个上能获得更高准确率。研究评估了三种难度递增的提示配置：(1) 归一化准确率基线；(2) 用数据集属性替代准确率的元数据丰富提示；(3) 仅包含架构源代码和数据集名称的纯代码提示。使用DeepSeek-Coder-7B-Instruct模型通过LoRA进行微调。

Result: 在纯代码提示配置下，模型在15个训练周期后达到80%的峰值准确率，而元数据提示配置峰值仅为70%。针对每个数据集的分析显示了互补优势：元数据方法在具有独特属性的数据集上表现优异，而在特征重叠的数据集上性能下降；纯代码提示则表现更为均衡。与DeepSeek-Coder-1.3B的对比实验证实了模型容量对此类架构推理任务有显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究证实LLM可以通过微调来从神经网络代码预测跨数据集的适用性，表明架构源代码包含比数据集元数据更丰富的判别信号。这一发现为AutoML领域提供了新思路，展示了LLM在无需实际训练的情况下进行架构性能推理的潜力。

Abstract: Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) frameworks increasingly leverage Large Language Models (LLMs) for tasks such as hyperparameter optimization and neural architecture code generation. However, current LLM-based approaches focus on generative outputs and evaluate them by training the produced artifacts. Whether LLMs can learn to reason about neural network performance across datasets remains underexplored. We present a classification task integrated into the NNGPT framework, in which a fine-tuned LLM predicts which of two image classification datasets a given neural network architecture achieves higher accuracy on. The task is built on the LEMUR dataset, which provides standardized PyTorch implementations with reproducible performance metrics. Three prompt configurations of increasing difficulty are evaluated: a normalized-accuracy baseline (trivially reaching 100%), a metadata-enriched prompt replacing accuracies with dataset properties, and a code-only prompt presenting only architecture source code and dataset names. Using DeepSeek-Coder-7B-Instruct fine-tuned with LoRA, the code-only prompt reaches 80% peak accuracy over 15 epochs, while the metadata prompt peaks at 70%. Perdataset analysis reveals complementary strengths: metadata excels for datasets with distinctive properties (CelebAGender at 90.9%) but degrades for overlapping characteristics, whereas the code-only prompt shows more balanced performance. A comparison with DeepSeek-Coder1.3B confirms that model capacity affects this form of architectural reasoning. The results establish that LLMs can be fine-tuned to predict cross-dataset suitability from neural network code, suggesting that architecture source code contains richer discriminative signal than dataset metadata alone.

</details>


### [115] [Distribution-Free Pretraining of Classification Losses via Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03722)
*Meng Xiang,Yan Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出演化动态损失(EDL)框架，通过无语义的排序一致性目标和混沌突变策略，在概率空间学习可迁移的分类损失函数，无需真实样本即可预训练，在CIFAR-10上达到与交叉熵相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统损失函数（如交叉熵）依赖人工设计且可能不是最优的，而现有损失学习方法通常需要访问真实样本。本文旨在探索一种无需真实样本即可学习可迁移损失函数的方法，以提升分类性能和泛化能力。

Method: EDL将损失函数参数化为轻量级网络，使用无限的合成预测-标签对进行训练。核心方法包括：(1)语义无关的排序一致性目标，对更错误的预测分配更大惩罚；(2)演化策略优化损失函数空间；(3)引入混沌突变机制，在噪声适应度评估下改善探索能力。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上使用ResNet骨干网络的实验表明：EDL可作为交叉熵的直接替代，达到相当或更优的准确率；消融实验验证混沌突变相比标准高斯突变具有更快的收敛速度和更好的合成预训练指标。

Conclusion: EDL框架成功实现了在概率空间学习可迁移的分类损失函数，无需真实样本即可完成损失预训练，演化策略结合混沌突变有效提升了损失函数的探索和优化效率，为自动损失函数学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose Evolutionary Dynamic Loss (EDL), a framework that learns a transferable classification loss in the probability space using unlimited synthetic prediction-label pairs, without accessing real samples during the main loss pretraining stage. EDL parameterizes the loss as a lightweight network and is trained with a semantics-free ranking-consistency objective that assigns larger penalties for more erroneous predictions. To robustly explore the space of loss functions, we optimize EDL via an evolutionary strategy and introduce chaotic mutation to improve exploration under noisy fitness evaluations. Experiments on CIFAR-10 with ResNet backbones show that EDL can serve as a drop-in replacement for cross-entropy and achieves competitive or improved accuracy, while ablation studies confirm that chaotic mutation yields faster convergence and better synthetic pretraining metrics than standard Gaussian mutation.

</details>


### [116] [GEM-FI: Gated Evidential Mixtures with Fisher Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03750)
*Marco Mustafa Mohammed,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEM（Gated Evidential Mixtures）模型系列，通过引入门控机制和混合头结构改进证据深度学习（EDL），在保持单次推断的前提下显著提升了校准性能、分布内/分布外（ID/OOD）分离能力以及认知不确定性的多模态表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据深度学习（EDL）虽然能实现单次推断的不确定性估计，但存在过度自信、校准性能差以及难以表示多模态认知不确定性等问题。作者旨在解决这些局限性，同时保持EDL单次推断的优势。

Method: 提出三个递进的模型变体：(1) GEM-CORE学习特征级能量信号，将其映射为有界门控，在支持度低时平滑抑制证据；(2) GEM-MIX添加轻量级证据头混合结构，通过学习的路由权重捕获认知不确定性多模态性，保持单次推断；(3) GEM-FI引入Fisher信息正则化器稳定混合分配，减少头坍塌并产生更平滑的边界不确定性。

Result: 在图像分类和OOD检测基准上，GEM显著提升校准性能和ID/OOD分离能力。在CIFAR-10上，GEM-FI相比DAEDL将准确率从91.11%提升至93.75%（+2.64 pp），Brier分数×100从14.27降至6.81（-7.46），误分类检测AUPR从99.08%提升至99.94%（+0.86%）。在认知OOD检测任务中，GEM-FI在CIFAR-10→SVHN上达到AUPR/AUROC 92.59/95.09，在CIFAR-10→CIFAR-100上达到90.20/89.06，均显著优于DAEDL。

Conclusion: GEM系列模型成功解决了传统EDL方法的关键局限性，在保持单次推断效率的同时，通过门控机制和混合结构实现了更好的校准性能、更准确的OOD检测以及更有效的认知不确定性多模态表示，为安全关键的深度学习应用提供了更可靠的不确定性估计框架。

Abstract: Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) enables single-pass uncertainty estimation by predicting Dirichlet evidence, but it can remain overconfident and poorly calibrated, and it often fails to represent multi-modal epistemic uncertainty. We introduce Gated Evidential Mixtures (GEM), a family of models that learns an in-model energy signal and uses it to gate evidential outputs end-to-end in a distance-informed manner. GEM-CORE learns a feature-level energy and maps it to a bounded gate that smoothly suppresses evidence when support is low. To capture epistemic multi-modality without multi-pass ensembling, GEM-MIX adds a lightweight mixture of evidential heads with learned routing weights while preserving single-pass inference. Finally, GEM-FI stabilizes mixture allocations via a Fisher-informed regularizer, reducing head collapse and producing smoother boundary uncertainty. Across image classification and OOD detection benchmarks, GEM improves calibration and ID/OOD separation with single-pass inference. On CIFAR-10, GEM-FI vs. DAEDL improves accuracy from 91.11 to 93.75 (+2.64 pp), reduces Brier x100 from 14.27 to 6.81 (-7.46), and also improves misclassification-detection AUPR from 99.08 to 99.94 (+0.86). For epistemic OOD detection, GEM-FI achieves AUPR/AUROC of 92.59/95.09 on CIFAR-10 to SVHN and 90.20/89.06 on CIFAR-10 to CIFAR-100, compared with 85.54/89.30 and 88.19/86.10 for DAEDL.

</details>


### [117] [Vanishing L2 regularization for the softmax Multi Armed Bandit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03752)
*Stefana-Lucia Anita,Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了L2正则化softmax策略梯度算法，证明了正则化参数趋近于零时的理论收敛性，并验证了该方案在标准基准测试中具有数值优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂老虎机（MAB）算法是强化学习的基石，softmax映射是其核心实现方式并被广泛应用于REINFORCE等下游算法。已有研究利用凸性分析L2正则化softmax策略梯度，但未能解决正则化参数趋于零时的理论收敛框架问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 针对L2正则化softmax策略梯度算法（在平均奖励中减去二次项），建立理论分析框架，研究正则化参数消失（趋近于零）极限情况下的收敛性质。

Result: 从理论上证明了该算法在正则化参数消失情况下的收敛性结果；通过在标准基准测试上进行实证实验，确认了该机制使L2正则化具有数值优势。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了L2正则化softmax策略梯度在正则化参数趋于零时的理论收敛框架，弥合了理论分析与数值实践之间的差距，证明了该正则化机制的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Multi Armed Bandit (MAB) algorithms are a cornerstone of reinforcement learning and have been studied both theoretically and numerically. One of the most commonly used implementation uses a softmax mapping to prescribe the optimal policy and served as the foundation for downstream algorithms, including REINFORCE. Distinct from vanilla approaches, we consider here the L2 regularized softmax policy gradient where a quadratic term is subtracted from the mean reward. Previous studies exploiting convexity failed to identify a suitable theoretical framework to analyze its convergence when the regularization parameter vanishes. We prove here theoretical convergence results and confirm empirically that this regime makes the L2 regularization numerically advantageous on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [118] [Nora: Normalized Orthogonal Row Alignment for Scalable Matrix Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03769)
*Jinghui Yuan,Jiaxuan Zou,Shuo Wang,Yong Liu,Feiping Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nora优化器，通过行级动量投影稳定权重范数和角速度，并利用Transformer海森矩阵的块对角优势近似结构化预条件，以O(mn)复杂度统一实现了效率、稳定性和速度三大目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的矩阵优化器在训练大语言模型时难以同时满足效率、稳定性和速度三个核心需求。Muon方法计算成本过高，RMNP方法存在径向抖动影响稳定性，亟需一种能够统一满足这三方面要求的优化器。

Method: Nora通过以下技术实现目标：（1）行级动量投影到权重的正交补空间，显式稳定权重范数和角速度；（2）利用Transformer海森矩阵的块对角优势，有效近似结构化预条件；（3）保持O(mn)的最优计算复杂度；（4）仅需两行代码的简洁实现。

Result: 初步实验验证了Nora作为大规模训练优化器的高效性和潜力。理论方面证明了Nora是可扩展优化器并建立了相应的缩放定理。

Conclusion: Nora成功统一了效率、稳定性和速度三大优化器设计需求，为大语言模型训练提供了一个高效、稳定且计算开销低的优化方案。

Abstract: Matrix-based optimizers have demonstrated immense potential in training Large Language Models (LLMs), however, designing an ideal optimizer remains a formidable challenge. A superior optimizer must satisfy three core desiderata: efficiency, achieving Muon-like preconditioning to accelerate optimization; stability, strictly adhering to the scale-invariance inherent in neural networks; and speed, minimizing computational overhead. While existing methods address these aspects to varying degrees, they often fail to unify them, either incurring prohibitive computational costs like Muon, or allowing radial jitters that compromise stability like RMNP. To bridge this gap, we propose Nora, an optimizer that rigorously satisfies all three requirements. Nora achieves training stability by explicitly stabilizing weight norms and angular velocities through row-wise momentum projection onto the orthogonal complement of the weights. Simultaneously, by leveraging the block-diagonal dominance of the Transformer Hessian, Nora effectively approximates structured preconditioning while maintaining an optimal computational complexity of $\mathcal{O}(mn)$. Furthermore, we prove that Nora is a scalable optimizer and establish its corresponding scaling theorems. With a streamlined implementation requiring only two lines of code, our preliminary experiments validate Nora as an efficient and highly promising optimizer for large-scale training.

</details>


### [119] [Task Vector Geometry Underlies Dual Modes of Task Inference in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03780)
*Hao Yan,Haolin Yang,Yiqiao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控合成环境中研究了Transformer中的任务向量几何结构，发现模型内部同时存在两种推理模式：分布内行为由贝叶斯任务检索控制，通过任务向量的凸组合实现；分布外行为则通过外推式任务学习产生，其表示位于与任务向量子空间几乎正交的子空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性研究虽然识别出了控制模型行为的任务向量，但缺乏严谨的理论基础来连接内部表示与外部模型行为，特别是无法解释训练分布如何塑造任务向量几何结构，以及何种几何结构能够实现分布外泛化。

Method: 在一个受控的合成环境中，从头开始训练小型Transformer模型，基于潜在任务序列分布进行训练，从而实现对任务向量几何结构的原理性数学表征和分析。

Result: 研究发现两种推理模式可以在单一模型中共存：分布内行为由贝叶斯任务检索控制，通过学习到的任务向量的凸组合在内部实现；分布外行为通过外推式任务学习产生，其表示占据一个与任务向量子空间几乎正交的子空间。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明任务向量几何结构、训练分布和泛化行为之间存在密切关系，为理解Transformer如何从上下文中推断潜在任务提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Transformers are effective at inferring the latent task from context via two inference modes: recognizing a task seen during training, and adapting to a novel one. Recent interpretability studies have identified from middle-layer representations task-specific directions, or task vectors, that steer model behavior. However, a lack of rigorous foundations hinders connecting internal representations to external model behavior: existing work fails to explain how task-vector geometry is shaped by the training distribution, and what geometry enables out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. In this paper, we study these questions in a controlled synthetic setting by training small transformers from scratch on latent-task sequence distributions, which allows a principled mathematical characterization. We show that two inference modes can coexist within a single model. In-distribution behavior is governed by Bayesian task retrieval, implemented internally through convex combinations of learned task vectors. OOD behavior, by contrast, arises through extrapolative task learning, whose representations occupy a subspace nearly orthogonal to the task-vector subspace. Taken together, our results suggest that task-vector geometry, training distributions, and generalization behaviors are closely related.

</details>


### [120] [Graph Convolutional Support Vector Regression for Robust Spatiotemporal Forecasting of Urban Air Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03795)
*Nourin Jahan,Madhurima Panja,Muhammed Navas T,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了图卷积支持向量回归（GCSVR）框架，通过结合图卷积网络和支持向量回归，有效解决了城市空气质量预测中的空间依赖、非线性时间动态及异常值敏感性问题，在印度两大城市的实验中表现出优越且稳定的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量预测面临巨大挑战，因为污染物浓度具有非线性、非平稳性、时空依赖性等特点，且常受到交通拥堵、工业排放和季节性气象变化等异常观测值的影响，现有方法难以同时妥善处理这些复杂特性。

Method: 提出图卷积支持向量回归（GCSVR）框架：利用图卷积学习捕捉监测站之间的空间依赖关系，结合支持向量回归对非线性时间动态进行建模，并降低模型对异常观测值的敏感性。此外，集成共形预测生成校准后的预测区间以量化不确定性。

Result: 在德里（37个站点）和孟买（18个站点）的空气质量数据上进行了评估。结果表明，GCSVR在多个预测时间跨度上持续提高了预测准确性，在季节变化和易出现异常值的污染事件中保持了稳定的性能。统计检验进一步证实了该方法在两个城市中的可靠性。

Conclusion: GCSVR框架为城市空气污染提供了鲁棒的时空预测能力，结合共形预测生成的预测区间增强了其在不确定性感知的空气质量监测和公共卫生决策中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Urban air quality forecasting is challenging because pollutant concentrations are nonlinear, nonstationary, spatiotemporally dependent, and often affected by anomalous observations caused by traffic congestion, industrial emissions, and seasonal meteorological variability. This study proposes a Graph Convolutional Support Vector Regression (GCSVR) framework for robust spatiotemporal forecasting of urban air pollution. The model combines graph convolutional learning to capture inter-station spatial dependence with support vector regression to model nonlinear temporal dynamics while reducing sensitivity to outlier observations. The proposed framework is evaluated using air quality records from 37 monitoring stations in Delhi and 18 stations in Mumbai, representing inland and coastal metropolitan environments in India. Forecasting performance is assessed across multiple horizons and compared with established temporal and spatiotemporal benchmarks. The results show that GCSVR consistently improves predictive accuracy and maintains stable performance across seasons and outlier-prone pollution episodes. Statistical test further confirms the reliability of the proposed approach across the two cities. Finally, conformal prediction is integrated with GCSVR to generate calibrated prediction intervals, enhancing its practical value for uncertainty-aware air quality monitoring and public health decision-making.

</details>


### [121] [Realizable Bayes-Consistency for General Metric Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03823)
*Dan Tsir Cohen,Steve Hanneke,Aryeh Kontorovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了可实现设定下一般度量损失学习的强通用贝叶斯一致性问题，提出了无限非减(γ_k)-Littlestone树概念，给出了假设类存在分布无关学习规则的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的一致性刻画仅限于0-1分类和实值回归任务。本文旨在将理论扩展到更一般的度量损失场景，解决Cohen等人(2022)提出的开放问题：在可实现设定下，一般假设类在什么条件下存在强通用贝叶斯一致的学习规则。

Method: 引入"无限非减(γ_k)-Littlestone树"这一组合结构概念（其中γ_k→∞），这是对Bousquet等人(2021)提出的Littlestone树在度量损失设定下的推广。通过该组合障碍结构来刻画假设类的可学习性。

Result: 获得了假设类H存在分布无关学习规则的充分必要条件：该学习规则的风险几乎必然收敛到类内最优风险（在可实现设定下为零），且对所有可实现的数据生成分布成立。这是一个精确的刻画。

Conclusion: 成功将0-1分类和实值回归的经典可学习性刻画推广到一般度量损失场景，填补了理论空白，并通过新的组合结构（无限非减γ_k-Littlestone树）提供了完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: We study strong universal Bayes-consistency in the realizable setting for learning with general metric losses, extending classical characterizations beyond $0$-$1$ classification \citep{bousquet_theory_2021, hanneke2021universalbayesconsistencymetric} and real-valued regression \citep{attias_universal_2024}. Given an instance space $(\mathcal X,ρ)$, a label space $(\mathcal Y,\ell)$ with possibly unbounded loss, and a hypothesis class $\mathcal H \subseteq \mathcal Y^{\mathcal X}$, we resolve the realizable case of an open problem presented in \citet{pmlr-v178-cohen22a}. Specifically, we find the necessary and sufficient conditions on the hypothesis class $\mathcal H$ under which there exists a distribution-free learning rule whose risk converges almost surely to the best-in-class risk (which is zero) for every realizable data-generating distribution. Our main contribution is this sharp characterization in terms of a combinatorial obstruction: Similarly to \citet{attias2024optimallearnersrealizableregression}, we introduce the notion of an infinite non-decreasing $(γ_k)$-Littlestone tree, where $γ_k \to \infty$. This extends the Littlestone tree structure used in \citet{bousquet_theory_2021} to the metric loss setting.

</details>


### [122] [A Domain Incremental Continual Learning Benchmark for ICU Time Series Model Transportability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03832)
*Ryan King,Conrad Krueger,Ethan Veselka,Tianbao Yang,Bobak J. Mortazavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对医院间机器学习模型迁移困难的问题，提出了一个跨区域迁移基准，并采用域增量学习方法解决患者结局预测中的数据分布差异问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型医院缺乏资源训练机器学习模型，而从大型医院迁移的模型在单一医院数据上训练，泛化能力存疑。研究发现美国不同区域间测量分布和频率存在显著差异，因此需要评估模型跨区域迁移的能力。

Method: 提出了一个基准测试，用于评估机器学习模型从源域迁移到全国不同区域的能力，将模型跨区域迁移构建为域增量学习问题。评估了两种主流域增量学习方法：数据回放（存储先前数据源的样本用于当前源的微调）和弹性权重巩固（EWC，一种模型参数正则化方法）。

Result: 论文建立了一个跨区域模型迁移基准，验证了不同区域间数据分布存在显著差异，并对比评估了数据回放和弹性权重巩固两种域增量学习方法在患者结局预测任务中的表现。

Conclusion: 该研究为医疗机器学习模型的跨区域迁移提供了系统的评估框架，证明了域增量学习方法是解决模型迁移中数据分布变化问题的有效途径，有助于促进医疗AI模型在不同医院间的推广应用。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning has made significant progress in clinical outcome prediction, demonstrating increasingly accurate results. However, the substantial resources required for hospitals to train these models, such as data collection, labeling, and computational power, limit the feasibility for smaller hospitals to develop their own models. An alternative approach involves transferring a machine learning model trained by a large hospital to smaller hospitals, allowing them to fine-tune the model on their specific patient data.
  However, these models are often trained and validated on data from a single hospital, raising concerns about their generalizability to new data. Our research shows that there are notable differences in measurement distributions and frequencies across various regions in the United States. To address this, we propose a benchmark that tests a machine learning model's ability to transfer from a source domain to different regions across the country. This benchmark assesses a model's capacity to learn meaningful information about each new domain while retaining key features from the original domain.
  Using this benchmark, we frame the transfer of a machine learning model from one region to another as a domain incremental learning problem. While the task of patient outcome prediction remains the same, the input data distribution varies, necessitating a model that can effectively manage these shifts. We evaluate two popular domain incremental learning methods: data replay, which stores examples from previous data sources for fine-tuning on the current source, and Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), a model parameter regularization method that maintains features important for both data sources.

</details>


### [123] [Complex Equation Learner: Rational Symbolic Regression with Gradient Descent in Complex Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03841)
*Sergei Garmaev,Maurice Gauché,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种复权重扩展的方程学习器，通过允许优化路径绕过实轴简并点，解决了现有梯度方法在处理除法、对数和平方根等奇异算子时的优化病态问题，实现了对包含奇异性的表达式的稳定发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于梯度的符号回归方法在处理引入奇异性或定义域约束的算子（如除法、对数、平方根）时存在优化失败问题。现有的方程学习器模型通常通过避免使用这些算子或施加约束（如限制分母以避免极点）来规避问题，这限制了假设类的范围和表达能力。

Method: 提出了方程学习器的复权重扩展方法。该方法通过将权重扩展到复数域，允许优化轨迹绕过实轴上的简并点，从而缓解实值优化中的病态问题。这使得模型可以在无约束的情况下使用包括对数和平方根在内的奇异算子。

Result: 在符号回归基准测试上验证了该方法的有效性，证明其能够稳定收敛，即使在目标表达式具有实域极点时也能正常工作。此外，成功从实验频率响应数据中恢复了奇异行为，展示了方法在实际应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: 复权重扩展方法有效解决了梯度符号回归中的奇异性优化问题，消除了对算子的人为限制，拓宽了可发现的方程类别，为包含奇异性的物理系统的符号回归提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to discover interpretable equations from data, yet modern gradient-based methods fail for operators that introduce singularities or domain constraints, including division, logarithms, and square roots. As a result, Equation Learner-type models typically avoid these operators or impose restrictions, e.g. constraining denominators to prevent poles, which narrows the hypothesis class. We propose a complex weight extension of the Equation Learner that mitigates real-valued optimization pathologies by allowing optimization trajectories to bypass real-axis degeneracies. The proposed approach converges stably even when the target expression has real-domain poles, and it enables unconstrained use of operations such as logarithm and square root. We Validate the method on symbolic regression benchmarks and show it can recover singular behavior from experimental frequency response data.

</details>


### [124] [Memory-Efficient Continual Learning with CLIP Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03866)
*Ryan King,Gang Li,Bobak Mortazavi,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种内存高效的分布鲁棒方法，通过在训练期间动态调整每类损失权重，解决了CLIP模型在小样本记忆缓冲区下的持续学习难题，在极低内存占用下实现了快速适应新任务并减少灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型虽然在图文对齐方面表现出色，但在适应新数据时存在灾难性遗忘问题。现有方法通常依赖新旧数据的联合微调，但CLIP的对比损失在记忆缓冲区较小的情况下表现不佳，导致旧任务性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种内存高效且分布鲁棒的训练策略。该方法在训练过程中动态地对每个类别的损失进行重新加权，以改善在小记忆缓冲区限制下的对比学习效果。

Result: 在类增量学习设置（CIFAR-100、ImageNet1K）和域增量学习设置（DomainNet）上的实验表明，该方法能快速适应CLIP模型，且即使内存占用极少，也能最小化灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 该方法为CLIP模型的持续学习提供了一种高效的解决方案，成功解决了小记忆缓冲区下的性能退化问题，在多种增量学习场景下均表现出优越的性能与内存效率。

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models excel at understanding image-text relationships but struggle with adapting to new data without forgetting prior knowledge. To address this, models are typically fine-tuned using both new task data and a memory buffer of past tasks. However, CLIP's contrastive loss suffers when the memory buffer is small, leading to performance degradation on previous tasks. We propose a memory-efficient, distributionally robust method that dynamically reweights losses per class during training. Our approach, tested on class incremental settings (CIFAR-100, ImageNet1K) and a domain incremental setting (DomainNet) adapts CLIP models quickly while minimizing catastrophic forgetting, even with minimal memory usage.

</details>


### [125] [On Adaptivity in Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03869)
*Hassan Dbouk,Nidham Gazagnadou,Matthias Reisser,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有自适应零阶优化方法（如ZO-Adam）在LLM微调中存在记忆开销大且无收敛优势的问题，并提出了一种记忆高效的自适应零阶优化器MEAZO，仅需跟踪单个标量即可实现全局步长自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存受限的场景下对大语言模型进行微调，零阶优化是一种重要方法。然而，先前研究认为自适应零阶方法（如ZO-Adam）优于ZO-SGD，但其内存开销显著。本文旨在探究自适应ZO方法的有效性，并解决其内存效率问题。

Method: 首先通过理论分析揭示在高维空间中，零阶梯度缺乏坐标层面的异质性，这使得自适应机制在内存使用上效率低下。基于此洞察，提出MEAZO优化器，它只跟踪一个标量来进行全局步长自适应，而非为每个参数维度维护独立的自适应状态，从而大幅降低内存开销。

Result: 在多个LLM系列和任务上的实验表明，MEAZO在匹配ZO-Adam性能的同时，内存占用与ZO-SGD相当。此外，在合成二次问题和LLM微调任务中，MEAZO在分组或块结构优化设置下对步长选择表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究证明现有自适应ZO方法的内存开销是不必要的，通过MEAZO可以在保持性能的同时实现内存高效的自适应优化，为内存受限的LLM微调提供了更优的解决方案，并且该方法有理论收敛保证。

Abstract: We investigate the effectiveness of adaptive zeroth-order (ZO) optimization for memory-constrained fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs). Contrary to prior claims, we show that adaptive ZO methods such as ZO-Adam offer no convergence advantage over well-tuned ZO-SGD, while incurring significant memory overhead. Our analysis reveals that in high dimensions, ZO gradients lack coordinate-wise heterogeneity, rendering adaptive mechanisms memory inefficient. Leveraging this insight, we propose MEAZO, a memory-efficient adaptive ZO optimizer that tracks only a single scalar for global step size adaptation. We support our method with theoretical convergence guarantees under standard assumptions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and tasks demonstrate that MEAZO matches ZO-Adam's performance with the memory footprint of ZO-SGD. Additional experiments on synthetic quadratic problems and LLM fine-tuning further demonstrate MEAZO's enhanced robustness to step size choices, particularly in grouped or block-structured optimization settings.

</details>


### [126] [From Data Lifting to Continuous Risk Estimation: A Process-Aware Pipeline for Predictive Monitoring of Clinical Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03895)
*Pasquale Ardimento,Mario Luca Bernardi,Marta Cimitile,Samuele Latorre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向过程的临床路径预测监控管道，在COVID-19患者ICU入院预测任务中表现出色（AUC 0.906）。研究发现预测性能随临床事件累积而逐步提升，证明了过程感知表示方法在动态风险估计中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的回顾性流程挖掘方法存在局限性，无法对部分观察到的患者轨迹进行持续推理。本研究旨在开发一种可重现、过程感知的预测监控管道，以支持对患者旅程进行动态、连续的风险估计。

Method: 提出一个集成化管道，包含数据提升、时间重构、事件日志构建、基于前缀的表示和预测建模五个核心步骤。该方法支持对部分观察的临床路径进行连续推理，使用病例级划分训练和评估预测模型。

Result: 在4,479名COVID-19患者、46,804个前缀的数据集上进行验证，逻辑回归模型表现最佳（AUC 0.906，F1分数 0.835）。详细的前缀分析显示预测性能随临床事件累积而提升：从早期阶段AUC 0.642增长到晚期阶段0.942。

Conclusion: 医疗保健中的预测监控应被视为一个连续的、动态感知的过程。预测信号沿临床路径逐步显现，过程感知表示能够从不断演化的患者轨迹中实现有效的早期风险估计，风险估计值随患者旅程演变而逐步精细化。

Abstract: This paper presents a reproducible and process-aware pipeline for predictive monitoring of clinical pathways. The approach integrates data lifting, temporal reconstruction, event log construction, prefix-based representations, and predictive modeling to support continuous reasoning on partially observed patient trajectories, overcoming the limitations of traditional retrospective process mining. The framework is evaluated on COVID-19 clinical pathways using ICU admission as the prediction target, considering 4,479 patient cases and 46,804 prefixes. Predictive models are trained and evaluated using a case-level split, with 896 patients in the test set. Logistic Regression achieves the best performance (AUC 0.906, F1-score 0.835). A detailed prefix-based analysis shows that predictive performance improves progressively as new clinical events become available, with AUC increasing from 0.642 at early stages to 0.942 at later stages of the pathway. The results highlight two key findings: predictive signals emerge progressively along clinical pathways, and process-aware representations enable effective early risk estimation from evolving patient trajectories. Overall, the findings suggest that predictive monitoring in healthcare is best conceived as a continuous, dynamically aware process, in which risk estimates are progressively refined as the patient journey evolves.

</details>


### [127] [Optimal Posterior Sampling for Policy Identification in Tabular Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03921)
*Cyrille Kone,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种随机化且计算高效的算法，用于有限视界情节MDP中的最优策略识别。该算法结合了后验采样与在线学习，在保持O(S²AH)计算复杂度的同时，实现了样本复杂度的渐近最优性，并避免了现有方法中对log(1/δ)的次优依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有限视界情节MDP中(ε, δ)-PAC策略识别方法虽然提供了有限时间 guarantees，但存在两大缺陷：计算成本过高导致难以实施；对log(1/δ)的依赖呈现次优性。这些限制阻碍了理论与实际应用的发展。

Method: 提出了一种结合后验采样与在线学习算法的新方法来引导MDP中的探索过程。该算法采用随机化设计，每轮迭代计算复杂度为O(S²AH)，与标准的基于模型的方法相匹配，实现了计算高效性。

Result: 算法在样本复杂度方面达到了渐近最优性，包括后验收缩率方面的最优性。与MOCA和PEDEL等先前算法不同，该方法在渐近机制下 guarantee 依然有效，且避免了对log(1/δ)的次优多项式依赖。

Conclusion: 本研究为表格型MDP中的高效策略识别提供了理论见解与实用工具，成功解决了现有方法计算成本高和对log(1/δ)依赖次优的问题，在保持理论严谨性的同时提升了实用性。

Abstract: We study the $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC policy identification problem in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes. Existing approaches provide finite-time guarantees for approximate settings ($\varepsilon>0$) but suffer from high computational cost, rendering them hard to implement, and also suffer from suboptimal dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. We propose a randomized and computationally efficient algorithm for best policy identification that combines posterior sampling with an online learning algorithm to guide exploration in the MDP. Our method achieves asymptotic optimality in sample complexity, also in terms of posterior contraction rate, and runs in $O(S^2AH)$ per episode, matching standard model-based approaches. Unlike prior algorithms such as MOCA and PEDEL, our guarantees remain meaningful in the asymptotic regime and avoid sub-optimal polynomial dependence on $\log(1/δ)$. Our results provide both theoretical insights and practical tools for efficient policy identification in tabular MDPs.

</details>


### [128] [Integrating Feature Correlation in Differential Privacy with Applications in DP-ERM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03945)
*Tianyu Wang,Luhao Zhang,Rachel Cummings*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑特征敏感度差异的松弛差分隐私定义CorrDP，通过相关性感知框架在保证隐私的同时提高了效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私对所有特征施加统一隐私约束，忽视了实际应用中敏感特征与非敏感特征之间的固有区别，导致不必要的隐私预算浪费和效用损失。

Method: 提出CorrDP框架，使用全变差距离量化特征间相关性，允许非敏感特征即使在相关性存在的情况下也能被区别对待；设计了CorrDP下的差分隐私经验风险最小化算法，在梯度中注入距离依赖的噪声以改善理论效用保证；针对相关性距离未知的情况，提出从数据集估计距离的方法。

Result: CorrDP框架提供了改进的理论效用保证；当相关性距离未知时，估计方法也能达到相当的隐私-效用保证；在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验表明，基于CorrDP的DP-ERM算法在存在非敏感特征时始终优于标准DP框架。

Conclusion: 所提出的CorrDP框架成功解决了标准差分隐私忽略特征敏感度异质性的问题，通过区分处理敏感和非敏感特征，在保持隐私保护的同时显著提升了算法效用，为实际应用中具有特征敏感度差异的场景提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard differential privacy imposes uniform privacy constraints across all features, overlooking the inherent distinction between sensitive and insensitive features in practice. In this paper, we introduce a relaxed definition of differential privacy that accounts for such heterogeneity, allowing certain features to be treated as insensitive even when correlated with sensitive ones. We propose a correlation-aware framework, $\textsf{CorrDP}$, which relaxes privacy for insensitive features while accounting for their correlations with sensitive features, with the correlations quantified using total variation distance. We design algorithms for differentially private empirical risk minimization (DP-ERM) under the $\textsf{CorrDP}$ framework, incorporating distance-dependent noise into gradients for improved theoretical utility guarantees. When the correlation distance is unknown, we estimate it from the dataset and show that it achieves a comparable privacy-utility guarantee. We perform experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets and show that $\textsf{CorrDP}$-based DP-ERM algorithms consistently outperform the standard DP framework in the presence of insensitive features.

</details>


### [129] [Transformers with Selective Access to Early Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03953)
*Skye Gunasekaran,Téa Wright,Rui-Jie Zhu,Jason Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SATFormer，通过上下文相关的门控机制选择性地重用第一层值表示，在检索密集型任务上相比静态残差方法提升约1.5个平均点，且保持基线Transformer的吞吐量和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构通过静态混合系数将第一层值投影（V₁）均匀地暴露给所有token和注意力头，但V₁的效用在不同token、头和上下文中不应该是恒定的；不同位置可能需要不同程度的早期词法或语义信息访问。

Method: 将早期表示重用建模为检索问题而非连接问题，保留第一层值通路，并引入上下文相关的门控机制来控制访问。该门控机制允许模型根据当前上下文选择性决定是否以及如何重用早期表示，而非均匀复制静态残差。

Result: 在130M到1.3B参数规模的模型上，SATFormer在验证损失和零样本准确率上持续优于静态值残差和Transformer基线。在检索密集型基准测试上，相比静态值残差方法提升约1.5个平均点，同时保持接近基线Transformer的吞吐量和内存使用。门控分析显示稀疏、深度依赖、头特定和类别敏感的访问模式。

Conclusion: SATFormer成功实现了早期表示的选择性重用而非均匀残差复制，证明了将早期表示重用视为检索问题的有效性。该方法在性能提升的同时保持了计算效率，为Transformer架构中的跨层信息流动提供了新视角。

Abstract: Several recent Transformer architectures expose later layers to representations computed in the earliest layers, motivated by the observation that low-level features can become harder to recover as the residual stream is repeatedly transformed through depth. The cheapest among these methods add static value residuals: learned mixing coefficients that expose the first-layer value projection V_1 uniformly across tokens and heads. More expressive dense or dynamic alternatives recover finer-grained access, but at higher memory cost and lower throughput. The usefulness of V_1 is unlikely to be constant across tokens, heads, and contexts; different positions plausibly require different amounts of access to early lexical or semantic information. We therefore treat early-representation reuse as a retrieval problem rather than a connectivity problem, and introduce Selective Access Transformer (SATFormer), which preserves the first-layer value pathway while controlling access with a context-dependent gate. Across models from 130M to 1.3B parameters, SATFormer consistently improves validation loss and zero-shot accuracy over the static value-residual and Transformer baselines. Its strongest gains appear on retrieval-intensive benchmarks, where it improves over static value residuals by approximately 1.5 average points, while maintaining throughput and memory usage close to the baseline Transformer. Gate analyses suggest sparse, depth-dependent, head-specific, and category-sensitive access patterns, supporting the interpretation that SATFormer learns selective reuse of early representations rather than uniform residual copying. Our code is available at https://github.com/SkyeGunasekaran/SATFormer.

</details>


### [130] [Pretrained Model Representations as Acquisition Signals for Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03964)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Shikha Surana,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Zachary Weller-Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，预训练机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的潜空间已包含用于主动学习的充分信息。基于此提出的NTK和激活核采集策略，在反应化学基准测试中表现优异，将达成目标所需的训练数据量减少了28%-38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应化学机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的训练常受限于量子化学标签的高昂成本及过渡态构型的稀缺。虽然主动学习（AL）可缓解此问题，但其有效性高度依赖于采集规则。现有的采集方法通常需要辅助的不确定性预测头、贝叶斯训练或集成模型，增加了复杂性。

Method: 作者提出直接利用预训练MACE模型的潜空间信息进行数据采集，无需额外的不确定性模块。具体引入了两种采集信号：一种是有限宽度的神经正切核（NTK），另一种是基于隐藏层潜空间特征构建的激活核。

Result: 在反应化学基准测试中，这两种基于潜空间的核方法均显著优于固定描述符基线、集成模型分歧法和随机采集法。结果显示，达到性能目标所需的数据量平均减少了38%（能量误差）和28%（力误差）。此外，预训练模型生成的相似性空间能保持化学结构意义，并提供比随机初始化或固定描述符更可靠的残差不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 预训练过程能够将潜空间的几何结构与模型误差相对齐，从而为反应MLIP的微调提供了一种实用且充分的主动学习采集信号。这一发现消除了对复杂辅助机制的需求，简化了高效MLIP训练流程。

Abstract: Training machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for reactive chemistry is often bottlenecked by the high cost of quantum chemical labels and the scarcity of transition state configurations in candidate pools. Active learning (AL) can mitigate these costs, but its effectiveness hinges on the acquisition rule. We investigate whether the latent space of a pretrained MLIP already contains the information necessary for effective acquisition, eliminating the need for auxiliary uncertainty heads, Bayesian training and fine-tuning, or committee ensembles. We introduce two acquisition signals derived directly from a pretrained MACE potential: a finite-width neural tangent kernel (NTK) and an activation kernel built from hidden latent space features. On reactive-chemistry benchmarks, both kernels consistently outperform fixed-descriptor baselines, committee disagreement, and random acquisition, reducing the data required to reach performance targets by an average of 38% for energy error and 28% for force error. We further show that the pretrained model induces similarity spaces that preserve chemically meaningful structure and provide more reliable residual uncertainty estimates than randomly initialised or fixed-descriptor-based kernels. Our results suggest that pretraining aligns latent-space geometry with model error, yielding a practical and sufficient acquisition signal for reactive MLIP fine-tuning.

</details>


### [131] [A Closed-Form Adaptive-Landmark Kernel for Certified Point-Cloud and Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04046)
*Sushovan Majhi,Atish Mitra,Žiga Virk,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PALACE，一种数据自适应的持久图分类引擎，通过少量交叉验证调参和闭式理论保证，在多个基准数据集上取得了与梯度训练方法相媲美甚至更优的性能，同时提供了理论性能证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有持久图分类方法中，网格划分和参数选择缺乏理论指导、需要大量训练数据或梯度优化的问题，本文旨在构建一种具有闭式理论保证、数据自适应且调参成本低的持久图分类框架。

Method: 提出PALACE框架，基于覆盖理论（Landmark覆盖的Lebesgue数准则）构建核心。通过三个超参数（预算、半径、带宽）的少量交叉验证实现数据自适应。理论推导了结构失真下界λ、最优权重与采样位置、基于核-RKHS的分类率及匹配的Le Cam下界，并提供过滤选择规则。采用核-Mahalanobis边缘作为排序指标，并为每个预测提供非渐近和渐近形式的性能证书。

Result: 理论分析提供了四个闭式保证：失真下界λ及其在图表集中时的预算缩减优势；最优权重和采样位置的闭式解；分类率和样本复杂度下界；以及预测性能证书。实验上，PALACE在Orbit5k数据集上达到91.3%的准确率（匹配Persformer），在COX2和MUTAG上领先其他基于持久图的方法，在DHFR上具有竞争力。在数据域扩展8倍时，PALACE仍保持94%的性能，而均匀网格方法性能坍塌至随机水平。核-Mahalanobis边缘指标在化学图池中平均Spearman相关系数约为+0.60。

Conclusion: PALACE成功展示了通过理论驱动的覆盖构建和闭式推导，可以实现无需梯度训练、调参成本低且具有可证明性能保证的持久图分类方法，其性能可与基于梯度的先进模型相媲美，且在数据分布变化时具有更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We introduce PALACE (Persistence Adaptive-Landmark Analytic Classification Engine), the data-adaptive companion to PLACE, paying a small cross-validation tier on three knobs (budget, radii, bandwidth; $\leq 5$ choices each). A cover-theoretic core (Lebesgue-number criterion on the landmark cover) yields four closed-form guarantees. (i) A structural lower distortion bound $λ(τ;ν)$ on $\mathcal{D}_n$ under cross-diagram non-interference, with a $(D/L)^2$ budget reduction over the uniform grid when diagrams concentrate. (ii) Equal weights $w_k = K^{-1/2}$ maximizing $λ$, and farthest-point-sampling positions $2$-approximating the optimal $k$-center covering radius; both derived from training labels alone, no gradient training. (iii) A kernel-RKHS classification rate $O((k-1)\sqrt{K}/(γ\sqrt{m_{\min}}))$ with binary necessity threshold $m = Ω(\sqrt K/γ)$ from a matching Le Cam lower bound, and a closed-form filtration-selection rule. The kernel-Mahalanobis margin $\hatρ_{\mathrm{Mah}}$ is the strongest closed-form ranker across the chemical-graph pool (mean Spearman $ρ\approx +0.60$); the isotropic surrogate $\hatγ/\sqrt{K}$ admits a selection-consistency rate, and $\widehatλ$ from (i) provides an independent data-level signal (positive on COX2 and PTC). (iv) A per-prediction certificate, in non-asymptotic Pinelis and asymptotic Gaussian forms, with no calibration split. Empirically, PALACE is the strongest closed-form diagram-based method on Orbit5k ($91.3 \pm 1.0\%$, matching Persformer), leads every diagram-based competitor on COX2 and MUTAG, and is competitive on DHFR (within 1 pp of ECP). At $8\times$ domain inflation, adaptive placement maintains $94\%$ while the uniform grid collapses to chance ($25\%$ on 4-class data).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [CreativityBench: Evaluating Agent Creative Reasoning via Affordance-Based Tool Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02910)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Bingxiang He,Jeonghwan Kim,Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Aditi Tiwari,Dwip Dalal,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Yunzhu Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CreativityBench基准，用于评估大语言模型的创造性工具使用能力。实验发现现有模型虽然能选择合理的物体，但在识别正确部件、功能性和物理机制方面存在显著不足，缩放和推理策略收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在推理和环境交互任务上表现出色，但其创造性问题解决能力仍未被充分探索。本文从创造性工具使用的角度切入，研究模型如何通过推理物体的可供性和属性来创造性地重新利用现有物体，而非依赖常规用法。

Method: 构建包含4K实体和15万+可供性标注的大规模可供性知识库（KB），明确关联物体、部件、属性和可操作用途。基于该知识库生成14K个需要识别非显而易见但物理上可行解决方案的约束任务。在10个先进的大语言模型（包括闭源和开源模型）上进行系统评估。

Result: 评估结果显示，模型通常能够选择合理的物体，但在识别正确部件、可供性以及解决任务所需的潜在物理机制方面表现不佳，导致性能显著下降。模型缩放的改进很快饱和，强大的通用推理能力不能可靠地转化为创造性的可供性发现，常见的推理时策略（如思维链）带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 创造性工具使用仍然是当前模型面临的主要挑战，CreativityBench为研究智能的这一缺失维度提供了有价值的测试平台，对未来智能体的规划和推理模块设计具有潜在意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have led to strong performance on reasoning and environment-interaction tasks, yet their ability for creative problem-solving remains underexplored. We study this capability through the lens of creative tool use, where a model repurposes available objects by reasoning about their affordances and attributes rather than relying on canonical usage. As a first step, we introduce CreativityBench, a benchmark for evaluating affordance-based creativity in LLMs. To this end, we build a large-scale affordance knowledge base (KB) with 4K entities and 150K+ affordance annotations, explicitly linking objects, parts, attributes, and actionable uses. Building on this KB, we generate 14K grounded tasks that require identifying non-obvious yet physically plausible solutions under constraints. Evaluations across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs, including closed and open-source models, show that models can often select a plausible object, but fail to identify the correct parts, their affordances, and the underlying physical mechanism needed to solve the task, leading to a significant drop in performance. Furthermore, improvements from model scaling quickly saturate, strong general reasoning does not reliably translate to creative affordance discovery, and common inference-time strategies such as Chain-of-Thought yield limited gains. These results suggest that creative tool use remains a major challenge for current models, and that CreativityBench provides a useful testbed for studying this missing dimension of intelligence, with potential implications for planning and reasoning modules in future agents.

</details>


### [133] [Stable Agentic Control: Tool-Mediated LLM Architecture for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03034)
*Kerri Prinos,Lilianne Brush,Cameron Denton,Zhanqi Wang,Joshua Knox,Snehal Antani,Anton Foltz,Amy Villaseñor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种工具介导的架构，使LLM代理在高风险对抗性决策中具备形式化稳定性保证。通过Lean 4机器检验的复合Lyapunov函数证书，在真实企业攻击图上实现59%的博弈收益降低，并证明系统稳定性不依赖于控制器能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法为高风险对抗压力下的代理系统提供形式化保证。安全运营中心(SOCs)在对抗压力下配置端点检测与响应(EDR)策略的实际操作需求，亟需一种能够提供稳定性、可控性和可观测性形式化验证的架构。

Method: 提出工具介导架构：LLM代理使用确定性工具（Stackelberg最优响应、贝叶斯观测器更新、攻击图原语），从有限动作目录中选择动作，并在工具输出接口层面强制执行。通过Lean 4机器检验的复合Lyapunov函数，零sorry地证明了可控性、非对称传感器数据的可观测性，以及智能对抗扰动下的输入到状态稳定性(ISS)鲁棒性，并给出两个推论将证书扩展到目录中的任意控制器或对手。

Result: 在282个真实企业攻击图上，所有声明均以裕度成立。在成对攻防遥测数据上，工具介导的Claude Sonnet 4控制器相比确定性贪婪基线，将攻击者期望收益（博弈值）降低59%，在四种温度下40次运行中方差为零。Claude Haiku 4.5控制器收敛到次优博弈值但在额外40次运行中保持目录有界，证明架构稳定性不依赖控制器能力。

Conclusion: LLM代理的非确定性促进了策略的创造性探索，而工具介导架构确保了系统稳定性。该架构成功解耦了控制器的创造性与系统的形式化保证，为高风险对抗性决策提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems involved in high-stake decision-making under adversarial pressure need formal guarantees not offered by existing approaches. Motivated by the operational needs of security operations centers (SOCs) that must configure endpoint detection and response (EDR) policies under adversarial pressure, we present a tool-mediated architecture: LLM agents use deterministic tools (Stackelberg best-response, Bayesian observer updates, attack-graph primitives) and select from finite action catalogs enforced at the tool-output interface. A composite Lyapunov function machine-checked in Lean 4 with zero sorry certifies controllability, observability from asymmetric sensor data, and Input-to-State Stability (ISS) robustness under intelligent adversarial disturbance, with two corollaries extending the certificate to any controller or adversary from the catalogs. On 282 real enterprise attack graphs, the claims hold with margin. On paired offensive/defensive telemetry, a tool-mediated Claude Sonnet 4 controller reduces the attacker's expected payoff (game value) by 59% relative to a deterministic greedy baseline, with zero variance across 40 runs at four temperatures. A Claude Haiku 4.5 controller converges to suboptimal game values but stays catalog-bounded over an additional 40 runs, demonstrating that architectural stability is not dependent on the controller capability. The LLM agent's non-determinism furthers creative exploration of strategies, while the tool-mediated architecture ensures system stability.

</details>


### [134] [Computing Thiele Rules on Interval Elections and their Generalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03067)
*Dimitris Avramidis,Alexandra Lassota,Ulrike Schmidt-Kraepelin,Adrian Vetta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文解决了投票区间(VI)域上Thiele规则的计算复杂度开放问题，证明了虽然相关矩阵不是完全幺模的，但标准线性规划仍存在最优整数解，并提供了快速求解算法。同时将技术扩展到VCI和LC域，揭示了它们的包含关系，并证明了一种树状推广使得问题变为NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thiele规则(特别是比例批准投票PAV)在委员会投票中具有比例代表性、帕累托最优性等优良性质，但其主要缺点是一般情况下计算是NP难的。已知在候选者区间(CI)域上可通过线性规划在多项式时间内求解，但在相关的投票者区间(VI)域上的复杂度一直是开放问题，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 证明了VI域上相关约束矩阵虽然不是完全幺模的，但"标准"线性规划仍至少存在一个最优整数解，并提供了快速算法来找到它。该方法自然地扩展到投票者-候选者区间(VCI)域和线性一致性(LC)域。利用图论方法分析了LC与VCI域之间的关系，并给出了LC的另一种定义。同时研究了一种基于树的VCI推广形式。

Result: (1)解决了VI域上Thiele规则计算复杂度的开放问题，证明可在多项式时间内求解；(2)证明了LC域严格包含VCI域；(3)给出了LC的替代定义，具有更接近VCI的精神和自然的批准选举解释；(4)证明了在树状推广域上Thiele规则的计算变为NP难。

Conclusion: 结构化偏好下的Thiele规则具有更好的计算性质。本文不仅解决了长期存在的开放问题，还揭示了不同偏好域之间的关系，为结构化偏好下的计算社会选择提供了新的理论洞察。同时，通过证明某些推广形式仍保持NP难性，界定了有效计算方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Approval-based committee voting has received significant attention in the social choice community. Among the studied rules, Thiele rules, and especially Proportional Approval Voting (PAV), stand out for desirable properties such as proportional representation, Pareto optimality, and support monotonicity. Their main drawback is that computing a Thiele outcome is NP-hard in general.
  A glimpse of hope comes from the fact that Thiele rules are better behaved under structured preferences. On the candidate interval (CI) domain, they are computable in polynomial time via a linear program (LP) that has a totally unimodular constraint matrix. Surprisingly, this approach fails for the related voter interval (VI) domain, and the complexity of the problem has repeatedly been posed as an open question. Our main result resolves this question: although the relevant matrix is not totally unimodular, the ``standard'' LP still admits at least one optimal integral solution, and we provide a fast algorithm for finding it.
  Our technique naturally extends to the voter-candidate interval (VCI) domain, also known as the 1-dimensional voter-candidate range (1D-VCR) domain, and to the linearly consistent (LC) domain, both of which generalize the candidate and voter interval domains. Although both the VCI and LC domains have been studied in social choice, their relationship was unknown. We show, through connections to graph theory, that LC strictly contains VCI. We also provide an alternative definition of LC that is closer in spirit to VCI and has a natural interpretation in approval elections; this equivalence may be of independent interest. Finally, we study an alternative tree-based generalization of VCI and show that Thiele rules become NP-hard to compute on this domain.

</details>


### [135] [Making the Invisible Visible: Understanding the Mismatch Between Organizational Goals and Worker Experiences in AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03078)
*Christine P. Lee,Min Kyung Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过访谈医疗、金融和管理领域的专业人士，揭示了AI落地失败的核心原因在于忽视了“人”的因素，发现由于可用性差、期望错位等问题导致员工抵制，并提出需要从个体、任务和组织三个层面进行适应，将员工作为AI融合的中心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管组织引入AI旨在推动创新和提高效率，但许多推广工作因员工的抵制和整合困难而失败。这揭示了更深层次的问题：作为AI预期协作者的员工，在AI的设计和使用决策中往往被忽视，导致组织期望与员工实际体验之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 研究者对医疗、金融和管理领域中每日与AI系统交互的专业人士进行了访谈，深入考察了组织期望与员工体验之间的脱节现象。

Result: 研究识别出了阻碍AI采纳的关键障碍，包括可用性和互操作性差、期望错位、控制权有限以及沟通不足。这些挑战凸显了组织实施AI的方式与不断变化的员工需求、任务及工作流程之间存在差距，且现有系统未能对此提供支持。

Conclusion: 成功的AI采纳要求将员工视为AI融合的核心。文章提出了在个体、任务和组织层面的适应策略，以使AI系统更好地与现实工作实践保持一致，从而弥合实施差距。

Abstract: While AI is often introduced into organizations to drive innovation and efficiency, many adoption efforts fail as workers resist and struggle to integrate these systems. These failures point to a deeper issue: workers, the very people expected to collaborate with AI, are often invisible in decisions about how AI is designed and used. Drawing on interviews with professionals who interact with AI systems daily in healthcare, finance, and management, we examine the disconnect between organizational expectations and worker experiences. We identify key barriers, including poor usability and interoperability, misaligned expectations, limited control, and insufficient communication. These challenges highlight a gap between how organizations implement AI and the evolving worker needs, tasks, and workflows that it fails to support. We argue that successful adoption requires recognizing workers as central to AI integration and propose adaptation strategies at the individual, task, and organizational levels to better align AI systems with real-world practices.

</details>


### [136] [Programmatic Context Augmentation for LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03101)
*Hao Liu,Xiao-Wen Yang,Atharva Sehgal,Yixin Wang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合程序化上下文增强的LLM进化搜索框架，用于符号回归任务。该方法允许模型通过代码与数据集交互，提取比单纯的标量评估指标更丰富的信息，从而在效率和准确性上超越了现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的符号回归方法通常仅依赖标量评估指标（如均方误差）作为搜索过程中的反馈，忽略了数据集中蕴含的丰富信息，限制了模型的表达能力和搜索效率。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于LLM的进化搜索框架，该框架引入了程序化上下文增强。通过支持基于代码的数据集交互，模型能够主动执行数据分析并提取有意义的信号，而不仅仅依赖于聚合的评估分数。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench等先进基准测试上的评估表明，该方法在效率和准确性方面均优于强力基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过引入程序化上下文增强，该方法有效解决了现有LLM符号回归方法反馈信号单一的问题，证明了利用代码交互从数据集中提取深层信息对于提升符号回归性能的重要性。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR), the task of discovering mathematical expressions that best describe a given dataset, remains a fundamental challenge in scientific discovery. Traditional approaches, primarily based on genetic algorithms and related evolutionary methods, have proven useful but suffer from scalability and expressivity limitations. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based evolutionary search methods have been introduced into SR and show promise. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically rely on scalar evaluation metrics, such as mean squared error, as the sole source of feedback during the search process, thereby overlooking the rich information embedded in the dataset. To address this limitation, we propose a novel LLM-based evolutionary search framework that incorporates programmatic context augmentation. By enabling code-based interactions with the dataset, our method can actively perform data analysis and extract informative signals, beyond aggregated evaluation scores. We evaluate our framework on advanced benchmarks, such as LLM-SRBench, and demonstrate superior efficiency and accuracy compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [137] [QKVShare: Quantized KV-Cache Handoff for Multi-Agent On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03884)
*Pratik Honavar,Tejpratap GVSL*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QKVShare框架，通过量化KV缓存移交实现了边缘设备上多智能体LLM系统的高效上下文切换。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上的实验表明，该方法在保持竞争力的同时，将TTFT显著降低（8K上下文下从1029.7ms降至397.1ms），为设备端系统优化提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的多智能体LLM系统需要高效移交潜在上下文，但现有方案（昂贵的重新预填充或全精度KV传输）成本过高，缺乏高效的量化KV缓存移交解决方案。

Method: 提出QKVShare框架，包含三个核心组件：1) token级混合精度分配的自适应量化策略；2) 自包含的CacheCard表示方法；3) 与HuggingFace兼容的缓存注入路径，实现量化KV缓存在智能体间的高效移交。

Result: 在150个GSM8K问题和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上的实验表明：自适应量化在深层跳跃、高预算设置下相比均匀量化有明显优势；TTFT显著降低，1K上下文下从150.2ms降至130.7ms，8K上下文下从1029.7ms降至397.1ms；后注入生成阶段（而非卡片创建）主导当前延迟路径。

Conclusion: 量化KV缓存移交是设备端系统的有前景方向，但未来研究需要更强的控制器消融实验和公平的运行时对比，以进一步验证和优化该方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems on edge devices need to hand off latent context efficiently, but the practical choices today are expensive re-prefill or full-precision KV transfer. We study QKVShare, a framework for quantized KV-cache handoff between agents that combines token-level mixed-precision allocation, a self-contained CacheCard representation, and a HuggingFace-compatible cache injection path. Our current results support a narrower but clearer story than the original draft: on 150 GSM8K problems with Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, adaptive quantization remains competitive under repeated handoff and shows its clearest gains against uniform quantization in deeper-hop, higher budget settings; for handoff latency, the QKVShare path reduces TTFT relative to full re prefill at every tested context, from 130.7 ms vs. 150.2 ms at nominal 1K context to 397.1 ms vs. 1029.7 ms at nominal 8K context;. Stage timing shows that post-injection generation, not card creation, dominates the current QKVShare latency path. These results position quantized KV handoff as a promising on-device systems direction while also highlighting the need for stronger controller ablations and apples-to-apples runtime comparisons.

</details>


### [138] [Are you with me? A Framework for Detecting Mental Model Discrepancies in Task-Based Team Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03149)
*Katharine Kowalyshyn,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架来识别和分类团队对话中出现的四类心理模型差异（无依据信念、错误信念、信念矛盾和遗漏），并证明了这些差异模式能够预测团队未来的心理模型错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队成员在协作过程中并非总能通过自然语言完整传达任务状态更新，导致成员间的心理模型产生差异，进而负面影响团队整体绩效。传统的共享心理模型（SMM）评估方法依赖回顾性专家编码，无法捕捉实时协调动态，因此需要一种新的方法来实时识别和预测这些差异。

Method: 研究提出了一个框架，用于识别和分类四种类型的心理模型差异：无依据信念、错误信念、信念矛盾和遗漏。研究收集了20个二人团队在四个连续层级中执行协作物体识别任务的对话数据，通过分析对话中自然产生的差异模式，使用历史差异计数的均匀加权平均作为探索性基线进行预测分析。

Result: 研究发现这些差异模式包含预测信号，使用历史差异计数的平均（采用均匀加权作为探索性基线）能够实现有意义的预测准确率，且不同类型的差异具有不同的可预测性。

Conclusion: 团队对话中自然产生的心理模型差异模式可以作为预测未来心理模型错位的有效信号，这为开发实时监测和干预团队协作问题的新方法提供了理论基础，克服了传统回顾性评估方法的局限性。

Abstract: Humans typically use natural language to update teammates on task states. Since not all updates are communicated, discrepancies arise between the team members' mental models that negatively affect overall team performance. How can we categorize such discrepancies? Do misalignments detected in team dialogue predict future mental model misalignments? Traditional shared mental model (SMM) assessment methods rely on retrospective expert coding that cannot capture real-time coordination dynamics. We propose a framework to identify and categorize four types of mental model discrepancies: unsupported beliefs, false beliefs, belief contradictions, and omissions, all of which can naturally emerge in team dialogues. Using dialogues from twenty dyad teams performing collaborative object identification tasks across four sequential levels, we demonstrate that these discrepancy patterns contain predictive signals. Averaging historical discrepancy counts achieves meaningful prediction accuracy using uniform weighting as an exploratory baseline, with differential predictability across discrepancy types.

</details>


### [139] [Learning Correct Behavior from Examples: Validating Sequential Execution in Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03159)
*Reshabh K Sharma,Gaurav Mittal,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合支配者分析与多模态大语言模型的算法，仅需2-10条通过执行的轨迹即可自动学习正确行为模型并验证新执行，解决了自主智能体序列行为验证需大量人工标注或训练数据的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体日益复杂，验证其序列行为面临巨大挑战。传统测试方法需要手动规约、精确序列匹配或成千上万的训练样本，成本高昂且扩展性差。

Method: 结合编译理论中的支配者分析与多模态大语言模型的语义理解能力，识别关键状态并处理非确定性行为；利用前缀树接受器构建泛化的真值模型，通过多层等价性检测合并轨迹，并采用拓扑子序列匹配验证新执行。

Result: 在控制实验中，系统仅使用3条训练轨迹即在检测产品缺陷和误报成功方面达到高准确率，并支持跨UI测试、代码生成和机器人流程等多样领域。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了验证门槛，仅需极少量样本即可实现高精度、可解释的自主智能体行为验证，具有良好的跨域适用性与实用价值。

Abstract: As autonomous agents become increasingly sophisticated, validating their sequential behavior presents a significant challenge. Traditional testing approaches require manual specification, exact sequence matching, or thousands of training examples. We present a novel algorithm that automatically learns correct behavior from just 2-10 passing execution traces and validates new executions against this learned model. Our approach combines dominator analysis from compiler theory with multimodal large language model-powered semantic understanding to identify essential states and handle non-deterministic behavior. The system constructs a generalized ground truth model using Prefix Tree Acceptors, merges traces through multi-tiered equivalence detection, and validates new executions via topological subsequence matching. In controlled experiments, our system achieved high accuracy in detecting product bugs and false successes using only 3 training traces. This approach provides explainable validation results with coverage metrics and works across diverse domains including UI testing, code generation, and robotic processes.

</details>


### [140] [Terminus-4B: Can a Smaller Model Replace Frontier LLMs at Agentic Execution Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03195)
*Spandan Garg,Vikram Nitin,Yufan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Terminus-4B，一个专门用于终端执行任务的微调小型语言模型（SLM）。研究表明，该模型在减少主代理约30%的token使用量的同时，在SWE-Bench Pro等基准测试中保持了与前沿模型（如Claude Sonnet、GPT-5.3-Codex）相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代编码代理系统常将专门的子任务（如搜索、调试、终端执行）委托给子代理，以保持主代理上下文窗口的清洁。通常这些子代理会使用昂贵的前沿模型。本文旨在探究微调后的小型语言模型是否能在智能体终端执行任务中达到与前沿模型相当的性能，从而在降低成本的同时提升系统效率。

Method: 作者提出了Terminus-4B模型，基于Qwen3-4B进行后训练。训练流程包括：1）监督微调（SFT）；2）基于评分标准的LLM-as-judge奖励信号的强化学习（RL）训练。整个模型专门针对智能体终端执行任务进行优化。研究进行了广泛的评估，涵盖多种前沿模型对比、训练消融实验以及主代理配置测试。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）Terminus-4B能够将主代理的token使用量减少高达约30%；2）在SWE-Bench Pro和内部SWE-Bench C#基准测试中，对代理性能无负面影响；3）关键指标改善，主代理更依赖子代理的输出，减少自身执行终端任务的频率；4）该模型不仅缩小了原始Qwen模型与Claude Sonnet/Opus/GPT-5.3-Codex等前沿模型的差距，在多项指标上甚至超越了这些前沿模型。

Conclusion: 微调的小型语言模型可以在特定子任务中有效替代前沿模型。Terminus-4B成功证明了通过SFT和RL训练的SLM能够在智能体终端执行任务中实现高效、低成本的解决方案，为构建更经济的分层智能体架构提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern coding agents increasingly delegate specialized subtasks to subagents, which are smaller, focused agentic loops that handle narrow responsibilities like search, debugging or terminal execution. This architectural pattern keeps the main agent's context window clean by isolating verbose outputs (e.g. build logs, test results, etc.) within the subagent context. Typically when agents employ subagents for such tasks, they use frontier models as these subagents. In this paper, we investigate whether a finetuned small language model (SLM) can achieve comparable performance to frontier models in the task of agentic terminal execution. We present Terminus-4B, which is a post-trained Qwen3-4B model via Supervised Finetuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) using rubric-based LLM-as-judge reward, specifically for this task. In our extensive evaluation spanning various frontier models, training ablations and main agent configurations, we find that Terminus-4B is able to reduce the token usage of the main agent by up to ~30% compared to the No Subagent baseline with no impact to agent performance on benchmarks like SWE-Bench Pro and our internal SWE-Bench C# benchmark, which tends to be heavy in verbose execution tasks. Furthermore, Terminus-4B improves key metrics showing the main agent relying on the outputs of the subagent and doing fewer terminal execution tasks by itself. We see that our model not only closes the gap between the Vanilla Qwen model and frontier models like Claude Sonnet / Opus / GPT-5.3-Codex, but often even exceeds their performance.

</details>


### [141] [Stop Automating Peer Review Without Rigorous Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03202)
*Joachim Baumann,Jiaxin Pei,Sanmi Koyejo,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文反对使用当前AI系统生成论文评审，通过实证研究发现AI评审存在过度共识和易被操控两大关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型被视为解决同行评审危机的潜在方案，但缺乏对其在学术评审场景中适用性的严格评估。作者旨在探讨当前AI系统是否适合用于自动化论文评审。

Method: 对比分析人类与AI生成的ICLR 2026评审意见，并评估自动化论文改写对不同AI评审者的影响，从而识别AI评审系统的潜在缺陷。

Result: 发现两个关键问题：1）AI评审者表现出群体思维效应，在论文内外过度一致，降低了观点多样性；2）AI评审分数极易被操控——通过提示LLM改写论文即可显著提高AI评审分数，表明AI评审者容易被文风变化而非科学内容本身所欺骗。

Conclusion: 不可操控性和评审多样性是自动化的必要但非充分条件。解决同行评审危机需要建立专门的科学评审自动化方法，而非未经严格评估就部署通用LLM系统。

Abstract: Large language models offer a tempting solution to address the peer review crisis. This position paper argues that today's AI systems should not be used to produce paper reviews. We ground this position in an empirical comparison of human- versus AI-generated ICLR 2026 reviews and an evaluation of the effect of automated paper rewriting on different AI reviewers. We identify two critical issues: 1) AI reviewers exhibit a hivemind effect of excessive agreement within and across papers that reduces perspective diversity. 2) AI review scores are trivially gameable through paper laundering: prompting an LLM to rewrite a paper could significantly increase the scores from AI reviewers, demonstrating that LLM reviewers are easy to game through stylistic changes rather than scientific results. However, non-gameability and review diversity are necessary but not sufficient conditions for automation. We argue that addressing the peer review crisis requires a science of peer review automation -- not general-purpose LLMs deployed without rigorous evaluation.

</details>


### [142] [ADAPTS: Agentic Decomposition for Automated Protocol-agnostic Tracking of Symptoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03212)
*Alexandria K. Vail,Marcelo Cicconet,Katie Aafjes-van Doorn,Ryan Maroney,Marc Aafjes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADAPTS框架，利用混合智能体大语言模型架构，对非结构化临床访谈进行抑郁和焦虑严重程度的自动化评估，在高差异访谈中表现优于原始人工评分，实现了协议无关的专家级精准度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非受限的临床互动中建模潜在的临床构念（如精神症状）是情感计算领域的独特挑战。现有的评估方法往往受限于特定的访谈协议，缺乏通用性和可扩展性，尤其在资源有限的环境中难以实现客观、标准化的精神病学评估。

Method: 提出了ADAPTS（Agentic Decomposition for Automated Protocol-agnostic Tracking of Symptoms）框架，采用混合智能体大语言模型架构。该方法将长篇临床访谈分解为针对特定症状的推理任务，在保留时间和说话人对齐的同时生成可审计的推理依据。研究引入了“扩展”协议以整合定性临床常规，并在两个独立数据集（N=204）上评估了泛化能力。

Result: 在两个具有不同访谈结构的独立数据集上进行了验证。在高差异访谈中，自动化评分的绝对误差（22）优于原始人工评分（绝对误差26）。实施“扩展”协议后，评分稳定性显著提高，绝对一致性达到ICC(2,1) = 0.877。

Conclusion: ADAPTS框架实现了协议无关的专家级精神症状严重程度评估，为客观、可扩展的精神病学评估奠定了基础。该架构目前基于文本，但可扩展至包括声学和视觉特征在内的多模态输入，特别适用于资源有限的医疗环境。

Abstract: Modeling latent clinical constructs from unconstrained clinical interactions is a unique challenge in affective computing. We present ADAPTS (Agentic Decomposition for Automated Protocol-agnostic Tracking of Symptoms), a framework for automated rating of depression and anxiety severity using a mixture-of-agents LLM architecture. This approach decomposes long-form clinical interviews into symptom-specific reasoning tasks, producing auditable justifications while preserving temporal and speaker alignment. Generalization was evaluated across two independent datasets ($N=204$) with distinct interview structures. On high-discrepancy interviews, automated ratings approximated expert benchmarks ($\text{absolute error}=22$) more closely than original human ratings ($\text{absolute error}=26$). Implementing an ``extended'' protocol that incorporates qualitative clinical conventions significantly stabilized ratings, with absolute agreement reaching $\text{ICC(2,1)} = 0.877$. These findings suggest that the ADAPTS framework enables promising evaluations of psychiatric severity. While the current implementation is purely text-based, the underlying architecture is readily extensible to multimodal inputs, including acoustic and visual features. By approximating expert-level precision in a protocol-agnostic manner, this framework provides a foundation for objective and scalable psychiatric assessment, especially in resource-limited settings.

</details>


### [143] [Evaluating Prompting and Execution-Based Methods for Deterministic Computation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03227)
*Hongkun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在精确计算任务上表现不佳，但通过生成可执行代码（PoT）或训练专用小模型可以实现完美准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在自然语言理解和推理方面表现出色，但其在执行精确、确定性计算任务的能力尚不清楚，现有提示策略的有效性有待系统性评估。

Method: 构建了包含多样化自然语言指令的合成数据集，系统评估了Chain-of-Thought (CoT)、Least-to-Most分解、Program-of-Thought (PoT)和Self-Consistency (SC)四种提示策略在二进制计数、最长子串检测和算术评估等精确计算任务上的表现。此外，还训练了CodeT5-small小模型来生成可执行程序。

Result: 标准提示方法在基于序列的任务上仅达到中等准确率；CoT改进有限；Least-to-Most存在误差累积问题；PoT通过生成可执行代码并由外部解释器执行实现了完美准确率；Self-Consistency通过多数投票提高了鲁棒性但计算开销大；CodeT5-small在所有任务的留出测试数据上均达到完美准确率且训练成本极低。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可能只是在模拟推理模式，而无法可靠地执行精确的符号计算。对于确定性任务，将LLMs与外部工具结合或使用专用模型能提供更可靠高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and reasoning. However, their ability to perform exact, deterministic computation remains unclear. In this work, we systematically evaluate multiple prompting strategies, including Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Least-to-Most decomposition, Program-of-Thought (PoT), and Self-Consistency (SC), on tasks requiring precise and error-free outputs, including binary counting, longest substring detection, and arithmetic evaluation. To support this study, we introduce a synthetic dataset with diverse natural language instructions, enabling controlled evaluation of exact computation across multiple task types. Our results show that standard prompting methods achieve only moderate accuracy on sequence-based tasks. CoT provides limited improvement, while Least-to-Most suffers from error accumulation. In contrast, PoT achieves perfect accuracy by generating executable code and delegating computation to an external interpreter. Self-Consistency improves robustness through majority voting, but incurs substantial computational overhead. We further train a small domain-specific model (CodeT5-small) to generate executable programs, which achieves perfect accuracy on held-out synthetic test data across all tasks with minimal training cost. Overall, our findings suggest that LLMs may simulate reasoning patterns rather than reliably perform exact symbolic computation. For deterministic tasks, combining LLMs with external tools or using specialized models provides a more reliable and efficient solution.

</details>


### [144] [cotomi Act: Learning to Automate Work by Watching You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03231)
*Masafumi Oyamada,Kunihiro Takeoka,Kosuke Akimoto,Ryoma Obara,Masafumi Enomoto,Haochen Zhang,Daichi Haraguchi,Takuya Tamura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了cotomi Act，一个浏览器智能体系统，在WebArena基准测试中以80.4%的成功率超越人类基线（78.2%），并具备从用户行为中学习组织知识的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索浏览器智能体能否通过观察用户操作来学习其工作流程，旨在同时解决可靠的多步任务执行与持久化组织知识积累两大挑战。

Method: 执行层面采用自适应惰性观察、基于语言差异的历史压缩、粗粒度动作及测试时Best-of-N动作选择等策略；知识层面通过行为到知识管道被动观察浏览行为，将其抽象为任务板和维基等工件，并通过共享工作空间实现用户与智能体的协同编辑。

Result: 在WebArena 179任务人工评估子集上达到80.4%的成功率，超越报告的78.2%人类基线；对照代理评估证实，随着行为衍生知识的积累，任务成功率得到提升。

Conclusion: cotomi Act成功证明了结合高性能任务执行与从用户行为中持续学习组织知识的可行性，为智能体辅助用户工作提供了新的方向。

Abstract: What if a browser agent could learn your work simply by watching you do it? We present cotomi Act, a browser-based computer-using agent that combines reliable multi-step task execution with persistent organizational knowledge learned from user behavior. For execution, an agent scaffold with adaptive lazy observation, verbal-diff-based history compression, coarse-grained actions, and test-time scaling via best-of-N action selection achieves 80.4% on the 179-task WebArena human-evaluation subset, exceeding the reported 78.2% human baseline.
