Notably, OpenSeeker-v2 represents the first state-of-the-art search agent within its model scale and paradigm to be developed by a purely academic team using only SFT. We are excited to open-source the OpenSeeker-v2 model weights and share our simple yet effective findings to make frontier search agent research more accessible to the community.

</details>


### [179] [An Information-Geometric Framework for Stability Analysis of Large Language Models under Entropic Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24076)
*Hikmat Karimov,Rahid Zahid Alekberli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受热力学启发的建模框架，通过整合任务效用、外部不确定性熵及内部结构代理指标，评估大语言模型在不确定性和扰动条件下的输出稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在高风险和操作场景中的广泛应用，仅基于聚合准确率的评估策略不足以表征系统的可靠性，亟需新的评估方法来分析模型在不确定性条件下的行为稳定性。

Method: 提出一种受热力学启发的建模框架，引入复合稳定性分数，该分数整合任务效用、作为外部不确定性度量的熵，以及两个内部结构代理指标（内部整合与对齐反思能力）。使用IST-20基准测试协议及相关元数据，对四个当代大语言模型的80个模型-场景观测数据进行分析。

Result: 所提出的公式在稳定性评分上始终高于简化的效用-熵基线，平均提升0.0299（95%置信区间：0.0247-0.0351）。在高熵条件下，观测到的增益更为显著，表明该框架捕捉到了一种不确定性的非线性衰减形式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种紧凑且可解释的建模视角，将不确定性、性能和内部结构连接在统一的评估框架内，旨在补充现有的基准测试方法，支持AI安全、可靠性和治理的持续讨论。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes and operational settings, evaluation strategies based solely on aggregate accuracy are often insucient to characterize system reliability. This study proposes a thermodynamic inspired modeling framework for analyzing the stability of LLM outputs under conditions of uncertainty and perturbation. The framework introduces a composite stability score that integrates task utility, entropy as a measure of external uncertainty, and two internal structural proxies: internal integration and aligned reective capacity. Rather than interpreting these quantities as physical variables, the formulation is intended as an interpretable abstraction that captures how internal structure may modulate the impact of disorder on model behavior. Using the IST-20 benchmarking protocol and associated metadata, we analyze 80 modelscenario observations across four contemporary LLMs. The proposed formulation consistently yields higher stability scores than a reduced utilityentropy baseline, with a mean improvement of 0.0299 (95% CI: 0.02470.0351). The observed gain is more pronounced under higher entropy conditions, suggesting that the framework captures a form of nonlinear attenuation of uncertainty. We do not claim a fundamental physical law or a complete theory of machine ethics. Instead, the contribution of this work is a compact and interpretable modeling perspective that connects uncertainty, performance, and internal structure within a unied evaluation lens. The framework is intended to complement existing benchmarking approaches and to support ongoing discussions in AI safety, reliability, and governance.

</details>


### [180] [The Kerimov-Alekberli Model: An Information-Geometric Framework for Real-Time System Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24083)
*Hikmat Karimov,Rahid Zahid Alekberli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kerimov-Alekberli模型，一个将非平衡态热力学与随机控制形式化关联的新型信息几何框架，通过物理熵增原理实现了对自主系统伦理失范行为的实时检测与量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将AI安全从基于启发式规则的伦理框架转变为具有严格物理基础的理论范式，解决自主系统伦理对齐缺乏可量化物理依据的问题。

Method: 建立非平衡态热力学与随机控制之间的形式化同构，将系统异常定义为黎曼流形上的偏离；以Kullback-Leibler散度为主度量，设定基于Fisher信息度量的动态阈值；利用Landauer原理证明对抗性扰动通过增加系统信息熵产生可测量的物理功。

Result: 在NSL-KDD数据集和无人机轨迹模拟上的验证表明，该模型通过FPT触发器实现了有效的实时检测，并在基准数据集上取得了高准确率和低误报率的优异性能指标。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将伦理违规根植于可量化的物理功和熵信息，为AI安全提供了严格的物理基础，成功将AI安全范式从启发式规则转变为基于热力学的稳定性范式。

Abstract: This study introduces the Kerimov-Alekberli model, a novel information-geometric framework that redefines AI safety by formally linking non-equilibrium thermodynamics to stochastic control for the ethical alignment of autonomous systems. By establishing a formal isomorphism between non-equilibrium thermodynamics and stochastic control, we define systemic anomalies as deviations from a Riemannian manifold. The model utilizes the Kullback-Leibler divergence as the primary metric, governed by a dynamic threshold derived from the Fisher Information Metric. We further ground this framework in the Landauer Principle, proving that adversarial perturbations perform measurable physical work by increasing the system's informational entropy. Validation on the NSL-KDD dataset and unmanned aerial vehicle trajectory simulations demonstrated that our model achieves effective real-time detection via the FPT trigger, with strong performance metrics (e.g., high accuracy and low FPR) on benchmark datasets. This study provides a rigorous physical foundation for AI safety, transitioning from heuristic, rule-based ethical frameworks to a thermodynamics-based stability paradigm by grounding ethical violations in quantifiable physical work and entropic information.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Degeneracy-Aware Functional and Algorithmic Resilience in Virtualized 6G Networks Under Correlated Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03035)
*Mohamed Khalafalla Hassan,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出“退化感知”视角，指出冗余副本数量可能高估系统韧性，通过FSS、ARQ和MLDI三个指标量化结构与功能的多样性，证明退化是6G系统的实用韧性基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可编程与虚拟化网络中，冗余是保障服务连续性的常用手段。然而，复制功能常共享平台、软件栈和控制依赖，易受相关性故障影响。仅依赖副本计数可能导致对真实韧性的过高估计，因此需要一种新的视角来评估系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 本文采用“退化感知”视角，即鲁棒性依赖于结构多样但功能等价的替代方案。研究提出了三个互补指标：功能替代得分（FSS），量化某功能在结构上独特的替代品；算法韧性商（ARQ），衡量在性能相当的前提下算法间的多样性；多层退化指数（MLDI），刻画功能多样性在架构各层间的分布情况。通过在合成数据上执行针对性的破坏协议进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，冗余度与鲁棒性可能存在显著分歧。FSS成功将结构多样性与副本计数剥离；ARQ能有效区分真正的算法替代方案与近乎重复的实现；MLDI揭示了在仅分析冗余时被忽略的跨层缓冲能力。

Conclusion: 研究发现确立“退化”作为一种实用的韧性基元，对于开放、解耦和虚拟化的6G系统具有重要价值，能够更准确地评估和提升系统的真实韧性。

Abstract: Redundancy is widely used to sustain service continuity in programmable and virtualized networks; however, replicated functions often share platforms, software stacks, and control dependencies, making them vulnerable to correlated failures. Consequently, replica counts alone may overestimate true resilience. This paper adopts a degeneracy-aware perspective, where robustness depends on the availability of structurally diverse yet functionally equivalent alternatives. We formalize this perspective through three complementary metrics: the Functional Substitution Score (FSS), which quantifies structurally distinct substitutes for a function; the Algorithmic Resilience Quotient (ARQ), which measures diversity among algorithms that remain comparable in delivered performance; and the Multi-Layer Degeneracy Index (MLDI), which captures how functional diversity is distributed across architectural layers. Using targeted disruption protocols on a synthesized data, we show that redundancy and robustness can diverge substantially. The results show that FSS separates structural diversity from replica count, ARQ distinguishes genuine algorithmic alternatives from near-duplicate implementations, and MLDI captures cross-layer buffering that remains hidden under redundancy-only analysis. These findings establish degeneracy as a practical resilience primitive for open, disaggregated, and virtualized 6G systems.

</details>


### [182] [Sensitivity Analysis of Tactical Wireless Network Design Under Realistic Operational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03072)
*Wissem Ahmed Zaid,Alain Hertz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对战术无线网络设计进行了全面的敏感性分析，揭示了配置参数对网络拓扑质量和性能的影响机制，并识别出具有尺度依赖性的技术转换和阈值效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于优化的方法已被广泛探索，但配置参数对战术无线网络拓扑质量和整体性能的影响仍未被充分理解，因此需要系统性地研究参数变化对网络设计的影响。

Method: 采用禁忌搜索元启发式算法生成优化拓扑，并将参数分为三类进行系统性研究：(i)结构拓扑规则（包括主枢纽选择）；(ii)技术因素（如天线波束宽度）；(iii)目标函数中的建模参数。使用Friedman检验和Wilcoxon检验进行统计分析，评估不同网络规模下观察到的变化的显著性。

Result: 研究发现结构约束中存在尺度依赖性的技术转换和阈值效应；分析区分了从根本上重塑网络拓扑的参数与主要影响性能幅度的参数；识别出不同参数对网络设计影响的本质差异。

Conclusion: 这些发现为任务关键型战术无线部署中的参数调优和拓扑配置提供了实用指导，有助于优化网络设计决策。

Abstract: The design of tactical wireless networks reflects a complex interplay among structural constraints, technological choices, and underlying modeling assumptions. Although optimization-based approaches have been widely explored, the impact of configuration parameters on network topology quality and overall performance is still not fully understood. This paper presents a comprehensive sensitivity analysis of tactical wireless network design under realistic operational constraints. It systematically investigates three categories of parameters: (i) structural topology rules, including master hub selection; (ii) technological factors such as antenna beamwidth; and (iii) modeling parameters embedded in the objective formulation. Optimized topologies are produced using a Tabu Search metaheuristic, and statistical analyses based on the Friedman and Wilcoxon tests are performed to assess the significance of observed variations across different network sizes. The findings reveal scale-dependent technological transitions and threshold effects in structural constraints. The analysis differentiates parameters that fundamentally reshape network topology from those that primarily influence performance magnitude. Together, these insights provide practical guidance for parameter tuning and topology configuration in mission-critical tactical wireless deployments.

</details>


### [183] [PERFECT: Personalized Federated Learning for CBRS Radar Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03199)
*Shafi Ullah Khan,Madan Baduwal,Vini Chaudhary,Debashri Roy*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出PERFECT联邦学习框架，在CBRS频段实现雷达信号的高效检测（召回率达99%），同时保护数据隐私并解决非独立同分布数据问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBRS频段的环境感知能力(ESC)传感器需要在强商业LTE/5G干扰下检测微弱的海军雷达信号，而传统集中式检测模型存在数据隐私隐患，且难以处理地理分散传感器产生的非独立同分布(non-IID)数据。

Method: 设计新型联邦学习框架PERFECT，通过ESC级个性化(model personalization)在本地传感器上训练模型以保护隐私，有效处理不同ESC观测不同雷达类型的non-IID场景。

Result: 通过大量仿真验证，PERFECT实现了雷达检测要求的99%召回率，性能与集中式方法相当，同时显著增强了隐私保护、效率和可扩展性。

Conclusion: PERFECT框架为动态频谱共享提供了一种保护隐私、高效且可扩展的解决方案，成功解决了传统集中式方法在隐私保护和non-IID数据处理方面的局限性。

Abstract: The Citizens Broadband Radio Service (CBRS) band is pivotal for expanding next-generation wireless services, but its success hinges on robustly protecting incumbent users, such as naval radar systems, from interference. This task is delegated to a network of Environmental Sensing Capability (ESC) sensors, which must detect faint radar signals amidst heavy co-channel interference from commercial LTE and 5G users. Traditional centralized detection models raise significant data privacy concerns and are ill-suited for the Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (non-IID) nature of data from geographically dispersed sensors. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel Federated Learning (FL) framework PERFECT that leverages ESC level personalization for robust and efficient radar detection. PERFECT preserves privacy by training models locally on ESC sensors. Furthermore, our framework is the first to effectively handle non-IID scenarios through model personalization where different ESCs observe distinct radar types. We demonstrate through extensive simulations that PERFECT achieves the mandated 99% recall for radar detection, matching centralized performance while significantly enhancing privacy, efficiency, and scalability for dynamic spectrum sharing.

</details>


### [184] [Renewables Power the Orbit? Achieving Sustainable Space Edge Computing via QoS-Aware Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03232)
*Xiaoyi Fan,Yi Ching Chou,Hao Fang,Long Chen,Haoyuan Zhao,Ershun Du,Chongqing Kang,Zhe Chen,Jiangchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出SQSO框架及$\text{AO}^2$算法，通过将LEO卫星任务自适应卸载到可再生能源富集区的数据中心，在保证QoS的同时显著降低能耗与电池损耗，实现了空间网络与能源基础设施的协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座在轨计算需求增长导致电池加速老化且引发可持续性担忧；与此同时，可再生能源富集地区因传输瓶颈存在大量弃能现象。作者识别出卫星-电网协同设计的机会：将任务关键数据卸载至与可再生能源电厂共址的数据中心，利用被弃置的清洁能源。

Method: 提出SQSO（可持续与QoS感知卫星卸载）框架，将每个时间间隔的任务卸载建模为在动态网络拓扑和电价约束下的优化问题。设计了$\text{AO}^2$（自适应卸载编排）算法求解该约束优化问题，联合考虑间歇性且容量有限的通信窗口以及时变的电力预算。

Result: 基于Starlink规模仿真和真实电价追踪数据的实验表明，与最先进方案相比，$\text{AO}^2$最多可降低76.03%的能耗和76.85%的电池寿命损耗，同时还能减少任务延迟。

Conclusion: LEO星座的可持续扩展需要空间网络与可再生能源基础设施的协同设计，所提方案通过可再生能源感知的任务卸载促进跨域协作，为6G时代空间-能源融合提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Low-Earth-Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are becoming integral to 6G infrastructure, but increasing in-orbit computation accelerates battery degradation and raises sustainability concerns. Meanwhile, renewable-heavy regions worldwide experience persistent energy curtailment due to transmission bottlenecks, leaving substantial clean energy stranded near generation sites. We identify a satellite-grid co-design opportunity: adaptively offloading task-critical data from satellite to data centers co-located with renewable power plants. However, realizing this vision requires jointly considering intermittent and capacity-limited communication windows, as well as time-varying electricity budgets. In this paper, we propose SQSO, a Sustainable and QoS-aware Satellite Offloading framework that models per-interval task offloading as a constrained optimization over dynamic topology and electricity prices. Under this framework, we design $\text{AO}^2$, an adaptive offloading orchestration algorithm to solve the formulated optimization problem. Using Starlink-scale simulations and real-world electricity price traces, $\text{AO}^2$ reduces energy consumption by up to 76.03% and battery life consumption by up to 76.85% compared to state-of-the-art schemes, while also lowering task delay. This work highlights that sustainable scaling of LEO constellations requires co-design of space networking and renewable energy infrastructure, while our solution promotes renewable-aware task offloading and cross-domain collaboration for space-energy integration in the 6G era.

</details>


### [185] [Single-Step Six-Dimensional Movable Antenna Reconfiguration for High-Mobility IoV: Modeling, Analysis, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03321)
*Maoxin Ji,Qiong Wu,Pingyi Fan,Kezhi Wang,Wen Chen,Cui Zhang,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对高移动性车联网场景，提出了一种无需CSI的单步6DMA天线重配置框架，在避免服务中断的同时显著提升了上行总速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维可移动天线(6DMA)系统虽能通过利用空间自由度提升无线容量，但在高移动性车联网(IoV)场景中面临两大核心挑战：一是难以获取瞬时信道状态信息(CSI)，二是机械重配置延迟可能导致服务中断。

Method: 提出了一种低复杂度、免CSI的单步重配置框架。首先设计了基于经纬度网格的确定性离散位置生成方案，利用图论推导了天线重配置的移动和时间成本下界；随后利用6DMA信道方向稀疏性，开发融合离线环境先验与在线历史反馈的自适应优化策略；最后引入基于预测累积车辆分布的周期性重配置机制，通过将天线调整严格限制在一阶空间邻域内消除服务中断。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案在上行总速率方面显著优于传统固定天线和基于全局搜索的基准方案，同时机械开销和延迟可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提出的方案验证了在高动态车联网环境中实现6DMA的可行性与鲁棒性，有效解决了CSI获取困难与服务中断问题。

Abstract: The Six-Dimensional Movable Antenna (6DMA) system has emerged as a promising technology to enhance wireless capacity by fully exploiting spatial degrees of freedom. However, applying 6DMA to high-mobility Internet of Vehicles (IoV) scenarios faces significant challenges, primarily due to the difficulty of acquiring instantaneous Channel State Information (CSI) and the risk of service interruptions caused by mechanical reconfiguration delays. To address these issues, this paper proposes a low-complexity, CSI-free single-step reconfiguration framework. First, we design a deterministic discrete position generation scheme based on a latitude-longitude grid with inherent topological structures. Leveraging graph theory, we explicitly model and theoretically derive the lower bounds of movement and time costs for antenna reconfiguration. Subsequently, utilizing the directional sparsity of 6DMA channels, we develop an adaptive optimization strategy that fuses offline environmental priors with online historical feedback. Furthermore, a periodic reconfiguration mechanism based on predicted cumulative vehicle distributions is introduced. By strictly restricting antenna adjustments to the first-order spatial neighborhood, the proposed single-step method effectively eliminates service interruptions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms traditional fixed and global-search-based benchmarks in terms of uplink sum rate, while incurring negligible mechanical overhead and latency, thereby validating its feasibility and robustness in highly dynamic vehicular networks.

</details>


### [186] [QoS Assurance Mechanism for 5G Network Slicing Based on the Deep Reinforcement Learning PPO Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03345)
*Qingyang Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的5G网络切片QoS保障机制，通过联合优化带宽、计算和无线资源，在时延、吞吐量、可靠性等多维度指标上优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着5G服务类型日益多样化和网络负载动态波动加剧，在网络切片环境中实现差异化的服务质量保障已成为资源管理的关键问题。

Method: 在传统近端策略优化演员-评论家框架基础上，将切片资源分配建模为受约束马尔可夫决策过程；引入图注意力网络和双向长短期记忆网络分别提取拓扑相关性和时序服务特征；结合自适应拉格朗日惩罚和动态奖励塑形机制，联合优化带宽、计算和无线资源。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在QoS满意度、时延控制、资源利用率和收敛稳定性方面均优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: 所提深度强化学习机制能有效解决5G网络切片中的差异化QoS保障问题，实现了时延、吞吐量、可靠性、公平性和切片隔离性能的综合优化。

Abstract: With the increasing diversity of 5G service types and the intensifying dynamic fluctuations of network load, achieve differentiated quality of service assurance in a network slicing environment has become a key issue in resource management. To address this problem, this paper proposes a deep reinforcement learning mechanism for 5G network slicing quality of service assurance based on the traditional proximal policy optimization actor-critic framework. First, the slicing resource allocation is modeled as a constrained Markov decision process, jointly considering the collaborative optimization of bandwidth, computing, and wireless resources. Meanwhile, a graph attention network and bidirectional long short-term memory are introduced to extract topological correlations and temporal service features, combined with an adaptive Lagrangian penalty and dynamic reward shaping mechanism, to comprehensively optimize delay, throughput, reliability, fairness, and slice isolation performance. Experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms existing baseline models in terms of quality of service satisfaction rate, delay control, resource utilization, and convergence stability.

</details>


### [187] [CRT: Collision-Tolerant Residence Time for Deterministic Transmission in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03382)
*Siqi Yang,Zonghui Li,Chaoqun You,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRT框架，一种针对LEO卫星网络的时间敏感传输解决方案，通过本地时钟调节逐跳驻留时间并采用冲突容忍调度策略，在无需严格全局同步的条件下实现了低时延抖动和高可调度性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络是6G非地面网络架构的关键组成部分，但其高度动态的拓扑结构和难以维持精确全局时间同步的特性，使得支持时间敏感服务面临巨大挑战。现有的时间敏感网络（TSN）机制主要依赖静态拓扑和严格同步，无法适应动态的LEO环境。

Method: 提出CRT确定性传输框架：1) 利用本地时钟调节逐跳驻留时间，补偿链路时延变化，避免对严格全局同步的依赖；2) 采用冲突容忍调度策略，在限制冲突引起抖动的同时最大化可调度流数量。针对该NP-hard调度问题，开发了CRT-Fast启发式算法，结合迭代分层与路径连续性来控制冲突强度并提高拓扑变化下的路径稳定性。

Result: 在Iridium和Starlink星座上的仿真结果表明，该方法在重负载流量下实现了更低的时延抖动和较高的可调度性，验证了CRT框架在动态LEO网络环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: CRT框架成功解决了LEO卫星网络中时间敏感传输的关键难题，为6G NTN架构提供了可行的确定性传输方案，在动态拓扑和非严格同步条件下实现了优异的性能表现。

Abstract: Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite networks are a key enabler for the 6G Non-Terrestrial Network (NTN) architecture. However, supporting time-sensitive services in LEO networks is challenging due to highly dynamic topologies and the difficulty of maintaining precise global time synchronization. Existing Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) mechanisms largely rely on static topologies and strict synchronization, which makes them ill-suited to dynamic LEO environments. To address this issue, we propose CRT, a deterministic transmission framework tailored for LEO networks. CRT regulates per-hop residence time using local clocks, thereby compensating for link-delay variations without requiring strict global synchronization. To handle asynchronous collisions, CRT adopts a collision-tolerant scheduling strategy that maximizes the number of schedulable flows while bounding collision-induced jitter. We formalize the corresponding scheduling problem and show that it is NP-hard. We further develop CRT-Fast, an efficient heuristic algorithm. It combines iterative layering with path continuity to control collision intensity and improve path stability under topology changes. Simulations on Iridium and Starlink constellations show that the proposed method achieves lower delay jitter and high schedulability under heavy traffic loads.

</details>


### [188] [DACP: A Scientific Data Access and Collaboration Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03411)
*Zhihong Shen,Xiaojie Zhu,Zhenjing Cheng,Hao Ren,Zhaoji Liang,Changfa Lu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了数据访问与协作协议（DACP），通过定义流式数据帧（SDF）核心数据模型，实现跨科学数据中心的数据发现、原位计算和结果流式返回，并提供了参考实现faird，旨在解决科学计算数据孤岛问题，支持AI4Science等新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算正快速进入数据密集型时代，但现有的通用网络协议栈在消除数据孤岛、提升数据可访问性和互操作性方面存在局限，难以有效满足AI4Science等新兴计算范式的需求。

Method: 提出数据访问与协作协议（DACP），定义流式数据帧（SDF）作为核心数据模型。通过统一资源标识、列式流分帧和反向供应机制，实现跨科学数据中心的数据发现、原位计算和结果流式返回。同时介绍了DACP的参考服务器实现faird。

Result: DACP成功实现了跨科学数据中心的数据发现、原位计算和流式结果返回，有效促进了跨域协作。faird作为参考实现验证了协议的可行性，为构建可扩展、协作式的科学数据基础设施提供了技术路径。

Conclusion: DACP协议及其实现faird为构建可扩展、协作式的科学数据基础设施提供了可行方案，有效解决了数据孤岛问题，提升了数据可访问性和互操作性，能够支持AI4Science等新兴计算范式的发展需求。

Abstract: Scientific computing is rapidly entering a data-intensive era. However, existing general-purpose network protocol stacks face limitations in eliminating data silos and improving data accessibility and interoperability, making it difficult to effectively meet the demands of emerging paradigms such as AI4Science. To address these challenges, we propose the Data Access and Collaboration Protocol (DACP). DACP defines the Streaming Data Frame (SDF) as its core data model. Through Unified Resource Identification, columnar stream framing, and a reverse supply mechanism, DACP enables data discovery, in-situ computation, and the streaming return of results across scientific data centers, thereby facilitating efficient cross-domain collaboration. Furthermore, this paper introduces faird, a reference server implementation of DACP. This work provides a viable path for building scalable and collaborative scientific data infrastructures.

</details>


### [189] [Dynamic Hypergame for Task Assignment in Multi-platform Mobile Crowdsensing Under Incomplete Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03569)
*Sumedh J. Dongare,Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Andrea Ortiz,Walid Saad,Anja Klein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PACMAB框架，一种去中心化的感知感知双边学习方法，用于解决多MCSP竞争场景下信息不完备的移动群智感知任务分配问题，相比基准方法至少多完成41%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动群智感知研究大多假设单一MCSP，但实际部署中存在多个竞争MCSP同时向移动单元提出任务。这种场景构成了带有契约的双边匹配博弈（MWC），MCSP需要其他平台偏好和MU质量信息来优化任务提议，MU需要任务执行努力信息来优化任务接受决策，但这些信息在实际中均不可获得。

Method: 将MWC建模为动态超博弈，每个MCSP通过感知建模未知偏好并在重复交互中不断优化。提出PACMAB去中心化感知感知双边学习框架：（i）每个MCSP在竞争环境下学习自适应任务提议策略；（ii）每个MU通过估计任务执行努力学习任务接受策略。该方法无需完全信息假设。

Result: PACMAB的计算复杂度对MCSP和MU均具有良好的可扩展性。大量仿真实验表明，PACMAB在不假设完全信息的情况下持续优于基准方法，任务完成量至少提高41%。

Conclusion: 所提出的PACMAB框架有效解决了多MCSP竞争场景下信息不完备的双边匹配问题，实现了MCSP任务提议策略和MU任务接受策略的分布式优化学习，在计算效率和任务完成率方面均表现优异，为实际移动群智感知系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile crowdsensing (MCS) is a promising distributed sensing paradigm for future wireless networks, where MCS platforms (MCSPs) recruit mobile units (MUs) through monetary incentives for sensing data collection. While most existing studies assume a single MCSP, practical deployments involve multiple competing MCSPs that simultaneously propose task offers to MUs, and MUs accept offers that maximize their revenue. This interaction gives rise to a two-sided matching game with contracts (MWC), decomposed into two components: (i) task proposal problem of the MCSPs and (ii) task acceptance problem of the MUs. To optimally solve (i), every MCSP requires information about other platforms' preferences and the qualities of the MUs in advance. Similarly, to solve (ii) optimally, the MUs require information about the task execution efforts of all tasks in advance. Such information is unavailable at the MCSPs and at the MUs. To address the challenge of unknown preferences of the other MCSPs, the MWC is posed as a dynamic hypergame, where every MCSP models the unknown preferences through perceptions and refines them over repeated interactions. To solve the dynamic hypergame under incomplete information, we propose PACMAB, a fully decentralized perception-aware two-sided learning framework where, (i) each MCSP learns an adaptive task proposal strategy under competition, and (ii) each MU learns task acceptance policy by estimating task execution efforts. Computational complexity of PACMAB shows that it scales favorably for the MCSPs as well as the MUs. Extensive simulations show that PACMAB consistently outperforms the benchmarks by completing at least 41% more tasks without assuming complete information.

</details>


### [190] [Surviving the Edge: Federated Learning under Networking and Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03870)
*Mike Mwanje,Okemawo Obadofin,Theophilus Benson,Joao Barros*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地揭示了联邦学习（FL）系统在资源受限网络环境下的传输层断点，发现FL的通信模式与默认TCP配置存在根本性失配，并提出了通过调整TCP参数提升边缘部署鲁棒性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着联邦学习在边缘环境中的普及，网络和计算资源高度受限的部署场景（如非洲等地区）日益增多。然而，目前缺乏对FL系统在此类环境下传输层故障边界的系统性研究。

Method: 构建了一个可复现的测试平台并结合混沌工程工具，模拟资源受限的网络条件。通过逐步恶化网络环境（如延迟、丢包、客户端掉线），对FL框架Flower进行了系统性评估，并分析了FL训练过程中的连接模式。

Result: 实验揭示了FL通信模式与标准TCP连接管理之间的关键失配：当单向延迟达到5秒、丢包率超过50%或客户端掉线率达90%时，FL训练会发生灾难性失败。研究证明仅调整三个TCP连接管理参数即可在极端延迟下显著缩短训练时间。

Conclusion: 传输层感知对边缘环境下的联邦学习部署至关重要。本研究为从业者提供了具体的故障阈值，明确了标准FL部署失效的边界条件及何时需采用高级可靠性技术。

Abstract: Motivated by the growing proliferation of federated learning (FL) in edge environments, we present the first systematic characterization of transport-layer breaking points in FL systems operating under conditions of highly constrained network and compute resources. Using a reproducible testbed with chaos engineering tools, we evaluate Flower under progressively degraded network conditions representative of resource-constrained deployments in Africa and similar environments. Our empirical investigation reveals a fundamental mismatch between FL's burst-idle communication pattern and standard TCP connection management. We identify precise operational boundaries: FL training catastrophically fails at 5-second one-way latency due to TCP handshake timeouts, above 50% packet loss due to buffer exhaustion, and with 90% client dropout rates. Through systematic analysis of connection patterns during training rounds, we demonstrate that FL's periodic model update bursts, separated by extended local training periods, violate the assumptions underlying default TCP configurations. To validate the significance of these findings, we show that adjusting just three TCP connection management parameters can significantly reduce training time under extreme latency, proving that transport-layer awareness is not merely beneficial but essential for FL deployment at the network edge. Our characterization methodology and findings provide practitioners with concrete thresholds for determining when standard FL deployments will fail and when advanced reliability techniques become necessary.

</details>


### [191] [Cross-Slice Co-Location Risk-Aware SFC Provisioning in Multi-Slice LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03656)
*Mohammed Mahyoub,Wael Jaafar,Sami Muhaidat,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多切片低地球轨道（LEO）卫星边缘网络中的跨切片同址风险，通过混合整数线性规划（MILP）建模服务功能链（SFC）放置问题，并提出三阶段混合优化器。实验表明，该方法将同址风险降低40%，可避免的VNF迁移减少80%，且热启动速度提升23倍，具备实时可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多切片LEO卫星边缘网络中，来自不同网络切片的虚拟网络功能（VNF）若共享同一卫星实例，会形成跨切片安全暴露通道。为解决此跨切片同址风险，需在动态演化的卫星星座中，联合优化风险、资源消耗与迁移稳定性。

Method: 将风险感知的SFC放置问题建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），联合优化跨切片同址风险、CPU资源消耗和VNF迁移稳定性，受卫星容量、星间链路（ISL）容量、可见性和端到端时延约束。风险模型采用乘法同址公式，提供精确和粗粒度（切片级）两种表述并推导边界。求解采用三阶段混合优化器：时间轮次预处理、基于模拟退火的热启动、以及分支定界精炼。

Result: 实验评估显示，与贪婪基线相比，同址风险降低40%，可避免的VNF迁移减少80%，且CPU开销可忽略。热启动将每个轮次的求解时间从冷启动的256秒降至11秒，加速23倍，从第二个轮次起具备实时可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效缓解了多切片LEO卫星边缘网络中的跨切片同址风险，在资源效率与迁移稳定性之间取得良好平衡，并通过热启动实现了实时部署能力，为安全可靠的卫星边缘网络提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We address cross-slice co-location risk in multi-slice low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite edge networks, where virtual network functions (VNFs) from different network slices sharing the same satellite instance create a cross-slice security exposure channel. We formulate a risk-aware service function chain (SFC) placement problem as a mixed-integer linear program (MILP) over a dynamically evolving LEO satellite constellation, jointly optimizing cross-slice co-location risk, CPU resource consumption, and VNF migration stability under satellite capacity, inter-satellite link (ISL) capacity, visibility, and end-to-end (E2E) delay constraints. The risk model employs a multiplicative co-location formulation, inspired by the risk assessment principles from ISO/NIST frameworks, with exact and coarse (slice-level)formulations that analytically establish bounds on the co-location exposure. To solve this problem, we propose a three-stage hybrid optimizer combining time epoch preprocessing, simulated annealing-based warm-start, and branch-and-bound refinement. Experimental evaluation demonstrates a 40% reduction in co-location risk and an 80% reduction in avoidable VNF migrations relative to the greedy baseline at negligible CPU overhead, and a 23x warm-start speedup from 256s cold-start to 11s per epoch, confirming real-time viability from the second epoch.

</details>


### [192] [SprayCheck: Finding Gray Failures in Adaptive Routing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03702)
*Jakob Krebs,Daniel Amir,Shir Landau Feibish,Mark Silberstein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了SprayCheck，一种针对分布式机器学习训练网络的被动式灰色故障检测系统，利用自适应路由的统计特性实现故障的快速检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代数据中心网络中，分布式机器学习训练规模庞大，灰色故障（gray failures）不可避免且难以检测。广泛部署的自适应路由虽然提高了网络利用率，但也加剧了灰色故障的影响。现有工作要么忽视灰色故障，要么提出的检测机制无法大规模扩展。

Method: SprayCheck是一种被动检测系统，利用自适应路由和网络负载均衡的统计特性，结合流级别信息，通过被动观察流量喷洒（traffic spraying）来识别故障。该方法无需向网络引入额外负载，支持故障定位和预防性重新路由。

Result: 在64 spine拓扑下的Llama-3 70B训练场景中，SprayCheck可在单次迭代内检测并定位1.5%的单链路丢包率，在5次训练迭代内可检测低至0.5%的丢包率。

Conclusion: SprayCheck能够有效解决大规模集群中灰色故障检测的扩展性问题，在不增加网络负载的前提下实现故障的早期检测，从而保障机器学习工作负载的性能需求。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning (ML) training has become a dominant workload in modern data center networks, operating at massive scale with clusters comprising tens to hundreds of thousands of GPUs. The scale of these networks makes failures, and particularly gray failures, inevitable. Gray failures can significantly degrade both network and application performance, yet they are notoriously difficult to detect, localize, and debug. To meet the performance demands of ML workloads, adaptive routing is widely deployed to maximize network utilization by dynamically spreading traffic across many paths. While adaptive routing increases network utilization, it also greatly intensifies the effect of gray failures. Prior work has either dismissed gray failures as negligible or proposed detection mechanisms that fail to scale, rendering these approaches increasingly impractical for large-scale clusters.
  We present SprayCheck, a passive gray failure detection system that leverages the statistical properties of adaptive routing and network load balancing. By combining these properties with flow-level information, SprayCheck can identify failures before they significantly impact application performance, enabling preemptive rerouting and improving overall performance. Importantly, this is achieved through passive observation of traffic spraying, without introducing additional load on the network. We evaluate SprayCheck and show that it can detect and localize a single-link packet-drop-rate $1.5\%$ within a single iteration and as little as $0.5\%$ within 5 training iterations of Llama-3 70B in a 64 spine topology.

</details>


### [193] [Nested array design of extended coprime sets for DOA estimation of non-circular signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03856)
*Dongqi Chen,Kun Ye,Chuanxi Xing,Waqas Khalid,Huiping Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩展互质框架的新型阵列结构，通过滑动平移技术优化传感器位置，将和阵与差阵无缝合并以消除冗余，从而显著扩展虚拟孔径和自由度，在非圆信号DOA估计中实现了优于传统嵌套阵列等基准结构的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，利用非圆信号进行到达方向（DOA）估计已成为研究热点。为了提升接收阵列在非圆信号DOA估计中的自由度（DOF），需要设计一种能够最大化利用阵列孔径并增加虚拟传感器数量的新型阵列结构。

Method: 提出了一种基于扩展互质框架的新型阵列配置。该方法应用滑动平移技术优化传感器位置，关键在于重组后的结构保持了差阵（DCA）的连续性，并将和阵（SCA）平移以与DCA无缝合并，从而消除冗余并大幅扩展虚拟孔径阵列（VAA）和自由度。

Result: 仿真结果与对比分析表明，与传统嵌套阵列（NA）、扩展滑动嵌套阵列（ESNA）及其他基准结构相比，提出的阵列结构获得了更好的自由度和更大的虚拟孔径阵列，在实际非圆信号DOA估计场景中实现了更高的估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的阵列结构通过有效合并和阵与差阵，成功提升了非圆信号DOA估计的性能，证明了其在增加自由度和虚拟孔径方面的优越性，为实际应用提供了一种更高效的阵列设计方案。

Abstract: In recent years, direction of arrival estimation utilizing non-circular signals has become a focal point for scholarly research. To enhance the degrees of freedom (DOF) in receiver arrays specifically for non-circular signal DOA estimation, this study introduces a novel array configuration. This design leverages an extended coprime framework, applying a sliding translation technique to optimize sensor placement. Crucially, this rearranged structure preserves the continuity of the difference co-array (DCA). Furthermore, the sum co-array (SCA) is shifted to merge seamlessly with the DCA, eliminating redundancy and substantially expanding both the virtual aperture array (VAA) and the DOF. Consequently, the proposed array demonstrates superior performance in practical DOA estimation tasks involving non-circular signals. Simulation results and comparative analyses confirm that, relative to traditional Nested Arrays (NA), Extended Sliding Nested Array (ESNA), and other benchmark structures, the proposed array achieves better DOF and VAA, leading to enhanced estimation accuracy in practical scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [Design and Implementation of BNN-Based Object Detection on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03396)
*Xuyu Zhao,Yunpeng Wu,Mengyuan Zhu,Haoyu Huang,Xiaoyu Xu,Yanjing Li,Gaolong Zhang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在低成本FPGA上实现了基于二值神经网络（BNN）的YOLOv3-tiny类目标检测器，在VOC数据集上达到39.6% mAP50，RTL仿真与ONNX模型输出相关系数达0.999964。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在低成本FPGA平台上实现高效的目标检测，降低计算复杂度和资源消耗，同时保持较高的检测精度。

Method: 设计了一种混合精度的BNN YOLOv3-tiny网络，主要卷积层采用1-bit权重和8-bit激活，首层和检测头使用定点标准卷积；从训练好的ONNX模型提取参数并转换为定点格式存储于BRAM；完全使用Verilog RTL实现硬件，包括填充、行缓冲、二值卷积、量化后处理、最大池化和检测头计算，并将Mul_prev融合到BNN PE中以实现通道级补偿。

Result: 在VOC数据集上获得39.6% mAP50，计算量为0.098 GFLOPs，参数量为0.74 M；RTL仿真显示最终原始检测输出与ONNX节点的相关系数为0.999964，平均绝对误差为0.020027。

Conclusion: 该方法成功在低成本FPGA上实现了高精度的BNN目标检测器，硬件实现与软件模型高度一致，验证了设计的有效性和准确性。

Abstract: This paper implements a Binary Neural Network (BNN) based YOLOv3-tiny-like object detector on a low-cost FPGA. The network takes 320*320*3 RGB images as input. Its main convolution layers use 1-bit weights and 8-bit activations, while Conv1 and the final detection head use fixed-point standard convolutions. From the trained ONNX model, weights, biases, and quantization parameters are extracted, converted to fixed point, packed into COE files, and stored in Vivado BRAM ROMs. The hardware is written fully in Verilog RTL and includes padding, line buffering, binary convolution, quantization post-processing, max pooling, and detection-head computation. For layers where Mul_prev is indexed by input channel and Div_current by output channel, Mul_prev is fused in-to the BNN PE so that channel-wise compensation is applied during accumulation. On VOC, the model obtains 39.6% mAP50 with 0.098 GFLOPs and 0.74 M parameters. RTL simulation shows that the final raw detection output reaches a correlation coefficient of 0.999964 and a mean absolute error of 0.020027 against the corresponding ONNX node.

</details>


### [195] [SPEC CPU2026: Characterization, Representativeness, and Cross-Suite Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03713)
*Ruihao Li,Andrew Jacob,Neeraja J. Yadwadkar,Lizy K. John*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次对SPEC CPU2026进行了跨九个平台的全面特性分析，发现其相比CPU2017增加了指令量和内存占用，更贴近现代CPU行为。研究识别出紧凑子集可保留96.4-99.9%的完整套件行为，大幅降低评估成本。CPU2026作为通用套件与MLPerf和DCPerf形成互补，并通过案例研究展示了其在架构评估中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用加速器主导AI工作负载，但CPU在协调加速器和运行数据中心服务方面仍至关重要，CPU性能日益影响端到端系统效率。然而，新兴CPU基准测试套件如何反映这些变化尚不明确，需要评估SPEC CPU2026是否能准确反映现代工作负载和瓶颈。

Method: 在涵盖Intel、AMD、Ampere和Nvidia处理器的九个平台上对SPEC CPU2026进行全面特性分析；使用基于聚类的代表性分析识别紧凑子集；通过跨套件微架构指标与SPEC CPU2017、DCPerf和MLPerf进行对比；通过页大小、分配器、预取、编译器优化、ISA敏感性和众核扩展等案例研究验证实用性。

Result: SPEC CPU2026相比CPU2017增加了指令量和内存占用，将压力转向新兴瓶颈（尤其是更高的指令缓存压力）；每类4-5个工作负载的紧凑子集可保留96.4-99.9%的完整套件行为；CPU2026向量密集度低于MLPerf，前端压力低于DCPerf，但比前代SPEC更接近真实CPU行为；新的轮循交错模式生成的代理工作负载将IPC差距缩小至13.7%。

Conclusion: SPEC CPU2026确立了严谨且经济高效的CPU评估新基础，作为通用套件与MLPerf和DCPerf形成互补特性，支持超越聚合分数的实际架构研究，能够有效反映现代CPU工作负载特征和瓶颈。

Abstract: Specialized accelerators dominate AI workloads, but CPUs remain critical for orchestrating these accelerators and running datacenter services. As a result, CPU performance increasingly shapes end-to-end system efficiency, making it necessary for benchmarks to reflect modern workloads and bottlenecks. However, it remains unclear how emerging CPU benchmark suites reflect these shifts. To address this, we present the first comprehensive characterization of SPEC CPU2026 across nine platforms spanning recent Intel, AMD, Ampere, and Nvidia processors. We find that, compared to SPEC CPU2017, SPEC CPU2026 increases instruction volume and memory footprint, and shifts pressure toward emerging bottlenecks, most notably higher instruction-cache stress. We next examine whether the full suite is necessary for architectural evaluation. Using clustering-based representativeness analysis, we identify that compact subsets of 4-5 workloads per group preserve 96.4-99.9% of full-suite behavior, substantially reducing evaluation costs without sacrificing fidelity. To better position SPEC CPU2026, we compare it against SPEC CPU2017, DCPerf, and MLPerf using cross-suite microarchitectural metrics. SPEC CPU2026 remains a general-purpose suite with complementary characteristics: it is less vector-intensive than MLPerf and has lower frontend pressure than DCPerf, yet moves closer to real-world CPU behavior than prior SPEC CPU generations. Finally, we show that SPEC CPU2026 supports practical architectural studies beyond aggregate scores through case studies on page sizes and allocators, prefetching, compiler optimizations, ISA sensitivity, and many-core scaling. The new round-robin stagger mode generates proxy workloads that approximate DCPerf, reducing the IPC gap to 13.7%. Overall, SPEC CPU2026 sets a new foundation for rigorous and cost-effective CPU evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [MARS-DA: A Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning Framework for Risk-Aware Multi-Agent Bidding in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03142)
*Jiayi Chen,Xuan Zhang,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文开源了一个基于PJM数据的高保真两结算电力市场博弈环境，并提出了MARS-DA分层框架，通过协调安全代理和投机代理，在波动市场中实现了优越的风险调整收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源渗透率的提高导致批发电力市场波动加剧，传统强化学习方法难以在利润最大化与风险管理之间取得平衡，容易过拟合或忽略日前（DA）与实时（RT）结算之间的随机价差。

Method: 1. 构建并开源了一个基于PJM实证数据的高保真两结算电力市场博弈环境，为通用及风险敏感型智能体提供标准化测试平台；\n2. 提出MARS-DA分层框架，顶层Meta-Controller动态融合“安全代理”（优化可靠DA分配）和“投机代理”（捕捉RT套利机会）的动作。

Result: 实验表明，MARS-DA在风险调整收益方面优于现有最先进基线，并在极端市场波动期间保持稳健的体制对齐。

Conclusion: MARS-DA通过分层协调风险管理与利润追求，有效应对电力市场波动，为两结算市场竞价提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of renewable energy has introduced substantial volatility into wholesale electricity markets, complicating the optimal bidding strategies for power producers. Traditional Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches often struggle to balance profit maximization with risk management, frequently overfitting to specific market conditions or failing to account for the stochastic spread between Day-Ahead (DA) and Real-Time (RT) settlements. To address these challenges, this paper makes two primary contributions. First, we introduce and open-source a high-fidelity gymnasium environment for two-settlement electricity market bidding. Grounded in extensive empirical data from the PJM Interconnection, the environment explicitly models the interplay between DA commitments and RT deviations, providing a standardized testbed for general and risk-sensitive agents. Second, we propose MARS-DA (Multi-Agent Regime-Switching for Day-Ahead markets), a novel hierarchical framework that orchestrates distinct sub-policies for risk management and profit seeking. MARS-DA utilizes a top-level Meta-Controller to dynamically blend the actions of two specialized base agents: a "Safe Agent" that optimizes for reliable DA allocation and a "Speculator Agent" that targets volatile RT arbitrage opportunities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MARS-DA achieves superior risk-adjusted returns compared to state-of-the-art baselines while maintaining robust regime alignment during periods of extreme market volatility.

</details>


### [197] [Enwar 3.0: An Agentic Multi-Modal LLM Orchestrator for Situation-Aware Beamforming, Blockage Prediction, and Handover Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03215)
*Ahmad M. Nazar,Abdulkadir Celik,Asmaa Abdallah,Mohamed Y. Selim,Daji Qiao,Ahmed M. Eltawil*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Enwar 3.0框架，通过融合多模态感知、智能体大语言模型和上下文驱动的模型选择，实现了毫米波车联网中的预测性波束赋形、遮挡检测和切换管理，在准确性和可解释性上均达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在车联网环境中，维持鲁棒的毫米波连接需要实时适应环境动态变化、传感器退化以及链路波动，这给现有系统带来了巨大挑战。

Method: 提出Enwar 3.0环境感知推理框架，集成了传感器健康评估分类器与经过链式思考（CoT）引导和人在环反馈训练的大语言模型（LLM），通过结构化任务感知提示协调多个专用智能体进行波束选择、遮挡预测和环境感知，并基于环境上下文动态加载传感器特定模型；同时设计了合成退化管道来训练传感器退化分类器。

Result: 传感器退化分类器在检测摄像头、雷达、LiDAR和GPS输入的实时损伤方面准确率超过99%；在15种传感器组合的评估中，波束选择准确率超过88%，遮挡检测F1分数超过98%，复杂决策提示下的推理正确率达到87%。

Conclusion: 该工作为集成LLM的无线系统建立了可扩展的基础，使其能够实时进行推理、感知和自适应，显著提升了毫米波车联网的连接鲁棒性。

Abstract: Maintaining robust millimeter-wave (mmWave) connectivity in vehicular networks requires real-time adaptation to environmental dynamics, sensor degradation, and link variability. This paper presents Enwar 3.0, an environment-aware reasoning framework that unifies multi-modal sensing, agentic large language models (LLMs), and context-driven model selection for predictive beamforming, blockage detection, and handover management. Building upon prior iterations of Enwar, the proposed architecture integrates a classifier-driven assessment of sensor health with a primed LLM that orchestrates multiple specialized agents through structured, task-aware prompting. A novel synthetic degradation pipeline enables the training of a sensor degradation classifier that detects real-time impairments across camera, radar, LiDAR, and GPS inputs, achieving over 99% accuracy. The LLM, trained via chain-of-thought (CoT) priming and human-in-the-loop feedback, coordinates agent calls for beam selection, blockage forecasting, and environment perception while dynamically loading sensor-specific models based on environmental context. Extensive evaluations across 15 sensor combinations demonstrate that Enwar 3.0 delivers state-of-the-art performance in both predictive accuracy and interpretability, with beam selection accuracy exceeding 88%, blockage F1-scores surpassing 98%, and reasoning correctness reaching 87% on complex decision prompts. This work establishes a scalable foundation for LLM-integrated wireless systems that reason, perceive, and adapt in real-time.

</details>


### [198] [Coordination as an Architectural Layer for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03310)
*Maksym Nechepurenko,Pavel Shuvalov*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统生产环境失败率高达41%-87%，主要源于协调缺陷。本文提出将协调视为可配置架构层，通过预测市场实验设计，利用Murphy分解分析五种协调配置的性能特征与故障模式签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究要么仅经验性编目失败模式，要么提供工程化的声明式编排框架，均未能提供从协调配置到可预测故障模式签名的原则性映射。作者认为协调应被视为独立于智能体逻辑和信息访问的可配置架构层，从而实现架构层面的推理而非仅仅是工程生产力提升。

Method: 采用信息控制设计：单一LLM（claude-opus-4-6）、固定工具集、固定单次调用输出上限、固定提示模板，对比五种参考协调配置。在100个Polymarket二元市场（模型训练截止后解决）上进行实验，使用Murphy分解分离校准度与判别力，使配置在聚合分数相同时仍留下可区分签名。同时在Foresight Arena部署实时智能体作为链上复制通道。

Result: 五个预设预测中三个方向成立；两种配置在成本-质量Pareto前沿上占主导；探索性bootstrap置信区间区分了共识对齐与其他配置，但成对检验未通过Bonferroni校正（n=100）。不同协调配置即使聚合分数相近，Murphy签名仍可区分。

Conclusion: 成功验证了协调配置作为可分析架构层的方法论可行性，建立了从配置到故障模式签名的原则性映射。这是方法论验证的首个实例，而非跨模型通用声明，为后续跨模型研究奠定基础。发布了测试框架、轨迹数据集和生产智能体。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems fail in production at rates between 41% and 87%, mostly due to coordination defects rather than base-model capability. Existing responses split between cataloguing failure modes empirically and shipping declarative orchestration frameworks as engineering tools; neither delivers a principled mapping from coordination configuration to predictable failure-mode signature. We argue that coordination should be treated as a configurable architectural layer, separable from agent logic and from information access, enabling architectural reasoning rather than only engineering productivity.
  We instantiate this with an information-controlled design on prediction markets: a single LLM, fixed tools, fixed per-call output cap, and fixed prompt template across five reference coordination configurations, with total compute per question treated as an endogenous architectural output. The Murphy decomposition of the Brier score separates calibration from discriminative power, so configurations leave distinguishable signatures even when aggregate scores coincide.
  On 100 Polymarket binary markets resolved after the model's training cutoff (claude-opus-4-6) we report Murphy signatures, a cost-quality Pareto frontier, category-conditioned analysis, and a bootstrap power-projection. Three of five pre-specified predictions are upheld in direction; two configurations dominate the Pareto frontier within this regime; exploratory bootstrap intervals separate consensus alignment from others, though pairwise tests do not survive Bonferroni correction at n=100. We also deploy the same configurations as live agents on Foresight Arena under web-search-enabled conditions, as an on-chain replication channel accumulating in parallel. Harness, trace dataset, and production agents are released. We position this as a methodology-validating first instantiation, not a general cross-model claim.

</details>


### [199] [MemFlow: Intent-Driven Memory Orchestration for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03312)
*Jiayi Chen,Yingcong Li,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了MemFlow框架，通过路由代理分类查询意图并分配到三个专门的记忆层级，实现动态令牌预算管理，在SLM上准确率提升近2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLM在长时序、多轮对话中面临上下文溢出、检索噪声和开放式智能体循环不可靠的问题。论文认为SLM记忆失败主要源于不匹配的记忆操作：不同查询类型需要不同的检索策略、证据转换和上下文预算，而SLM无法通过开放式推理可靠地自我编排。

Method: 提出MemFlow，一个免训练的记忆编排框架，将记忆规划外部化。路由代理按意图分类查询并分发给记忆代理，后者执行三个专门层级之一（档案查询、定向检索或深度推理），并在动态的层级感知令牌预算下组装证据。答案代理从紧凑上下文生成响应，验证代理在证据不支持响应时可选重试更重的记忆层级。

Result: 在冻结的Qwen3-1.7B骨干网络上，于LongMemEval、LoCoMo和LongBench三个长时序记忆基准测试中，MemFlow相比全上下文SLM基线准确率提升近2倍。

Conclusion: 结构化的意图路由和确定性证据准备可以使有限容量的模型在资源受限的长时序智能体中显著更有效。

Abstract: Modern language agents must operate over long-horizon, multi-turn histories, yet deploying such agents with Small Language Models (SLMs) remains fundamentally difficult. Full-context prompting causes context overflow, flat retrieval exposes the model to noisy evidence, and open-ended agentic loops are unreliable under limited reasoning capacity. We argue that a substantial portion of SLM memory failure arises from mismatched memory operations: different query types demand categorically different retrieval strategies, evidence transformations, and context budgets that SLMs cannot reliably self-orchestrate through open-ended reasoning. We introduce MemFlow, a training-free memory orchestration framework that externalizes memory planning from the SLM. A Router Agent classifies each query by intent and dispatches it to the Memory Agent, which executes one of three specialized tiers (Profile Lookup, Targeted Retrieval, or Deep Reasoning) and assembles the resulting evidence under a dynamic, tier-aware token budget. An Answer Agent then generates a response from this compact context, and a Validator Agent optionally retries with a heavier memory tier when the response is not supported by the provided evidence. This route-then-compile design avoids tool-selection hallucination and reasoning loops while keeping the answer context compact. Evaluated on a frozen Qwen3-1.7B backbone across long-horizon memory benchmarks - LongMemEval, LoCoMo, and LongBench - MemFlow improves accuracy by nearly 2x over full-context SLM baselines. These results suggest that structured intent routing and deterministic evidence preparation can make limited-capacity models substantially more effective in resource-constrained long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [200] [Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent Architecture for Traceable, Risk-Aware Human-AI Decision Support in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04003)
*Danny Hoang,Ryan Matthiessen,Christopher Miller,Nasir Mannan,Ruby ElKharboutly,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了多智能体知识分析（MAKA）架构，用于航空航天自由曲面零件的高精度CNC加工决策支持，在Ti-6Al-4V转子叶片测试平台上，相比非结构化单模型交互模式，工具执行成功率提升高达87.5个百分点，预测表面偏差从10⁻²英寸降至约±10⁻³英寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现成的通用大语言模型（LLM）助手虽然能生成文本，但无法可靠执行风险约束的多步数值工作流，也无法为高风险决策提供可审计的可追溯性，而高精度航空航天自由曲面零件CNC加工需要基于检测、仿真和工艺知识的边界补偿。

Method: 提出人机回环决策支持架构MAKA，将意图路由、纯工具定量分析、知识图谱检索和基于批评者的验证分离，在推荐结果提交人工审批前强制执行物理合理性、安全边界和可追溯性完整性。在Ti-6Al-4V转子叶片加工测试平台上融合虚拟加工路径跟踪误差场、切削力/变形仿真以及16个叶片的扫描式3D检测偏差图，将偏差分解为证据关联的路径分量、漂移式磨损代理、残差系统性柔度项和不稳定性感知升级的变异性代理。

Result: 在三层级工具编排基准测试（单步至≥3步有状态序列）中，MAKA相比具有相同工具访问权限的非结构化单模型交互模式，工具执行成功率提升高达87.5个百分点；数字孪生假设研究表明，MAKA能协调可追溯的补偿方案，在仿真环境中将预测表面偏差从10⁻²英寸量级降至约±10⁻³英寸。

Conclusion: MAKA为风险感知的人机决策提供了预部署验证信号，证明了多智能体架构在高风险精密制造领域的有效性，能够实现可追溯的边界补偿决策支持。

Abstract: High-precision CNC machining of free-form aerospace components requires bounded compensations informed by inspection, simulation, and process knowledge. Off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) assistants can generate text, but they do not reliably execute risk-constrained multi-step numerical workflows or provide auditable provenance for high-stakes decisions. We present multi-agent knowledge analysis (MAKA), a human-in-the-loop decision-support architecture that separates intent routing, tools-only quantitative analysis, knowledge graph retrieval, and critic-based verification that enforces physical plausibility, safety bounds, and provenance completeness before recommendations are surfaced for human approval. MAKA is instantiated on a Ti-6Al-4V rotor blade machining testbed by fusing virtual-machining path-tracking error fields, cutting-force and deflection simulations, and scan-based 3D inspection deviation maps from 16 blades. The analysis decomposes deviation into an evidence-linked pathing component, a drift-based wear proxy capturing systematic evolution across parts, a residual systematic compliance term, and a variability proxy for instability-aware escalation. In a three-level tool-orchestration benchmark (single-step through $\geq$3-step stateful sequences), MAKA improves successful tool execution by up to 87.5 percentage points relative to an unstructured single-model interaction pattern with identical tool access. Digital twin what-if studies show MAKA can coordinate traceable compensation candidates that reduce predicted surface deviation from order $10^{-2}$in to approximately $\pm 10^{-3}$in over most of the blade within the simulation environment, providing a pre-deployment verification signal for risk-aware human decision-making.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Tutti: Making SSD-Backed KV Cache Practical for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03375)
*Shi Qiu,Yifan Hu,Xintao Wang,Wenhao Zhu,Jianqin Yan,Hao Chen,Kaiqiang Xu,Kai Chen,Yiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出Tutti，一种GPU中心化的SSD后端KV缓存方案，通过消除CPU干预、引入GPU原生对象抽象和GPU io_uring，将TTFT降低78.3%，吞吐量提升2倍，接近DRAM缓存性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中，随着上下文增长，KV缓存容量远超GPU HBM和CPU DRAM容量，需要卸载到SSD。然而从SSD恢复KV缓存存在I/O性能差、导致GPU严重停顿的问题。根本原因在于GPU内存碎片化导致大量细小随机I/O，即使使用GDS仍依赖CPU发起每个I/O，低并行度CPU成为严重瓶颈。

Method: Tutti采用GPU中心化设计，核心包括：(1)GPU原生对象抽象，支持批量KV缓存传输与管理；(2)重构GPU存储栈，引入GPU io_uring支持异步GPU直接对象I/O；(3)松弛感知I/O调度避免GPU资源竞争。CPU仅负责每层一次异步加载I/O内核到GPU，完全消除关键数据和控制路径上的CPU干预。

Result: 集成到vLLM的实验表明：相比GDS使能的SSD后端LMCache，在严格SLO约束下TTFT降低78.3%，可达成请求率提升2倍，服务成本降低27%。Tutti实现与DRAM后端LMCache几乎相同的推理性能，同时提供近乎无限容量。

Conclusion: Tutti通过GPU中心化架构成功解决了SSD后端KV缓存的I/O瓶颈问题，消除了CPU干预，充分利用NVMe SSD带宽，将GPU停顿降至近乎零，实现了接近内存级性能的SSD缓存方案。

Abstract: LLM serving relies on prefix caching to improve inference performance. As growing contexts push key-value (KV) cache footprint far beyond GPU HBM and CPU DRAM capacity, KV cache is increasingly offloaded to NVMe SSDs. Unfortunately, restoring KV cache from SSDs suffers from poor I/O performance and incurs significant GPU stalls. This is primarily because the fragmented GPU memory layout results in a massive number of tiny random I/Os, rendering the low-parallelism CPU a severe bottleneck even with GPU Direct Storage (GDS), which still relies on CPU intervention to initiate each I/O and thus remains CPU-centric. This paper presents Tutti, an efficient SSD-backed KV caching solution that eliminates CPU intervention from the critical data and I/O control paths between HBM and SSDs. At the core of Tutti is a GPU-centric KV cache object store, in which the CPU is only responsible for asynchronously loading I/O kernels once per layer to the GPU. Tutti saturates NVMe SSD bandwidth and reduces GPU stalls to near zero through the following designs: (i) we provide a GPU-native object abstraction that enables bulk KV cache transfers and management; (ii) we re-architect the GPU storage stack by introducing GPU io_uring to support asynchronous GPU direct object I/O; and (iii) we propose slack-aware I/O scheduling to avoid GPU resource contention. We have implemented Tutti and integrated it to vLLM. Extensive evaluation shows that compared to the state-of-the-art GDS-enabled, SSD-backed LMCache, Tutti reduces TTFT by 78.3% under strict SLO constraints and improves the achievable request rate by 2x. The serving cost is reduced by 27%. Tutti achieves nearly the same inference performance as DRAM-backed LMCache, while providing almost infinite capacity.

</details>
